# P6 Extend and v1.0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Close the research lifecycle with three model skills (`ethics-and-crf`, `journal-selection`, `reviewer-response`) backed by three stdlib scripts (`crf.py`, `journal_check.py`, `response.py`), add a maintainer autorater, Codex/Antigravity manifests and a landing page, fix the P1–P5 leftovers that affect correctness, safety or cost, and release 0.7.0 — spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-23-p6-extend-design.md` (authoritative; commit 051aa22 or later).

**Architecture:** Scripts measure, the agent reasons. `crf.py` turns the G2-locked `variables.md` into a CRF and a data dictionary and checks them against the SAP register (`E-CRF`); `journal_check.py` records journal facts from NLM Catalog, DOAJ and Crossref with source and date and lists signals, never verdicts; `response.py` splits reviewer comments, snapshots the submitted drafts and verifies every claimed change mechanically (`E-RESP`), and builds `out/response-letter.docx` only through G4 (gate, hook and `build_docx.py` treat it as a final name). The reviewer loop gets per-deliverable reports saved by the skill (agents lose `Write`), one scored round per pass, and an audit warning on unscored reports. `tools/autorater.py` judges old-versus-new skill text outside CI. Cleanup items are small, test-first edits in the files that own them.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.10+ standard library (every new or changed script), Markdown skills and references, JSON/CSV project files, `unittest` (offline, HTTP mocked), Claude Code CLI (`claude -p`) for scenarios, the autorater and the end-to-end run, GitHub Actions (CI and a gated Pages workflow), static HTML.

## Global Constraints

- Everything in the Global Constraints of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-p5-reviews-case-reports.md` (lines 13–28) still applies unless this plan overrides it.
- **Python 3.10+ standard library only** for every script, new or changed; no stdlib API newer than 3.10 (`tarfile` extraction filters are used only when present).
- **Exit codes** for every script: 0 = ok / clean / written; 1 = findings with at least one `block`, or an action refused (gate closed, already initialised, unknown key); 2 = usage error, missing or unreadable input, network or API error, internal error. Results to stdout, errors to stderr.
- **Finding format**: `<CODE> <block|warn> <file>:<line>: <msg>`, project-relative POSIX paths. Exit 1 only when a `block` exists. New codes: `E-CRF` (CRF and data dictionary), `E-RESP` (reviewer response); consent readability warns `CF01`–`CF03`; numbers in responses keep `E-NUM`.
- Every script calls `sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")` (and stderr) inside `try/except` at import time.
- **LF only**: every text file a script writes is opened with `open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")`; fixtures and examples are byte-stable.
- **Skills**: kebab-case folder = `name`; bodies ≤ 500 words (`python tools/lint_skills.py`); model skills' `description: 'Use when … Triggers: "<vi>", "<en>", ….'` (Vietnamese and English triggers, never the workflow); script calls written `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/<script>.py" …` from the project folder; every `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/…` link resolves (`test_reference_library.py`). **A skill change ships with a scenario, an autorater case, or a stated reason** (D35).
- **Never type a PMID, DOI, ISSN, APC, journal indexing status or regulation from memory.** Journal facts only from `journal_check.py` lookups; regulation content is generic paraphrase (Declaration of Helsinki, CIOMS, ICH-GCP named, no article numbers); unit tests mock every HTTP call and use the fictional ISSNs `0000-0000` and `0000-0019` (valid check digits, no real journal) (D8).
- **No real patient data**: fixtures, examples, reviewer comments and scenarios are fictional and say so.
- **Unit tests are offline** and never need R, the `claude` CLI or the network.
- **Public repo**: no machine- or user-specific paths in plugin code, skills, references, tests, examples, site or README; OS-aware behaviour (Windows, macOS, Linux); external tools optional.
- Scripts never write inside `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`; they write only under the project given on the command line (`tools/autorater.py` writes only under `evals/results/` and a scratch folder).
- **Commits**: one final paragraph ending `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. A commit that changes `build_docx.py`, `references/statistics/*`, `skills/sample-size-sap/*` or `skills/protocol-design/*` puts `Co-authored-by: Amelia Le <267516761+ngoclnl189-amelia@users.noreply.github.com>` on the line before it, in the same paragraph (Task 3 touches `build_docx.py`).
- Owner name in any document, commit, manifest or README: **Trần Quang Hưng**.
- **Before each commit the implementer runs** `python tools/lint_skills.py` and the unit-test files the task touched (`python -m unittest discover -s tests/unit -p "test_<name>.py" -v` per file). Implementers do **not** run the whole suite; **the controller runs** `python -m unittest discover -s tests/unit -v` after each batch merge and before the release.
- Version 0.7.0 at release (Task 15). **Not 1.0.0**: the 1.0.0 steps are in the appendix "Owner sign-off checklist" and run only on the owner's "duyệt 1.0".

### Plan decisions (where the spec is open or self-contradictory)

| # | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | `crf.py` reads every Markdown table in `03_protocol/variables.md` whose header has a label or code column **and** a type column; headers matched after accent folding: code {code, mã biến, mã, variable code, variable_name}; label {biến, biến số, variable, tên biến, name}; type {loại, loại biến, kiểu, type}; values {giá trị / đơn vị, giá trị, values, values / unit, unit or values}; unit {đơn vị, unit}; range {range, khoảng, khoảng giá trị}; source {nguồn, nguồn trong emr/his, source, collection method, cách thu thập, phương pháp thu thập}; role {vai trò, role}; objective {mục tiêu, objective(s)}; form {form, biểu mẫu, visit, lần thu thập, thời điểm}; required {required, bắt buộc}; need {need, justification, lý do, lý do thu thập}. Type from the cell text in this order: time-to-event, binary (nhị giá), ordinal (thứ tự), categorical (phân loại, danh mục, nominal), count, continuous (liên tục), date, text; nothing matched = "" (a block). Variable code = the code column, else an ASCII slug of the label (≤ 32 chars, `_2` suffix on clashes). Coded types (binary, ordinal, categorical) take their value list from the values cell split on " / ", ";" or "," after dropping parentheses; other types take that cell as the unit. Form = the form column, else `main`. Required = `yes` unless the column says no/không/optional | the spec fixes the outputs but not how a free-form Vietnamese or English table is read; the cohort example's table (`examples/fictional-cohort/03_protocol/variables.md`) is the reference shape |
| D2 | `crf.py check` reads the SAP analysis register with `analysis_plan.parse_register`; the cells whose folded header is outcome, kết cục, covariates, biến hiệu chỉnh, exposure, phơi nhiễm, factor or yếu tố are split on "," ";" " và " " and " " hoặc " " or " after dropping parentheses; "như A1"/"as in"/"none"/"không" are skipped; row A0 (descriptive) is skipped. A term matches a dictionary row when ≥ 60 % of its content words (folded, ≥ 2 letters, stop-words and pure numbers removed) are in the row's label or code. An unmatched term is an `E-CRF` block at the register row's line. `03_protocol/ethics/crf-map.csv` (`term,variable`) maps a term the matcher misses; a map line naming a variable that is not in the dictionary is a block | the register is free text and names no variable codes (§3 "a variable used in the SAP register"); a visible, checked map is the honest escape hatch, and a missing variable can never be mapped away |
| D3 | `crf.py build` refuses (exit 1) unless G2 is open; `check` runs any time. Warns: a dictionary variable with no objective (variables.md) that no register term uses (data minimisation); a direct identifier (full name, address, phone, email, national ID/CCCD/CMND, date of birth, record number, insurance number) with no Need/Lý do thu thập cell; `03_protocol/ethics/dossier.md` missing or not naming `crf.md`, `data-dictionary.csv` and `consent-form.md` | §3 "G2 must be open" and the two warn rules; the dossier check makes "lists every document" checkable |
| D4 | `lint_prose.py --profile consent` (default `academic`): `CF01` a sentence over 25 words (English) or 40 syllables (a sentence with Vietnamese letters: Vietnamese words are syllables), `CF02` a jargon term (fixed vi/en list: randomised, placebo, double-blind, cohort, biomarker, pharmacokinetic, adverse event, pseudonymised, de-identified; ngẫu nhiên hoá, giả dược, mù đôi, mù đơn, đoàn hệ, dấu ấn sinh học, dược động học, biến cố bất lợi, mã hoá) with no "(", "nghĩa là", "tức là", "that is", "which means", "meaning" or "i.e." within 25 characters after it, `CF03` a passive obligation ("must be signed", "is required to", "phải được", "bắt buộc phải", "được yêu cầu"). Warns only, exit 0 | §3 "consent profile … as warns"; thresholds are plan defaults |
| D5 | `journal_check.py` sources: NLM Catalog `esearch.fcgi?db=nlmcatalog&term=<ISSN>[issn]` (or `"<title>"[Title]`) then `efetch.fcgi?db=nlmcatalog&retmode=xml` (title, ISSNs, publisher, `IndexingSourceName` "MEDLINE" with `IndexingStatus` containing "Currently"); DOAJ `https://doaj.org/api/search/journals/issn:<ISSN>` (title, ISSNs, publisher, `bibjson.apc.has_apc` and `max` prices, `admin.seal`); Crossref `https://api.crossref.org/journals/<ISSN>` or `journals?query=<title>`. HTTP 404 = not found; any other HTTP, URL or JSON/XML error = exit 2 and nothing written. A title query resolves an ISSN from NLM or Crossref first, then every source is asked for that ISSN | §4 names the three sources; "network failures are 2, never not found" (AGENTS.md) |
| D6 | Signals (text + source): ISSN not found in any of the three; invalid ISSN check digit; `--claim medline` not confirmed as currently indexed by the NLM Catalog; `--claim scopus` ("not checkable with the open sources used here"); `--open-access` and absent from DOAJ; publisher or title differing across sources (folded, legal suffixes dropped, neither contains the other); each `--concern` from the author. The script never prints or stores the word "predatory" (tested); the skill links Think. Check. Submit. | §4 signal list and "never a verdict" (§2, §12.4) |
| D7 | `08_submission/journals.json` = `{"journals": [record]}`; a record has `key` (ISSN or folded title), `query`, `looked_up`, `title`/`issn`/`publisher` lists of `{value, source}`, `medline {value, detail, source, url}`, `doaj {listed, has_apc, apc, seal, source, url}`, `crossref {listed, source, url}`, `claims`, `signals`, `notes` and `picked`. `note <key> --source-url URL [--scope --scope-url --words --abstract --ref-style]` records what Claude read on the journal's own pages, each with URL and date. `pick <key> --by NAME` writes `target.journal` (title), `target.word_limit` (abstract limit) and `target.text_words` (main-text limit) into `project.yaml` and nothing else; `citation_style` is left alone (the style is shown in `journals.md`) | §4 "every field with its source and lookup date", "only then the skill writes `target.journal` and the limits"; §9 "no new YAML keys beyond existing `target.*`" — the existing keys hold the limits (`case_lint.py` and `manuscript-writing` read them) |
| D8 | Journal unit tests use synthetic NLM XML, DOAJ and Crossref JSON built in the test with fictional journals and ISSNs `0000-0000`/`0000-0019`. During Task 2 the implementer makes one live call per source for a title found by a live search (never a typed ISSN), saves the raw answers to the scratch folder only, and aligns the parser's element and key names if they differ; nothing captured is committed | unit tests stay offline and free of real journal facts; the parser is still grounded in real responses |
| D9 | `response.py <p> init <file>` splits comments: a line "Editor…"/"Biên tập…" starts the editor section, "Reviewer N"/"Referee N"/"Reviewer #N"/"(Người) phản biện N" starts reviewer N; text before any header is the editor's. Inside a section, numbered or bulleted lines ("1.", "1)", "(1)", "-", "*", "•", "Comment 1:") start items and following lines continue them; a section with no numbering splits on blank lines. HTML comments (a "fictional" label) are ignored. Ids `E1…`, `R<n>.1…`. `09_revision/comments.json` = `{deliverable, source, source_sha256, created, approved, items: [{id, who, text}], submitted: {<project-relative path>: sha256}}`; the snapshot mirrors paths under `09_revision/submitted/` | §5 gives ids but no splitting rules or schema |
| D10 | `init --deliverable manuscript\|case-report\|review` (default manuscript). `init` refuses (exit 1) when `comments.json` exists, and when G4 is not currently open for that deliverable unless `--unapproved` (recorded as `"approved": false`), because the snapshot must be the submitted version | §5 speaks only of "the manuscript", but case reports and reviews are also submitted; a re-init after revising would snapshot the revised text and void the check |
| D11 | `09_revision/response.md`: one section per item, heading `## <id>` (text after the id is free); lines starting `>` quote the comment and are not response text; a change is `[[CHANGE: <project-relative file> \| "<exact new text>"]]` on one line; a quote is found whitespace-insensitively after NFC normalisation; its section name is the nearest Markdown heading above it in the current draft | §5 gives the locator, not the file layout |
| D12 | `E-RESP` rules: block — an item with no response; a section id not in `comments.json`; a CHANGE file outside the project or missing; a CHANGE quote not in the current draft; a CHANGE quote already in the submitted snapshot; **a response that claims a change ("we have revised/added/clarified…", "has been revised…", "đã sửa/thêm/bổ sung/chỉnh/viết lại/xoá/cập nhật/làm rõ/thay đổi/điều chỉnh") with no CHANGE locator**; **a `NEW-ANALYSIS:` line (awaits the author; never sent)**. Numbers: each response line (quotes, locators and `NEW-ANALYSIS:` lines removed; item ids and page/line references masked) is rendered with `render_results.render_text` and checked with `check_numbers.find_unmatched` → `E-NUM block 09_revision/response.md:<n>`. Warn — no CHANGE and no "no change because …"/"không sửa/thay đổi vì …" | §5 lists four rules; scenario 4 ("reports the block" for "we have revised the Methods" with no change) and "never run silently" need the two bold rules |
| D13 | `gate.py` gains `FINAL_NAMES` (`thesis.docx`, `manuscript.docx`, `case-report.docx`, `review.docx`, `response-letter.docx`), `final_deliverable(project, name)` (the response letter belongs to `comments.json`'s deliverable, default manuscript) and `final_problems(project, name)` = `export_problems(project, d)` plus, for the response letter, every `E-RESP`/`E-NUM` block of `response.check`. `build_docx.py` and the hook call it; the hook also treats `response.py <project> build` as a final export | §5 "the hook treats `out/response-letter.docx` like the other final names"; one rule for every export path |
| D14 | C9: `gate.py check` prints `G<n>: open (locked, N file(s) unchanged) — <results locked \| approved> by X on DATE` for G1–G3 when files are locked, `G<n>: open — approved by X on DATE` without files; G4 keeps its wording | §8 names G3 only; G2 locks files too and was misread the same way; G4 already says "approved after a clean audit" |
| D15 | C2: on Windows only, `gate_hook.map_path` maps Git Bash `/tmp` and `/tmp/...` to `tempfile.gettempdir()`; elsewhere `/tmp` is a real folder | Git Bash's `/tmp` is the user temp folder on Windows |
| D16 | Autorater: old plugins = `git archive <ref> plugins` extracted to scratch; new plugins = a copy of the working tree's `plugins/`; each side runs the scenario recipe (`claude -p <prompt> --model sonnet --allowedTools <case> --max-turns <case> --setting-sources "" --strict-mcp-config --mcp-config '{"mcpServers":{}}' --no-chrome --output-format stream-json --verbose --plugin-dir …`) in its own copy of the fixture, transcript outside it. Materials per side: final answer, tool calls (name + first 200 characters of input), changed or new files (≤ 6 000 characters each, 30 000 in all). Judge: `claude -p --model opus --allowedTools Read` in a scratch folder holding `materials.md` (A then B) and `rubric.md`; answer one fenced JSON object `{"winner": "A"\|"B"\|"tie", "reasons": "<≤ 80 words>", "rubric": [{"item": "...", "A": "met\|partly\|not met", "B": "..."}]}`. Two judgements with the sides swapped; the verdict is `old`, `new` or `tie` only when both map to the same side, else `inconsistent` (an unparseable answer counts as disagreement). Result `evals/results/<case>-<UTC stamp>.json` with base ref, HEAD and dirty flag, per-side cost and turns, both judgements, verdict, total cost | §6 fixes the method, not the mechanics or the JSON shape |
| D17 | A case is `evals/cases/<name>/{case.json,prompt.md,rubric.md}`; `case.json` = `{"fixture": "<repo-relative folder>", "allowed_tools", "max_turns", "skills": [skills it exercises]}`. The three starter cases reuse the P4/P5 scenario fixtures `discussion-overclaim`, `case-identifiers`, `sr-pool-heterogeneous`. `autorater.py list [--skill S]` prints the cases that exercise a skill (the AGENTS.md rule uses it) | §6 "fixture project"; reusing fixtures avoids a second copy that could drift |
| D18 | `plugins/<p>/.codex-plugin/plugin.json` and `.antigravity-plugin/plugin.json` follow the grand-rounds 0.2.0 field set (names and texts our own; no `logo`/`brandColor`-referenced assets since the repo has none; Codex `"skills": "./skills/"` only for plugins that have `skills/`); versions equal `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json → metadata.version` (`test_manifests.py`) | §7 |
| D19 | `site/index.html`: one static file, inline CSS, no script, both languages on the page (`lang="vi"` and `lang="en"` sections), light/dark through `prefers-color-scheme`. `.github/workflows/pages.yml` deploys `site/` with `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact`, `actions/deploy-pages`; it runs on `workflow_dispatch`, and on pushes to `main` touching `site/**` only when the repository variable `PAGES_ENABLED` is `true` | §7 and §11: a deploy job on a repository without Pages would fail on every push to main until the owner enables Pages |
| D20 | C3: before E-NUM parsing, `check_numbers` blanks a 1900–2100 integer (or a range of two) preceded by "năm", "in", "since", "from", "until", "during", "between", "before", "after", "từ", "đến", "trước", "sau", "giai đoạn", "thời kỳ", "period", a month name, "tháng N/" or "tháng N năm", "N/", "et al.," or "và cộng sự", a year inside a parenthesis with a word of ≥ 2 letters ("(Nguyen et al., 2020)") and a parenthesised year range ("(2021–2023)"), unless the year is followed by patients/participants/people/cases/subjects/người/bệnh nhân/ca/trường hợp; every other 1900–2100 integer is checked like any number | §8 C3 lists the contexts; "giai đoạn 2021–2022" and "(2021–2023)" occur in the cohort example and the P4 fixtures (a dry run of the rule without them broke `test_g4_export_blocked`); the follow-word exception keeps "in 1995 patients" checked |
| D21 | C4: `check_numbers.trend_findings(text, results)` warns (`E-NUM warn`) per sentence containing "tăng dần", "giảm dần", "xu hướng", "peak/peaked/peaks", "trend(s)" or "increasing(ly)" unless the sentence holds ≥ 2 distinct `{{R-…}}` ids or one id whose ledger label names a trend test (trend, xu hướng, Cochran-Armitage, Jonckheere, Mann-Kendall, Cuzick, p for trend, joinpoint), or the sentence is negated (no, not, without, không, chưa). It runs on the text with placeholders (`check_numbers.py` and `audit.py`) | §8 C4 rule; negations deny a trend rather than claim one |
| D22 | C5: `figures.stale_findings(project, files)` reads every image a draft shows whose name is `F<n>[.poster].(png\|svg\|pdf)`, loads `F<n>.data.json` beside it and blocks (`E-NUM`, audit row "results numbers") when a value's ledger `ref` no longer exists or its `est`/`lo`/`hi` differ from the ledger `value`/`ci`, or when the recorded `data_file` hash differs from the file; a missing `data.json` warns | §8 C5 "as posters do": documents have no render digest, so the numbers drawn are compared with their sources directly |
| D23 | C6: verified, not rebuilt — `check_pptx.py` S22 already flags `[@key]`, `@key`, backticks and `{{…}}` (tests in `test_check_pptx.py`); "typed numbers" is closed by reading `03_protocol/sample-size.md` as a reference source in `consistency_audit.py`'s same-quantity check only (none of its other checks run on it), so a slide or chapter that types a number differing from it under the same key words is an `E-CONS` **warn** | P3 finding: sample-size figures typed from `sample-size.md` that "no check reads"; a dry run as a block flagged the cohort example's deck ("535" per group against "n yêu cầu = 1.070" total) and broke `test_fictional_cohort_has_no_blocks`, so it warns |
| D24 | C7: day + month without a year (`5 March`, `March 5`, `ngày 5 tháng 3`, `ngày 05/03`) is an `E-CASE` block | §8 C7 |
| D25 | C10: the implementer runs `search_lit.py search '42776991[pmid] AND "<heading>"[Mesh]'` for the heading the references name and for "Polycystic Ovary Syndrome", records date, query and hit count beside the claim in `references/literature/search-strategy.md`, `references/evidence-policy.md` and `references/workflows/literature-search.md`, and corrects the heading if the probe disagrees | §8 C10; the PMID is already in the repo, the heading must come from a tool |
| D26 | C11: `tests/requirements-ci.txt` pins the six direct dependencies (`pandas scipy statsmodels openpyxl matplotlib python-pptx`) to versions that have wheels for both CPython 3.10 and 3.12 on Linux and Windows, confirmed with `pip download --only-binary=:all: --python-version 3.10\|3.12 --platform …`; transitive packages are not pinned | §8 C11; the CI matrix is 3.10 and 3.12 on ubuntu and windows |
| D27 | D4: `grade.py` writes the SoF outcome cell through `grade.outcome_cell(name)`, which puts `<!-- not-a-result: outcome name -->` right after every number in the name | the E-NUM marker must directly follow its number |
| D28 | D5: `meta.py r-status` prints the Microsoft Store hint when `meta.ms_store_python(sys.executable)` is true (Windows and the path contains `WindowsApps`) and R or metafor is not found: R started from this Python may not see the user library; set `R_LIBS_USER` to the folder R prints for `Sys.getenv("R_LIBS_USER")`, or `project.yaml → tools.rscript`, or use a python.org Python | §8 D5 |
| D29 | K5: reviewer reports are `reviews/<agent>-<d>.md` (`reviews/consistency-<d>.md` for the consistency auditor) via `reviews.report_rel(agent, d)`; `reviews.report_path(project, agent, d)` returns that file, else the legacy `reviews/<agent>.md` unless all its scores blocks name other deliverables (a malformed legacy file stays readable so `worklog` names the problem), else the per-deliverable path (absent, used in messages). `worklog.score`, `worklog.snapshot` and `audit.check_reviews` use it; snapshots keep the file's own name; `audit.pick` tries the per-deliverable name, then the legacy one | §8 K5 "legacy fallback as for checklists" (`guideline_checklist.checklist_path`) |
| D30 | A1: `worklog.unscored_reports(project, d) -> (last_round, [paths])` lists reports with a scores block for `<d>` whose bytes differ from the copy in the last scored round's snapshot (all such reports when no round is scored); `audit.check_reviews` adds one `E-REV warn` per path. The cap of 3 rounds stays `worklog.MAX_ROUNDS` | §8 A1 "audit warns when reports are newer than the last scored round"; content comparison is immune to clock and copy-time effects |
| D31 | A2: the six agents get `tools: Read, Grep, Glob, Bash` and `disallowedTools: Write, Edit, NotebookEdit`, never write files (Bash included) and return the report in the reply; the orchestrating skill saves it to `reviews/<agent>-<d>.md`. The reporting-guideline-checker validates its checklist with `guideline_checklist.py . --deliverable <d> --stdin` (new: checklist JSON from stdin) and returns it as a ```` ```checklist ```` block the skill saves to `reviews/guideline-checklist-<d>.json` | §8 A2 and §12.9 ("reviewer agents keep no write access") |
| D32 | The review-round procedure (dispatch, save replies, score once per pass, at most 3, exit-2 handling, KEEP/REVERT/STOP, NEW-ANALYSIS route, never edit what you did not draft) lives once in `references/writing/review-round.md`; the four writing skills point to it in a few words | `thesis-writing` is at 499 words and `manuscript-writing` at 501 by `wc`; one reference keeps the rule identical everywhere |
| D33 | A3 reads §8 "never edit a chapter the author has not accepted" as: edit only sections you drafted in this run; a reused (accepted) section gets mechanical E-FMT/E-NUM fixes only; any other author file (not drafted by you, not accepted) is never edited — its findings go to the author | the P4 run edited the author's unaccepted `ch3` during the discussion task; the literal sentence would forbid editing Claude's own drafts |
| D34 | `init_project.py` creates `03_protocol/ethics/` for every type and `08_submission/`, `09_revision/` for `manuscript`, `case-report`, `systematic-review` and `review` (thesis types, `de-tai-co-so` and `poster-only` get `ethics/` only) | §9 "study types that publish" |
| D35 | Skill-change evidence: new skills → scenarios 1–4 (Task 12); A1/A2/K5/A3 text → autorater cases `thesis-discussion-overclaim`, `case-anonymisation`, `sr-pooling` (Task 13) and the S1 rerun; A5 (poster) and A6 (defense-prep) → stated reason in the commit (one explicit step each; their P3 scenarios `poster-87dpi`/`mzl-consistency` are not rerun, cost); `data-preparation` dictionary line → stated reason (source preference only, exercised by the E2E) | AGENTS.md rule plus §6 "or states why not" |
| D36 | `tests/scenarios/make_p6_fixtures.py` builds the four P6 fixtures from the project template; G4 in the response fixtures is opened from a fixture `reviews/audit.json` that hashes the drafts (as `test_gate_hook.open_g4` does); everything fictional | P5 fixture pattern |
| D37 | S1: rerun `sr-pool-heterogeneous` once after Task 10. S2: `scoping-asks-pooling` gets numeric charting columns and a prompt that presses for a weighted pooled mean, so the unaided model computes one (RED must fail; if RED still refuses, record "does not discriminate" and keep the unit test as the guard). S3: `undeclared-deviation` rerun twice; both must be GREEN | §8 S1–S3, §1 "reworked P5 scenarios are GREEN" |
| D38 | E2E human acts are scripted inputs performed by the controller (P5 D30): G-gate openings, the author's journal pick, the author's NEW-ANALYSIS decision; the fictional reviewer comments are committed as `09_revision/comments-received.md`, labelled fictional | reproducibility; Claude never acts as the author or the reviewer |
| D39 | Autorater dogfood results go to `evals/README.md` ("Dogfood" section), not `BASELINE.md` (owned by the scenario task in the same batch) | disjoint file ownership |
| D40 | Release 0.7.0 only; the owner sign-off steps for 1.0.0 are an appendix, not a task | §1, §11, §12.1 |
| D41 | `data-preparation` builds its codebook from `03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv` when present (names, types, values kept), else from `variables.md` | §3 "its codebook reads the dictionary when present" |
| D42 | A journal revision does not reopen the internal reviewer loop: `reviewer-response` goes from the revised drafts to assemble, audit and G4; the old reports' `E-REV` digest warnings are expected and stated | `worklog.py score` refuses a fourth round or a stopped loop; spec §5 asks for "a fresh audit", not a reviewer round; the journal's reviewers are that round's reviewers |

## File structure (P6)

```
plugins/orchestra/scripts/crf.py                   NEW build/check: CRF + data dictionary, E-CRF
plugins/orchestra/scripts/journal_check.py         NEW lookup/note/table/pick: NLM Catalog, DOAJ, Crossref; signals
plugins/orchestra/scripts/response.py              NEW init/check/build: comments, snapshot, E-RESP, response letter
plugins/orchestra/scripts/gate.py                  + FINAL_NAMES, final_deliverable, final_problems; C9 wording
plugins/orchestra/scripts/gate_hook.py             + response-letter and `response.py build` as final exports; C2 /tmp
plugins/orchestra/scripts/build_docx.py            + response-letter.docx final name (via gate.final_problems)
plugins/orchestra/scripts/lint_prose.py            + --profile consent (CF01–CF03)
plugins/orchestra/scripts/cite_render.py           C1 retracted is True; K3 --help exit 0
plugins/orchestra/scripts/check_numbers.py         C3 year context; C4 trend_findings
plugins/orchestra/scripts/case_lint.py             C7 day+month blocks
plugins/orchestra/scripts/grade.py                 D4 outcome_cell
plugins/orchestra/scripts/meta.py                  D5 ms_store_python hint
plugins/orchestra/scripts/figures.py               C5 stale_findings

plugins/orchestra/references/ethics/{consent-elements,dossier-template}.md        NEW

plugins/orchestra/references/{literature/search-strategy.md,evidence-policy.md}   C10
plugins/orchestra/agents/*.md                                                      A2
plugins/orchestra/skills/{ethics-and-crf,journal-selection,reviewer-response}/SKILL.md   NEW
plugins/orchestra/skills/{thesis-writing,manuscript-writing,case-report,systematic-review,research-poster,defense-prep,data-preparation}/SKILL.md   edited
plugins/{orchestra,phi-guard}/{.codex-plugin,.antigravity-plugin}/plugin.json      NEW
tools/autorater.py, evals/README.md, evals/cases/{thesis-discussion-overclaim,case-anonymisation,sr-pooling}/   NEW
site/index.html, .github/workflows/pages.yml, tests/requirements-ci.txt            NEW; .github/workflows/ci.yml edited
tests/unit/test_{crf,journal_check,response,autorater,ci_config}.py                 NEW
tests/unit/test_{lint_prose,gate,gate_hook,build_docx,cite_render,check_numbers,case_lint,grade,meta,figures,consistency_audit,reviews,worklog,guideline_checklist,audit,agents,init_project,writing_commands,manifests,scenarios,examples}.py   extended
tests/scenarios/{crf-missing-variable,journal-predatory-signals,reviewer-asks-new-analysis,response-claims-unmade-change}.md, make_p6_fixtures.py, fixtures/<id>/
examples/fictional-cohort/  (03_protocol/ethics/, 08_submission/, 09_revision/, out/ethics/, out/response-letter.docx)
AGENTS.md, .gitignore, README.md, CHANGELOG.md, NOTICE.md, main spec, manifests  (release)
```

## Execution batches

Tasks in a batch own disjoint files (listed) and may run in parallel worktrees; the controller merges a batch, runs the full suite and the lint, then starts the next. At most about six implementers run at once: start Batch A's first six, then Tasks 7 and 8 as slots free (they share no file with the others).

- **Batch A (8 tasks, all independent):**
  - Task 1 owns `crf.py`, `lint_prose.py`, `references/ethics/**`, `tests/unit/test_crf.py`, `tests/unit/test_lint_prose.py`.
  - Task 2 owns `journal_check.py`, `references/submission/**`, `tests/unit/test_journal_check.py`.
  - Task 3 owns `response.py`, `gate.py`, `gate_hook.py`, `build_docx.py`, `tests/unit/test_{response,gate,gate_hook,build_docx}.py`.
  - Task 4 owns `tools/autorater.py`, `evals/**`, `.gitignore`, `AGENTS.md`, `tests/unit/test_autorater.py`.
  - Task 5 owns `plugins/*/.codex-plugin/**`, `plugins/*/.antigravity-plugin/**`, `site/**`, `.github/workflows/pages.yml`, `README.md`, `tests/unit/test_manifests.py`.
  - Task 6 owns `cite_render.py`, `check_numbers.py`, `case_lint.py`, `grade.py`, `meta.py`, `tests/unit/test_{cite_render,check_numbers,case_lint,grade,meta}.py`.
  - Task 7 owns `figures.py`, `consistency_audit.py`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `tests/requirements-ci.txt`, `references/literature/search-strategy.md`, `references/evidence-policy.md`, `references/workflows/literature-search.md`, `tests/unit/test_{figures,consistency_audit,ci_config}.py`.
  - Task 8 owns `crolib/reviews.py`, `worklog.py`, `guideline_checklist.py`, `tests/unit/test_{reviews,worklog,guideline_checklist}.py`.
- **Batch B (3 tasks, in parallel):**
  - Task 9 (needs 6, 7, 8) owns `audit.py`, `tests/unit/test_audit.py`.
  - Task 10 (needs 8) owns `agents/*.md`, `references/writing/review-round.md`, skills `thesis-writing`, `manuscript-writing`, `case-report`, `systematic-review`, `research-poster`, `defense-prep`, workflows `thesis.md`, `manuscript.md`, `case-report.md`, `systematic-review.md`, `research-poster.md`, `defense-prep.md`, `tests/unit/test_agents.py`.
  - Task 11 (needs 1, 2, 3) owns `init_project.py`, skills `ethics-and-crf`, `journal-selection`, `reviewer-response`, `data-preparation`, workflows `ethics-and-crf.md`, `journal-selection.md`, `reviewer-response.md`, `data-preparation.md`, `tests/unit/test_{init_project,writing_commands}.py`.
- **Batch C (2 tasks, after B):** Task 12 (scenarios) owns `tests/scenarios/**` and `tests/unit/test_scenarios.py`; Task 13 (autorater dogfood) owns `evals/README.md` only.
- **Batch D (2 tasks):** Task 14 (E2E) owns `examples/fictional-cohort/**` and `tests/unit/test_examples.py`; Task 15 (release) owns the manifests' `version` fields, `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `README.md`, `NOTICE.md`, the main spec. Task 15 Steps 1–3 run in parallel with Task 14; its Step 4 (E2E line, final suite, commit) runs after Task 14 is merged.

**Controller prerequisites.** Python 3.10 and 3.12 available for the C11 wheel check (Task 7 uses `pip download`, no install). Network for Tasks 2 (one live shape check per source), 7 (C10 MeSH probe, C11 wheel check), 12 (scenarios 2 and the literature parts), 13 and 14. The `claude` CLI, logged in, for Tasks 12, 13 and 14. Budget guides: Task 12 scenarios and reruns USD 25; Task 13 autorater dogfood USD 20 (stop and record when reached); Task 14 E2E USD 80. A stage stalled more than 45 minutes is killed and rerun once. No plan step installs software; if an implementer reports a missing tool, the controller decides.

**Pre-flight done while writing this plan.** The code of Tasks 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, 9 and 11 and the Task 12 fixture generator was applied to a scratch copy of the repository at 051aa22 and the listed tests passed there (Windows, Python 3.14; no f-string or stdlib feature newer than 3.10 is used). It found and fixed three things now in the plan: "giai đoạn 2021–2022" and "(2021–2023)" as year contexts (D20), the sample-size check as a warn (D23), and the legacy-report fallback keeping malformed files readable (D29). Tasks 5 and 10 were checked for anchors and word counts only. Implementers still run every step; CI (3.10 and 3.12) is the real check.

### Task 1: `crf.py` (E-CRF), ethics references, consent readability profile (spec 3)

**Files:**
- Create: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/crf.py`, `plugins/orchestra/references/ethics/consent-elements.md`, `plugins/orchestra/references/ethics/dossier-template.md`, `tests/unit/test_crf.py`
- Modify: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/lint_prose.py` (docstring, `--profile`, `lint_consent`), `tests/unit/test_lint_prose.py` (new class)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `gate.check_gate(project, "G2") -> (bool, list[str])`, `analysis_plan.parse_register(sap_text) -> list[dict]` (keys are lower-cased headers; `id`), `crolib.projectcfg.output_lang(project)`.
- Produces: CLI `crf.py <project> {build,check}`; importable `crf.build(project) -> tuple[int, list[str]]` (raises `crf.Refused`, `crf.InputError`), `crf.check(project) -> list[tuple[level, loc, msg]]`, `crf.parse_variables(text) -> list[dict]` (keys `variable, label, type, unit, values (list), range, source_form, required, section, objective, role, need, source, line`), `crf.fold(s) -> str`, constants `CRF = "03_protocol/ethics/crf.md"`, `DICT = "03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv"`, `MAP = "03_protocol/ethics/crf-map.csv"`, `DOSSIER = "03_protocol/ethics/dossier.md"`, `FIELDS`. CLI `lint_prose.py --profile consent FILE…` (codes `CF01`–`CF03`, warn only, exit 0) and `lint_prose.lint_consent(path) -> {"file", "sentences", "findings"}`. Task 11 (skill `ethics-and-crf`) cites both CLIs; Task 12 scenario 1 relies on the block text `uses '<term>', which matches no CRF variable`.
- Rules: D1, D2, D3, D4.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `tests/unit/test_crf.py`:

```python
import contextlib
import csv
import io
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import crf
import gate
import init_project

VARIABLES = """# Bảng biến số nghiên cứu (hư cấu, cho kiểm thử)

## 1. Biến phơi nhiễm

| Mục tiêu | Biến | Định nghĩa / cách đo | Loại | Giá trị / đơn vị | Nguồn | Vai trò |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MT2 | HbA1c lúc nhập viện (phân nhóm) | HbA1c trong 48 giờ đầu | Thứ tự (ordinal) | < 7% / 7–9% / > 9% (nhóm < 7% tham chiếu) | EMR | Phơi nhiễm |

## 2. Biến kết cục

| Mục tiêu | Biến | Định nghĩa / cách đo | Loại | Giá trị / đơn vị | Nguồn | Vai trò |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MT1 | Tái nhập viện 1 năm (mọi nguyên nhân) | Nhập viện lại trong 365 ngày | Nhị giá | Có / Không | HIS | Kết cục chính |

## 3. Biến nhiễu

| Mục tiêu | Biến | Định nghĩa / cách đo | Loại | Giá trị / đơn vị | Nguồn | Vai trò |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MT2 | Tuổi | Tuổi khi nhập viện | Liên tục | Năm | EMR | Nhiễu |
| MT2 | Giới | Giới trong hồ sơ | Phân loại nhị giá | Nam / Nữ | EMR | Nhiễu |
|  | Họ tên | Họ tên trong hồ sơ | Văn bản |  | EMR | Đối chiếu |
"""
SAP = """# SAP (hư cấu)

## Analysis register

| ID | Objective | Outcome | Population | Method | Covariates | Multiplicity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | MT1 | Đặc điểm chung theo 3 nhóm HbA1c (Bảng 1) | Tất cả | Mô tả | Không | Không |
| A1 | MT2 | Tái nhập viện mọi nguyên nhân trong 1 năm | Như A0 | Hồi quy logistic | {cov} | Không |
"""
DOSSIER = ("# Hồ sơ đạo đức\n\n| Tài liệu | Trạng thái |\n|---|---|\n| crf.md | drafted |\n"
           "| data-dictionary.csv | drafted |\n| consent-form.md | drafted |\n")


class TestCrf(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "Fictional cohort", "thesis-masters", "cohort", "vi", "vi")
        self.write("03_protocol/variables.md", VARIABLES)
        self.write("03_protocol/sap.md", SAP.format(cov="Tuổi, giới (xem mục 8)"))

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def write(self, rel, text):
        path = self.p / rel
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

    def lock_g2(self, files=("03_protocol/variables.md", "03_protocol/sap.md")):
        gate.open_gate(self.p, "G2", by="Example author", files=list(files))

    def cli(self, *argv):
        out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            code = crf.main([str(self.p), *argv])
        return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()

    def dictionary(self):
        with open(self.p / crf.DICT, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh))

    def test_build_needs_g2(self):
        code, out, _ = self.cli("build")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("G2", out)
        self.assertFalse((self.p / crf.CRF).exists())

    def test_build_writes_crf_and_dictionary(self):
        self.lock_g2()
        code, out, err = self.cli("build")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        raw = (self.p / crf.DICT).read_bytes()
        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", raw)
        self.assertEqual(raw.decode("utf-8").splitlines()[0], "variable,label,type,unit,values,range,source_form,required")
        rows = {r["variable"]: r for r in self.dictionary()}
        self.assertEqual(set(rows), {"hba1c_luc_nhap_vien", "tai_nhap_vien_1_nam", "tuoi", "gioi", "ho_ten"})
        self.assertEqual((rows["tuoi"]["type"], rows["tuoi"]["unit"]), ("continuous", "Năm"))
        self.assertEqual((rows["gioi"]["type"], rows["gioi"]["values"]), ("binary", "Nam|Nữ"))
        self.assertEqual(rows["hba1c_luc_nhap_vien"]["type"], "ordinal")
        self.assertEqual(len(rows["hba1c_luc_nhap_vien"]["values"].split("|")), 3)
        self.assertEqual(rows["ho_ten"]["type"], "text")
        self.assertTrue(all(r["source_form"] == "main" and r["required"] == "yes" for r in rows.values()))
        text = (self.p / crf.CRF).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("## Biểu mẫu: main", text)
        self.assertIn("### 2. Biến kết cục", text)
        self.assertIn("| tai_nhap_vien_1_nam |", text)

    def test_clean_check_warns_identifier_and_minimisation(self):
        self.lock_g2()
        self.cli("build")
        self.write(crf.DOSSIER, DOSSIER)
        found = crf.check(self.p)
        self.assertFalse([f for f in found if f[0] == "block"], found)
        warns = " ".join(m for lv, _, m in found if lv == "warn")
        self.assertIn("ho_ten: a direct identifier", warns)
        self.assertIn("ho_ten: no objective", warns)
        self.assertNotIn("tuoi:", warns)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("check")[0], 0)

    def test_register_variable_missing_blocks(self):
        self.write("03_protocol/sap.md", SAP.format(cov="Tuổi, giới, BMI"))
        self.lock_g2()
        self.cli("build")
        code, out, _ = self.cli("check")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("E-CRF block 03_protocol/sap.md:", out)
        self.assertIn("A1 (covariates) uses 'BMI', which matches no CRF variable", out)

    def test_crf_map_must_name_a_dictionary_variable(self):
        self.write("03_protocol/sap.md", SAP.format(cov="Tuổi, giới, BMI"))
        self.lock_g2()
        self.cli("build")
        self.write(crf.MAP, "term,variable\nBMI,bmi\n")
        self.assertIn("not in the data dictionary", " ".join(m for lv, _, m in crf.check(self.p) if lv == "block"))
        self.write(crf.MAP, "term,variable\nBMI,tuoi\n")
        self.assertFalse([f for f in crf.check(self.p) if f[0] == "block"])

    def test_dictionary_and_crf_problems_block(self):
        self.lock_g2()
        self.cli("build")
        rows = self.dictionary()
        rows[0]["type"] = ""
        rows[1]["values"] = ""
        rows.append(dict(rows[2], variable="extra_field"))
        with open(self.p / crf.DICT, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=crf.FIELDS, lineterminator="\n")
            w.writeheader()
            w.writerows(rows)
        msgs = [m for lv, _, m in crf.check(self.p) if lv == "block"]
        self.assertTrue(any(m.endswith(": no type") for m in msgs), msgs)
        self.assertTrue(any("without a value list" in m for m in msgs), msgs)
        self.assertTrue(any("extra_field is in the data dictionary but not in the CRF" in m for m in msgs), msgs)

    def test_missing_outputs_block_and_errors(self):
        self.assertEqual(len([f for f in crf.check(self.p) if f[0] == "block"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(crf.main([str(self.t / "none"), "check"]), 2)
        (self.p / "03_protocol" / "variables.md").unlink()
        self.lock_g2(files=("03_protocol/sap.md",))
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("build")[0], 2)

    def test_english_headers_and_dossier_warn(self):
        self.write("03_protocol/variables.md", "| Variable | Definition | Type | Values / unit | Role | Objective |\n"
                   "|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Smoking status | self-report | Categorical | never / former / current "
                   "| Confounder | O2 |\n| Weight | scale | Continuous | kg | Descriptive | O1 |\n")
        self.write("03_protocol/sap.md", SAP.format(cov="Smoking status"))
        self.lock_g2()
        self.cli("build")
        rows = {r["variable"]: r for r in self.dictionary()}
        self.assertEqual(rows["smoking_status"]["values"], "never|former|current")
        self.assertEqual(rows["weight"]["unit"], "kg")
        warns = [m for lv, _, m in crf.check(self.p) if lv == "warn"]
        self.assertIn("no dossier.md listing the ethics documents and their status", warns)

    def test_fold_and_type_order(self):
        self.assertEqual(crf.fold("  Đái Tháo   Đường "), "dai thao duong")
        vs = crf.parse_variables("| Biến | Loại |\n|---|---|\n| A | Thứ tự (ordinal)/phân loại |\n| B | Phân loại nhị giá |\n"
                                 "| C | Thời gian-đến-sự kiện |\n| D | ? |\n")
        self.assertEqual([v["type"] for v in vs], ["ordinal", "binary", "time-to-event", ""])
        self.assertEqual([v["variable"] for v in crf.parse_variables("| Code | Biến | Loại |\n|---|---|---|\n"
                                                                     "| x1 | A | Liên tục |\n| x1 | B | Liên tục |\n")],
                         ["x1", "x1_2"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

Append to `tests/unit/test_lint_prose.py` (before the `if __name__` line):

```python
CONSENT = ("# Phiếu đồng ý (hư cấu)\n\n"
           "Bạn sẽ được chia ngẫu nhiên hoá vào một trong hai nhóm và nhóm còn lại sẽ dùng giả dược (thuốc không có "
           "hoạt chất) trong suốt thời gian nghiên cứu kéo dài mười hai tháng tại bệnh viện cùng với các lần tái khám "
           "định kỳ mỗi tháng một lần.\n\n"
           "Phiếu này phải được ký trước khi tham gia.\n\n"
           "Nghiên cứu là mù đôi (cả bạn và bác sĩ đều không biết bạn ở nhóm nào).\n")


class TestConsentProfile(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.f = self.d / "consent-form.md"
        self.f.write_text(CONSENT, encoding="utf-8")

    def tearDown(self):
        import shutil
        shutil.rmtree(self.d, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_codes_and_warn_only(self):
        res = lint_prose.lint_consent(str(self.f))
        codes = [(x["code"], x["line"]) for x in res["findings"]]
        self.assertIn(("CF01", 3), codes)
        self.assertIn(("CF02", 3), codes)
        self.assertIn(("CF03", 5), codes)
        self.assertTrue(all(x["level"] == "warn" for x in res["findings"]))
        msgs = " ".join(x["message"] for x in res["findings"])
        self.assertIn("ngẫu nhiên hoá", msgs)
        self.assertNotIn("giả dược", msgs)      # explained in the parenthesis right after it
        self.assertNotIn("mù đôi", msgs)

    def test_cli_exit_0_and_profiles(self):
        r = run_cli("--profile", "consent", self.f)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        out = r.stdout.decode("utf-8")
        self.assertIn("CF03 warn", out)
        self.assertIn("consent profile", out)
        self.assertNotIn("CF0", run_cli(self.f).stdout.decode("utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(run_cli("--profile", "consent", "--slides", self.f).returncode, 2)

    def test_english_sentence_limit(self):
        self.f.write_text("At each visit you answer a few short questions about your health and your treatment "
                          "during the study, which lasts one year.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual([x["code"] for x in lint_prose.lint_consent(str(self.f))["findings"]], [])
        self.f.write_text(" ".join(["word"] * 26) + ".\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual([x["code"] for x in lint_prose.lint_consent(str(self.f))["findings"]], ["CF01"])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `plugins/orchestra/scripts/crf.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""E-CRF: case report form and data dictionary from the locked variables table.

  python crf.py <project> build     03_protocol/variables.md -> 03_protocol/ethics/crf.md + data-dictionary.csv
  python crf.py <project> check     E-CRF findings

build needs G2 open (the protocol is locked). It reads every Markdown table of 03_protocol/variables.md that has a
variable (or code) column and a type column, Vietnamese or English headers, and writes one CRF section per form
(column Form/Biểu mẫu/Visit/Lần thu thập; none = one form "main"), grouped by the variables.md heading, plus
data-dictionary.csv with the header variable,label,type,unit,values,range,source_form,required.
check blocks: CRF or dictionary not built; a dictionary row without a type; a coded variable (binary, categorical,
ordinal) without a value list; CRF and dictionary listing different variables; a term of the SAP analysis register
(Outcome, Covariates, Exposure, Factor cells; A0 skipped) that matches no dictionary variable;
03_protocol/ethics/crf-map.csv (term,variable) maps a term the matcher misses and must name a dictionary variable.
check warns: a variable with no objective that no register term uses (data minimisation); a direct identifier
without a stated need (column Need/Lý do thu thập); dossier.md missing or not listing crf.md, data-dictionary.csv
and consent-form.md.
Exit codes: 0 = built / no block; 1 = G2 not open (build) or a block (check); 2 = usage, missing project or
variables.md, unreadable file. Standard library only.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import datetime as dt
import io
import re
import sys
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import projectcfg  # noqa: E402
import analysis_plan  # noqa: E402
import gate  # noqa: E402

VARIABLES = "03_protocol/variables.md"
SAP = "03_protocol/sap.md"
CRF = "03_protocol/ethics/crf.md"
DICT = "03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv"
MAP = "03_protocol/ethics/crf-map.csv"
DOSSIER = "03_protocol/ethics/dossier.md"
FIELDS = ("variable", "label", "type", "unit", "values", "range", "source_form", "required")
CODED = ("binary", "categorical", "ordinal")
HEADERS = {
    "code": {"code", "ma bien", "ma", "variable code", "variable_name"},
    "label": {"bien", "bien so", "variable", "ten bien", "name"},
    "definition": {"dinh nghia / cach do", "dinh nghia", "definition", "definition / measurement"},
    "type": {"loai", "loai bien", "kieu", "type"},
    "values": {"gia tri / don vi", "gia tri", "values", "values / unit", "unit or values"},
    "unit": {"don vi", "unit"},
    "range": {"range", "khoang", "khoang gia tri"},
    "source": {"nguon", "nguon trong emr/his", "source", "collection method", "cach thu thap", "phuong phap thu thap"},
    "role": {"vai tro", "role"},
    "objective": {"muc tieu", "objective", "objectives"},
    "form": {"form", "bieu mau", "visit", "lan thu thap", "thoi diem"},
    "required": {"required", "bat buoc"},
    "need": {"need", "justification", "ly do", "ly do thu thap"},
}
TYPES = [("time-to-event", ("thoi gian-den-su kien", "thoi gian den su kien", "time to event", "time-to-event", "survival")),
         ("binary", ("nhi gia", "binary", "dichotomous")),
         ("ordinal", ("thu tu", "ordinal")),
         ("categorical", ("phan loai", "categorical", "nominal", "danh muc")),
         ("count", ("dem", "count", "roi rac", "discrete")),
         ("continuous", ("lien tuc", "continuous", "numeric")),
         ("date", ("ngay thang", "date")),
         ("text", ("van ban", "free text", "text"))]
REGISTER_CELLS = {"outcome", "ket cuc", "covariates", "bien hieu chinh", "exposure", "phoi nhiem", "factor", "yeu to"}
SKIP_TERM = re.compile(r"^(?:nhu|as in|same as|see|xem)\b|^(?:khong|none|no|n/?a|-)$")
SPLIT = re.compile(r"\s*[,;]\s*|\s+(?:và|and|hoặc|or)\s+", re.IGNORECASE)
VSPLIT = re.compile(r"\s+/\s+|\s*;\s*|\s*,\s*")
PAREN = re.compile(r"\([^()]*\)")
STOP = {"trong", "cua", "va", "voi", "theo", "cac", "nhung", "mot", "hoac", "the", "of", "and", "or", "in", "at",
        "to", "for", "by", "with", "on", "per", "as", "an"}
IDENT = re.compile(r"\b(?:ho ten|ho va ten|ten benh nhan|full name|name|dia chi|address|so dien thoai|dien thoai|"
                   r"phone|email|cccd|cmnd|can cuoc|national id|ngay sinh|date of birth|dob|so ho so|ma ho so|"
                   r"medical record number|mrn|so the bhyt|bhyt|insurance number)\b")
TEXT = {"en": {"title": "Case report form (CRF)", "form": "Form",
               "cols": "| Code | Field | Type | Unit | Allowed values / range | Required |",
               "note": "Built by crf.py from 03_protocol/variables.md on {date}; edit variables.md and rebuild."},
        "vi": {"title": "Phiếu thu thập số liệu (CRF)", "form": "Biểu mẫu",
               "cols": "| Mã | Trường | Loại | Đơn vị | Giá trị cho phép / khoảng | Bắt buộc |",
               "note": "Tạo bởi crf.py từ 03_protocol/variables.md ngày {date}; sửa variables.md rồi tạo lại."}}


class InputError(Exception):
    """Exit 2."""


class Refused(Exception):
    """Exit 1."""


def fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", str(s).replace("đ", "d").replace("Đ", "D"))
    s = "".join(c for c in s if not unicodedata.combining(c))
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip().casefold()


def words(s: str) -> set[str]:
    return {w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", fold(s)) if len(w) >= 2 and w not in STOP and not w.isdigit()}


def _cells(line: str) -> list[str]:
    return [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]


def _column(header: str) -> str | None:
    h = fold(re.sub(r"[*_`]", "", header))
    return next((k for k, names in HEADERS.items() if h in names), None)


def _type(text: str) -> str:
    f = fold(text)
    return next((t for t, keys in TYPES if any(k in f for k in keys)), "")


def _slug(label: str, taken: set) -> str:
    base = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", fold(PAREN.sub(" ", label))).strip("_")[:32].strip("_") or "var"
    s, i = base, 2
    while s in taken:
        s, i = f"{base}_{i}", i + 1
    taken.add(s)
    return s


def _variable(rec: dict, heading: str, line: int, taken: set) -> dict:
    typ = _type(rec["type"])
    code = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_]+", "_", rec["code"]).strip("_").lower()
    if code:
        code = _slug(code, taken)           # a duplicate code gets a _2 suffix
    else:
        code = _slug(rec["label"], taken)
    unit, values = rec["unit"], []
    if typ in CODED:
        values = [v for v in (x.strip() for x in VSPLIT.split(PAREN.sub(" ", rec["values"]))) if v]
    elif rec["values"] and not unit:
        unit = rec["values"]
    required = "no" if fold(rec["required"]) in ("no", "khong", "optional", "tuy chon") else "yes"
    return {"variable": code, "label": rec["label"] or rec["code"], "type": typ, "unit": unit, "values": values,
            "range": rec["range"], "source_form": rec["form"] or "main", "required": required, "section": heading,
            "objective": rec["objective"], "role": rec["role"], "need": rec["need"], "source": rec["source"],
            "line": line}


def parse_variables(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    rows: list[dict] = []
    taken: set = set()
    lines, heading, i = text.splitlines(), "", 0
    while i < len(lines):
        m = re.match(r"^#{1,6}\s+(.*\S)\s*$", lines[i])
        if m:
            heading, i = m.group(1), i + 1
            continue
        if lines[i].strip().startswith("|") and i + 1 < len(lines) and re.match(r"^\s*\|?\s*:?-{3,}", lines[i + 1]):
            cols = [_column(h) for h in _cells(lines[i])]
            j, body = i + 2, []
            while j < len(lines) and lines[j].strip().startswith("|"):
                body.append((j + 1, _cells(lines[j])))
                j += 1
            if ("label" in cols or "code" in cols) and "type" in cols:
                for n, cells in body:
                    rec = dict.fromkeys(HEADERS, "")
                    for k, v in zip(cols, cells):
                        if k and not rec[k]:
                            rec[k] = re.sub(r"\*\*|__|`", "", v).strip()
                    if rec["label"] or rec["code"]:
                        rows.append(_variable(rec, heading, n, taken))
            i = j
            continue
        i += 1
    return rows


def _write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(text)


def _cell(s) -> str:
    return str(s).replace("|", "/").replace("\n", " ")


def crf_markdown(vs: list[dict], lang: str) -> str:
    t = TEXT.get(lang, TEXT["en"])
    out = [f"# {t['title']}", "", f"<!-- {t['note'].format(date=dt.date.today().isoformat())} -->", ""]
    for form in dict.fromkeys(v["source_form"] for v in vs):
        out += [f"## {t['form']}: {form}", ""]
        for sec in dict.fromkeys(v["section"] for v in vs if v["source_form"] == form):
            if sec:
                out += [f"### {sec}", ""]
            out += [t["cols"], "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
            for v in vs:
                if v["source_form"] == form and v["section"] == sec:
                    allowed = " · ".join(v["values"]) or v["range"]
                    out.append(f"| {v['variable']} | {_cell(v['label'])} | {v['type'] or '?'} | {_cell(v['unit'])} | "
                               f"{_cell(allowed)} | {v['required']} |")
            out.append("")
    return "\n".join(out)


def _load_variables(project: Path) -> list[dict]:
    path = project / VARIABLES
    if not path.is_file():
        raise InputError(f"{VARIABLES} not found: write the variables table first (protocol-design)")
    return parse_variables(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))


def build(project: Path) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    ok, why = gate.check_gate(project, "G2")
    if not ok:
        raise Refused("G2 is not open: the CRF is built from the locked protocol (" + "; ".join(why) + ")")
    vs = _load_variables(project)
    if not vs:
        raise InputError(f"{VARIABLES} has no table with a variable and a type column")
    _write(project / CRF, crf_markdown(vs, projectcfg.output_lang(project)))
    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator="\n")
    w.writerow(FIELDS)
    for v in vs:
        w.writerow([v["variable"], v["label"], v["type"], v["unit"], "|".join(v["values"]), v["range"],
                    v["source_form"], v["required"]])
    _write(project / DICT, buf.getvalue())
    return len(vs), [CRF, DICT]


def _read_dict(project: Path) -> list[dict] | None:
    p = project / DICT
    if not p.is_file():
        return None
    with open(p, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def _crf_codes(project: Path) -> set[str] | None:
    p = project / CRF
    if not p.is_file():
        return None
    codes = set()
    for line in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines():
        if line.strip().startswith("|"):
            c = _cells(line)[0]
            if c and not set(c) <= set("-: ") and fold(c) not in ("code", "ma"):
                codes.add(c)
    return codes


def register_terms(project: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, int]]:
    """(analysis id, column, term, SAP line) for the register cells that name variables (A0 skipped)."""
    p = project / SAP
    if not p.is_file():
        return []
    text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    try:
        reg = analysis_plan.parse_register(text)
    except ValueError:
        return []
    out = []
    for row in reg:
        if row.get("id") == "A0":
            continue
        m = re.search(rf"(?m)^\s*\|\s*{re.escape(row['id'])}\s*\|", text)
        line = text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1 if m else 1
        for col, cell in row.items():
            if fold(col) not in REGISTER_CELLS:
                continue
            for term in SPLIT.split(PAREN.sub(" ", cell)):
                term = term.strip(" .")
                if term and not SKIP_TERM.search(fold(term)) and words(term):
                    out.append((row["id"], col, term, line))
    return out


def matches(term: str, row: dict) -> bool:
    tw = words(term)
    lw = words(row.get("label", "")) | words(str(row.get("variable", "")).replace("_", " "))
    return bool(tw) and len(tw & lw) / len(tw) >= 0.6


def _map(project: Path, names: set) -> tuple[dict, list]:
    p, out, problems = project / MAP, {}, []
    if p.is_file():
        with open(p, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
            for i, r in enumerate(csv.DictReader(fh), 2):
                term, var = (r.get("term") or "").strip(), (r.get("variable") or "").strip()
                if var not in names:
                    problems.append(("block", f"{MAP}:{i}", f"maps '{term}' to '{var}', which is not in the data dictionary"))
                else:
                    out[fold(term)] = var
    return out, problems


def check(project: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    rows, codes = _read_dict(project), _crf_codes(project)
    if rows is None or codes is None:
        return [("block", f"{rel}:1", "not built: run crf.py <project> build")
                for rel, x in ((CRF, codes), (DICT, rows)) if x is None]
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    names = {r.get("variable", "") for r in rows}
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, 2):
        v, typ = r.get("variable", ""), (r.get("type") or "").strip()
        if not typ:
            found.append(("block", f"{DICT}:{i}", f"{v}: no type"))
        elif typ in CODED and not (r.get("values") or "").strip():
            found.append(("block", f"{DICT}:{i}", f"{v}: coded variable ({typ}) without a value list"))
    found += [("block", f"{CRF}:1", f"{v} is in the data dictionary but not in the CRF") for v in sorted(names - codes)]
    found += [("block", f"{DICT}:1", f"{v} is in the CRF but not in the data dictionary") for v in sorted(codes - names)]
    mapping, problems = _map(project, names)
    found += problems
    used: set = set()
    for aid, col, term, line in register_terms(project):
        hit = mapping.get(fold(term))
        hits = [hit] if hit else [r["variable"] for r in rows if matches(term, r)]
        if hits:
            used.update(hits)
            continue
        found.append(("block", f"{SAP}:{line}", f"register {aid} ({col}) uses '{term}', which matches no CRF variable: "
                                                f"the author adds it to variables.md (a protocol change), or map it in {MAP}"))
    src = {v["variable"]: v for v in _load_variables(project)} if (project / VARIABLES).is_file() else {}
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, 2):
        v = r.get("variable", "")
        s = src.get(v, {})
        if not s.get("objective") and v not in used:
            found.append(("warn", f"{DICT}:{i}", f"{v}: no objective and no analysis uses it; collect it only if "
                                                 "needed (data minimisation)"))
        if IDENT.search(fold(f"{r.get('label', '')} {v.replace('_', ' ')}")) and not s.get("need"):
            found.append(("warn", f"{DICT}:{i}", f"{v}: a direct identifier collected without a stated need (add a "
                                                 "Need / Lý do thu thập column in variables.md, or drop it)"))
    dossier = project / DOSSIER
    if not dossier.is_file():
        found.append(("warn", f"{DOSSIER}:1", "no dossier.md listing the ethics documents and their status"))
    else:
        text = dossier.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        found += [("warn", f"{DOSSIER}:1", f"dossier.md does not list {d}")
                  for d in ("crf.md", "data-dictionary.csv", "consent-form.md") if d not in text]
    return found


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["build", "check"])
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not project.is_dir():
        print(f"crf: project folder {a.project} not found", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "build":
            n, files = build(project)
            print(f"crf: {n} variables → {', '.join(files)}")
            return 0
        found = check(project)
    except Refused as e:
        print(f"crf: refused — {e}")
        return 1
    except (InputError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, csv.Error) as e:
        print(f"crf: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    for lv, loc, msg in found:
        print(f"E-CRF {lv} {loc}: {msg}")
    blocks = sum(1 for lv, _, _ in found if lv == "block")
    print(f"crf: {blocks} block, {len(found) - blocks} warn")
    return 1 if blocks else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Implementation notes: `analysis_plan` must stay importable without pandas (it is stdlib in P5; if an import error appears, stop and report). The `register {aid} ({col})` text uses the register's lower-cased header (`covariates`), which the test pins.

`lint_prose.py`: add the docstring line `python lint_prose.py --profile consent FILE...  plain-language warns CF01-CF03 for consent forms (exit 0)`, then after `lint_file`:

```python
# --- consent profile (plan D4) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CONSENT_WORDS = {"en": 25, "vi": 40}     # Vietnamese words are syllables
VI_LETTER = re.compile(r"[ăâđêôơưàảãáạằẳẵắặầẩẫấậèẻẽéẹềểễếệìỉĩíịòỏõóọồổỗốộờởỡớợùủũúụừửữứựỳỷỹýỵ]", re.I)
JARGON = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:randomi[sz](?:ed|ation)|placebo|double[- ]blind|single[- ]blind|cohort|biomarkers?|"
                    r"pharmacokinetics?|adverse events?|pseudonymi[sz](?:ed|ation)|de-?identified|ngẫu nhiên ho[áa]|"
                    r"giả dược|mù đôi|mù đơn|đoàn hệ|dấu ấn sinh học|dược động học|biến cố bất lợi|mã ho[áa])\b")
EXPLAIN = re.compile(r"\(|\bnghĩa là\b|\btức là\b|\bthat is\b|\bwhich means\b|\bmeaning\b|\bi\.e\.")
OBLIGATION = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:must|shall|will) be [a-z]+(?:ed|en)\b|\b(?:is|are) (?:required|obliged|expected) to\b|"
                        r"\bphải được\b|\bbắt buộc phải\b|\bđược yêu cầu\b")
CONSENT_SPLIT = re.compile(r"(?<=[.!?;])\s+")


def lint_consent(path: str) -> dict:
    """Plain-language warns for a consent form or information sheet: CF01 long sentence, CF02 unexplained jargon,
    CF03 passive obligation. Headings, tables, fences and HTML comments are skipped."""
    text = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig", errors="replace")
    text = re.sub(r"<!--.*?-->", lambda m: re.sub(r"[^\n]", " ", m.group(0)), text, flags=re.S)
    findings, sentences, fence = [], 0, False

    def add(code: str, n: int, msg: str) -> None:
        findings.append({"code": code, "level": "warn", "file": path, "line": n, "message": msg})

    for n, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
        if FENCE.match(line):
            fence = not fence
            continue
        s = line.strip()
        if fence or not s or s.startswith(("#", "|")):
            continue
        s = re.sub(r"^(?:[-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+", "", s)
        s = re.sub(r"\[\[[^\]]*\]\]|\{\{[^{}]*\}\}", "X", s)
        for sent in CONSENT_SPLIT.split(s):
            if not sent.strip():
                continue
            sentences += 1
            nw = len(re.findall(r"[^\W_]+", sent))
            limit = CONSENT_WORDS["vi" if VI_LETTER.search(sent) else "en"]
            if nw > limit:
                add("CF01", n, f"sentence of {nw} words (plain-language target ≤ {limit}); split it")
            for m in JARGON.finditer(sent):
                if not EXPLAIN.search(sent[m.end():m.end() + 25]):
                    add("CF02", n, f'"{m.group(0)}" is not explained in plain words right after it')
            for m in OBLIGATION.finditer(sent):
                add("CF03", n, f'"{m.group(0)}" is a passive obligation; say who does what ("you sign", '
                               '"the study team keeps")')
    return {"file": path, "sentences": sentences, "findings": findings}
```

In `main`: `ap.add_argument("--profile", choices=["academic", "consent"], default="academic")`; after the file-exists loop, `if args.profile == "consent" and args.slides:` print `lint_prose: --profile consent is for text files, not --slides` to stderr and return 2; build `results` with `lint_consent(f) if args.profile == "consent" else (lint_slides(f) if args.slides else lint_file(f, args.lang))`; in the text printer print `f"lint_prose: consent profile, {r['sentences']} sentences, {len(r['findings'])} warnings"` for the consent profile instead of the density line. Nothing else changes (`audit.py` keeps calling `lint_file(str(p), "auto")`).

`plugins/orchestra/references/ethics/consent-elements.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Consent form elements (paraphrased checklist)

Use with the skill `ethics-and-crf`. The elements below are the ones internationally recognised guidance (the
Declaration of Helsinki, the CIOMS international ethical guidelines, ICH-GCP) expects an information sheet and
consent form to cover. They are paraphrased here, never quoted, and give no article or clause numbers: a citation
to any of these documents, or to a national regulation, is added only from a tool lookup recorded in
`ledgers/references.json`. **The author's ethics committee template always wins**: when the author gives one,
follow its order, headings and wording rules and use this list only to spot gaps.

| # | Element | What the participant must be able to understand |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Purpose | why the study is done; that it is research, not routine care |
| 2 | Procedures | what happens to the participant, how often, what is collected (data, samples), what is experimental |
| 3 | Duration | how long participation lasts; how many contacts or visits |
| 4 | Risks and discomforts | foreseeable risks, including privacy risks in record-only studies |
| 5 | Benefits | to the participant (possibly none) and to others |
| 6 | Alternatives | other care, or not taking part, with no loss of usual care |
| 7 | Confidentiality and data handling | who sees the data, how it is pseudonymised, where and how long it is kept, whether it is shared |
| 8 | Voluntariness and withdrawal | taking part is free; leaving at any time without penalty; what happens to data already collected |
| 9 | Contacts | whom to ask about the study; whom to contact about the participant's rights (the committee) |
| 10 | Compensation and costs | costs, reimbursement, care for study-related harm, where relevant |
| 11 | Future use | storage or re-use of data or samples, if planned, as a separate choice |
| 12 | Signature block | participant (or legal representative), person obtaining consent, date; a witness when the participant cannot read |

Writing rules (warned by `lint_prose.py --profile consent`): plain words, short sentences, every technical term
explained in brackets the first time, active voice that says who does what ("you sign", "the study team keeps"),
the participant addressed directly.

Institution-specific facts are never filled by Claude: committee name and approval number, investigator names and
phone numbers, compensation amounts, insurance, storage periods set by the institution → `[[AUTHOR: …]]`
(`[[TÁC GIẢ: …]]` in Vietnamese).

Record-only (retrospective) studies: a committee may waive individual consent. Whether it does is the committee's
decision, recorded by the author in `decisions.md`; Claude never assumes a waiver.
```

`plugins/orchestra/references/ethics/dossier-template.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Ethics dossier (template)

`03_protocol/ethics/dossier.md` lists every document the ethics committee will receive and its status. Statuses:
`drafted` (written by Claude, the author reviews), `author to supply`, `author reviewed`, `approved` (with the
committee's reference, written by the author). Never mark a document approved; only the author does.

| Document | File | Status | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Protocol | `03_protocol/protocol.md` | locked (G2) | |
| Statistical analysis plan | `03_protocol/sap.md` | locked (G2) | |
| Case report form | `03_protocol/ethics/crf.md` | drafted | built by `crf.py` |
| Data dictionary | `03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv` | drafted | built by `crf.py` |
| Information sheet and consent form | `03_protocol/ethics/consent-form.md` | drafted | or the committee's waiver decision |
| Investigator CVs, GCP certificates | | author to supply | |
| Committee application form | | author to supply | the institution's own form |
| Data management or protection statement | | `[[AUTHOR: …]]` | if the committee asks for one |

Rules, regulations and committee requirements are never stated from memory: a requirement is written only when the
author or a tool lookup gives it, with its source.
```
