```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import init_project
import journal_check
from crolib import projectcfg

# Fictional journal and ISSNs (valid check digits, no real journal); shapes as the three APIs answer (plan D8).
NLM_SEARCH = json.dumps({"esearchresult": {"idlist": ["900000001"]}}).encode()
NLM_EMPTY = json.dumps({"esearchresult": {"idlist": []}}).encode()
NLM_XML = ('<NLMCatalogRecordSet><NLMCatalogRecord><NlmUniqueID>900000001</NlmUniqueID>'
           '<TitleMain><Title Sort="N">Journal of Fictional Medicine.</Title></TitleMain>'
           '<PublicationInfo><Publisher>Fictional Press Ltd</Publisher></PublicationInfo>'
           '<ISSN IssnType="Print">0000-0000</ISSN><ISSN IssnType="Electronic">0000-0019</ISSN>'
           '<IndexingSourceList><IndexingSourceName IndexingStatus="Currently-indexed">MEDLINE</IndexingSourceName>'
           '</IndexingSourceList></NLMCatalogRecord></NLMCatalogRecordSet>').encode()
DOAJ_HIT = json.dumps({"total": 1, "results": [{"bibjson": {
    "title": "Journal of Fictional Medicine", "pissn": "0000-0000", "eissn": "0000-0019",
    "publisher": {"name": "Fictional Press"}, "apc": {"has_apc": True, "max": [{"price": 1000, "currency": "USD"}]}},
    "admin": {"seal": False}}]}).encode()
DOAJ_NONE = json.dumps({"total": 0, "results": []}).encode()
CR_MSG = {"title": "Journal of Fictional Medicine", "publisher": "Fictional Press", "ISSN": ["0000-0000", "0000-0019"],
          "issn-type": [{"value": "0000-0000", "type": "print"}, {"value": "0000-0019", "type": "electronic"}]}
FOUND = [("esearch.fcgi", NLM_SEARCH), ("efetch.fcgi", NLM_XML), ("doaj.org", DOAJ_HIT),
         ("api.crossref.org/journals/0000-0000", json.dumps({"message": CR_MSG}).encode())]


def router(routes):
    calls = []

    def get(url):
        calls.append(url)
        for key, val in routes:
            if key in url:
                if isinstance(val, BaseException):
                    raise val
                return val
        raise urllib.error.HTTPError(url, 404, "Not Found", {}, None)
    get.calls = calls
    return get


class TestJournalCheck(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "Fictional study", "manuscript", "cohort", "en", "en")
        self._get, self._gap = journal_check.http_get, journal_check.MIN_GAP
        journal_check.MIN_GAP = 0

    def tearDown(self):
        journal_check.http_get, journal_check.MIN_GAP = self._get, self._gap
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def use(self, routes):
        journal_check.http_get = router(routes)
        return journal_check.http_get

    def cli(self, *argv):
        out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            code = journal_check.main([str(self.p), *argv])
        return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()

    def data(self):
        return json.loads((self.p / journal_check.JSON_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_issn_helpers(self):
        self.assertTrue(journal_check.issn_check_ok("0000-0000"))
        self.assertTrue(journal_check.issn_check_ok("0000-0019"))
        self.assertFalse(journal_check.issn_check_ok("0000-0011"))
        self.assertEqual(journal_check.norm_issn("00000019"), "0000-0019")
        self.assertIsNone(journal_check.norm_issn("Journal of Fictional Medicine"))

    def test_lookup_by_issn_records_sources_and_dates(self):
        self.use(FOUND)
        code, out, err = self.cli("lookup", "0000-0000")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        rec = self.data()["journals"][0]
        self.assertEqual(rec["key"], "0000-0000")
        self.assertRegex(rec["looked_up"], r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")
        self.assertEqual({t["source"] for t in rec["title"]}, {"NLM Catalog", "DOAJ", "Crossref"})
        self.assertEqual(rec["title"][0]["value"], "Journal of Fictional Medicine")
        self.assertIs(rec["medline"]["value"], True)
        self.assertIn("Currently", rec["medline"]["detail"])
        self.assertTrue(rec["doaj"]["listed"])
        self.assertEqual(rec["doaj"]["apc"], ["1000 USD"])
        self.assertEqual(rec["signals"], [])
        md = (self.p / journal_check.MD_REL).read_bytes()
        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", md)
        self.assertIn("| Journal of Fictional Medicine |", md.decode("utf-8"))

    def test_signals_are_listed_never_a_verdict(self):
        self.use([("esearch.fcgi", NLM_EMPTY), ("doaj.org", DOAJ_NONE)])
        code, out, _ = self.cli("lookup", "0000-0019", "--claim", "medline", "--claim", "scopus", "--open-access",
                                "--concern", "promises publication in two weeks")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        rec = self.data()["journals"][0]
        text = " | ".join(s["signal"] for s in rec["signals"])
        for s in ("not found in the NLM Catalog, DOAJ or Crossref", "claims MEDLINE indexing", "not checkable",
                  "not listed in DOAJ", "two weeks"):
            self.assertIn(s, text)
        self.assertTrue(all(s["source"] for s in rec["signals"]))
        for blob in (out, (self.p / journal_check.JSON_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                     (self.p / journal_check.MD_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            self.assertNotIn("predatory", blob.lower())

    def test_publisher_mismatch_and_check_digit(self):
        other = json.dumps({"message": dict(CR_MSG, publisher="Another House")}).encode()
        self.use(FOUND[:3] + [("api.crossref.org/journals/0000-0000", other)])
        self.cli("lookup", "0000-0000")
        self.assertIn("publisher differs", " ".join(s["signal"] for s in self.data()["journals"][0]["signals"]))
        self.use([("esearch.fcgi", NLM_EMPTY), ("doaj.org", DOAJ_NONE)])
        self.cli("lookup", "0000-0011")
        rec = [r for r in self.data()["journals"] if r["key"] == "0000-0011"][0]
        self.assertIn("invalid check digit", " ".join(s["signal"] for s in rec["signals"]))

    def test_title_lookup_resolves_issn_for_doaj(self):
        get = self.use([("esearch.fcgi", NLM_SEARCH), ("efetch.fcgi", NLM_XML), ("doaj.org", DOAJ_HIT),
                        ("journals?query=", json.dumps({"message": {"items": [CR_MSG]}}).encode())])
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("lookup", "Journal of Fictional Medicine")[0], 0)
        self.assertTrue(any("doaj.org" in u and "0000-0000" in u for u in get.calls), get.calls)
        self.assertEqual(self.data()["journals"][0]["key"], "0000-0000")

    def test_network_error_exits_2_and_writes_nothing(self):
        self.use([("esearch.fcgi", urllib.error.URLError("offline"))])
        code, _, err = self.cli("lookup", "0000-0000")
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn("offline", err)
        self.assertFalse((self.p / journal_check.JSON_REL).exists())

    def test_note_and_pick_write_only_target_keys(self):
        self.use(FOUND)
        self.cli("lookup", "0000-0000")
        before = (self.p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n").splitlines()
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("note", "0000-0000", "--words", "3000", "--abstract", "250", "--ref-style", "Vancouver",
                                  "--scope", "Clinical epidemiology", "--scope-url", "https://example.org/scope",
                                  "--source-url", "https://example.org/authors")[0], 0)
        self.assertIsNone(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.journal"))
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("pick", "0000-0000", "--by", "Example author")[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.journal"), "Journal of Fictional Medicine")
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.word_limit"), "250")
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.text_words"), "3000")
        after = (self.p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        changed = [a for a, b in zip(after, before) if a != b]
        self.assertEqual(len(after), len(before))
        self.assertTrue(all(re.match(r"^  (journal|word_limit|text_words): ", c) for c in changed), changed)
        rec = self.data()["journals"][0]
        self.assertEqual(rec["picked"]["by"], "Example author")
        self.assertEqual(rec["notes"]["word_limit"]["source"], "https://example.org/authors")
        self.assertIn("picked by Example author", (self.p / journal_check.MD_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_pick_unknown_key_is_refused(self):
        code, out, _ = self.cli("pick", "0000-0019", "--by", "Example author")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIsNone(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.journal"))

    def test_set_target_keeps_colons_and_quotes_readable(self):
        journal_check.set_target(self.p, {"journal": 'Journal: "Fictional" Medicine', "word_limit": 200})
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.journal"), "Journal: 'Fictional' Medicine")
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(self.p, "target.word_limit"), "200")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `plugins/orchestra/scripts/journal_check.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Journal facts from open registries for a submission shortlist: signals with sources, never verdicts.

  python journal_check.py <project> lookup <ISSN|title> [...] [--claim medline|scopus] [--open-access] [--concern TEXT]
  python journal_check.py <project> note <key> --source-url URL [--scope TEXT --scope-url URL] [--words N]
                                       [--abstract N] [--ref-style TEXT]
  python journal_check.py <project> table
  python journal_check.py <project> pick <key> --by NAME

lookup asks the NLM Catalog (E-utilities, db=nlmcatalog: MEDLINE indexing status, ISSNs, publisher), DOAJ (listing,
APC as listed, seal) and Crossref (title, publisher, ISSNs) through crolib/http.py and writes one record per journal
to 08_submission/journals.json (every field with its source and the lookup date) and the shortlist table
08_submission/journals.md. A title is first resolved to an ISSN (NLM Catalog, then Crossref); every source is then
asked for that ISSN. Signals: ISSN not found in any registry; an invalid ISSN check digit; a claimed MEDLINE indexing
the NLM Catalog does not confirm; a claimed Scopus indexing (not checkable with these open sources); an open-access
journal absent from DOAJ; publisher or title differing across sources; the author's own concerns. The tool never
judges a journal: it lists signals with their sources and the author decides.
note records what was read on the journal's own pages (scope, word and abstract limits, reference style) with the
page URL and date. pick, after the author has chosen, writes project.yaml target.journal, target.word_limit (abstract
limit) and target.text_words (main-text limit) and records who picked.
Exit codes: 0 = done; 1 = unknown journal key (note, pick); 2 = usage, missing project, network or API error
(nothing written). Standard library only.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import itertools
import json
import re
import sys
import time
import unicodedata
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import http  # noqa: E402

NCBI = "https://eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/entrez/eutils/"
DOAJ = "https://doaj.org/api/search/journals/"
CROSSREF = "https://api.crossref.org/journals"
TOOL = "clinical-research-orchestra"
JSON_REL = "08_submission/journals.json"
MD_REL = "08_submission/journals.md"
ISSN = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{4})-?(\d{3}[\dXx])\s*$")
NAME_STOP = {"the", "ltd", "limited", "inc", "llc", "co", "publishing", "publishers", "publisher", "press", "group",
             "company", "gmbh", "bv", "sa", "plc", "of", "and"}
MIN_GAP = 0.34          # NCBI: at most 3 requests per second without a key
_last = 0.0
COLS = ("| Journal | ISSN | MEDLINE (NLM Catalog) | Open access / APC (DOAJ) | Limits: words / abstract | "
        "Reference style | Scope fit | Signals |")


class NetworkError(Exception):
    """Exit 2."""


class Refused(Exception):
    """Exit 1."""


def http_get(url: str) -> bytes:
    return http.get(url)


def _get(url: str) -> bytes | None:
    try:
        return http_get(url)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        if e.code == 404:
            return None
        raise NetworkError(f"{url.split('?')[0]}: HTTP {e.code}") from e
    except OSError as e:            # URLError, timeouts, refused connections
        raise NetworkError(f"{url.split('?')[0]}: {getattr(e, 'reason', e)}") from e


def _ncbi(endpoint: str, **params) -> bytes | None:
    global _last
    wait = MIN_GAP - (time.monotonic() - _last)
    if wait > 0:
        time.sleep(wait)
    params.setdefault("tool", TOOL)
    try:
        return _get(NCBI + endpoint + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode(params))
    finally:
        _last = time.monotonic()


def fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", str(s).replace("đ", "d").replace("Đ", "D"))
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", "".join(c for c in s if not unicodedata.combining(c))).strip().casefold()


def norm_issn(s) -> str | None:
    m = ISSN.match(str(s or ""))
    return f"{m.group(1)}-{m.group(2).upper()}" if m else None


def issn_check_ok(issn: str) -> bool:
    d = issn.replace("-", "").upper()
    total = sum(int(c) * w for c, w in zip(d[:7], range(8, 1, -1)))
    check = (11 - total % 11) % 11
    return d[7] == ("X" if check == 10 else str(check))


def _tag(e) -> str:
    return e.tag.rsplit("}", 1)[-1]


def nlm(query: str, issn: str | None) -> dict | None:
    term = f"{issn}[issn]" if issn else f'"{query}"[Title]'
    raw = _ncbi("esearch.fcgi", db="nlmcatalog", term=term, retmode="json", retmax=5)
    ids = json.loads(raw).get("esearchresult", {}).get("idlist", []) if raw else []
    if not ids:
        return None
    xml = _ncbi("efetch.fcgi", db="nlmcatalog", id=ids[0], retmode="xml")
    if not xml:
        return None
    root = ET.fromstring(xml)
    els = list(root.iter())
    main = next((e for e in els if _tag(e) == "TitleMain"), None)
    title = next(((t.text or "").strip() for t in (main.iter() if main is not None else els)
                  if _tag(t) == "Title" and (t.text or "").strip()), "")
    publisher = next(((e.text or "").strip() for e in els if _tag(e) in ("Publisher", "Entity") and (e.text or "").strip()), "")
    statuses = [e.get("IndexingStatus", "") for e in els
                if _tag(e) == "IndexingSourceName" and (e.text or "").strip().upper() == "MEDLINE"]
    nlm_id = next(((e.text or "").strip() for e in els if _tag(e) == "NlmUniqueID"), ids[0])
    return {"title": title.rstrip(". "), "publisher": publisher,
            "issns": [(norm_issn(e.text), e.get("IssnType", "")) for e in els if _tag(e) == "ISSN" and norm_issn(e.text)],
            "medline": any("currently" in s.lower() for s in statuses),
            "medline_detail": ", ".join(s for s in statuses if s) or "not listed for MEDLINE",
            "url": f"https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nlmcatalog/{nlm_id}"}


def doaj(issn: str) -> dict | None:
    raw = _get(DOAJ + urllib.parse.quote(f"issn:{issn}") + "?pageSize=5")
    res = (json.loads(raw).get("results") or []) if raw else []
    if not res:
        return None
    r = res[0]
    bib = r.get("bibjson") or {}
    apc = bib.get("apc") or {}
    pub = bib.get("publisher")
    prices = [f"{m.get('price')} {m.get('currency', '')}".strip() for m in apc.get("max") or []
              if isinstance(m, dict) and m.get("price") is not None]
    return {"title": str(bib.get("title") or ""),
            "issns": [(i, t) for i, t in ((norm_issn(bib.get("pissn")), "print"), (norm_issn(bib.get("eissn")), "electronic")) if i],
            "publisher": str(pub.get("name", "") if isinstance(pub, dict) else pub or ""),
            "has_apc": apc.get("has_apc"), "apc": prices, "seal": bool((r.get("admin") or {}).get("seal")),
            "url": "https://doaj.org/toc/" + issn}


def crossref(query: str, issn: str | None) -> dict | None:
    if issn:
        raw = _get(f"{CROSSREF}/{issn}")
        items = [json.loads(raw)["message"]] if raw else []
    else:
        raw = _get(f"{CROSSREF}?query={urllib.parse.quote(query)}&rows=5")
        items = json.loads(raw)["message"].get("items", []) if raw else []
    if not items:
        return None
    m = items[0]
    issns = [(norm_issn(x.get("value")), x.get("type", "")) for x in m.get("issn-type") or [] if norm_issn(x.get("value"))]
    issns = issns or [(norm_issn(x), "") for x in m.get("ISSN") or [] if norm_issn(x)]
    return {"title": str(m.get("title") or ""), "publisher": str(m.get("publisher") or ""), "issns": issns,
            "url": f"{CROSSREF}/{issns[0][0]}" if issns else CROSSREF}


def _norm_name(s: str) -> str:
    return " ".join(w for w in re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]+", " ", fold(s)).split() if w not in NAME_STOP)


def _differ(values: list[str]) -> bool:
    vals = sorted({_norm_name(v) for v in values} - {""})
    return any(not (a in b or b in a) for a, b in itertools.combinations(vals, 2))


def _signals(query, issn, claims, open_access, concerns, sources: dict) -> list[dict]:
    n, d, c = sources["NLM Catalog"], sources["DOAJ"], sources["Crossref"]
    out: list[dict] = []

    def add(text: str, source: str) -> None:
        out.append({"signal": text, "source": source})
    if issn and not issn_check_ok(issn):
        add(f"ISSN {issn} has an invalid check digit", "ISSN check digit")
    if not any((n, d, c)):
        add(f"'{query}' not found in the NLM Catalog, DOAJ or Crossref", "NLM Catalog, DOAJ, Crossref")
    if "medline" in claims and not (n and n["medline"]):
        add("claims MEDLINE indexing; the NLM Catalog does not list it as currently indexed for MEDLINE", "NLM Catalog")
    if "scopus" in claims:
        add("claims Scopus indexing; not checkable with the open sources used here; check the Scopus source list",
            "author's information")
    if open_access and d is None:
        add("presents itself as open access but is not listed in DOAJ", "DOAJ")
    for field in ("publisher", "title"):
        vals = {name: s[field] for name, s in sources.items() if s and s[field]}
        if _differ(list(vals.values())):
            add(f"{field} differs across sources: " + "; ".join(f"{k} '{v}'" for k, v in vals.items()),
                ", ".join(vals))
    for text in concerns:
        add(text, "author")
    return out


def lookup(query: str, claims=(), open_access: bool = False, concerns=(), today: str | None = None) -> dict:
    today = today or dt.date.today().isoformat()
    issn = norm_issn(query)
    n, c = nlm(query, issn), crossref(query, issn)
    if not issn:
        issn = next((i for s in (n, c) if s for i, _ in s["issns"]), None)
        if issn and n is None:
            n = nlm(query, issn)
        if issn and c is None:
            c = crossref(query, issn)
    d = doaj(issn) if issn else None
    sources = {"NLM Catalog": n, "DOAJ": d, "Crossref": c}
    issns: list[dict] = []
    for name, s in sources.items():
        for i, typ in (s["issns"] if s else []):
            if not any(x["value"] == i and x["source"] == name for x in issns):
                issns.append({"value": i, "type": typ, "source": name})
    return {
        "key": issn or fold(query), "query": query, "looked_up": today,
        "title": [{"value": s["title"], "source": name} for name, s in sources.items() if s and s["title"]],
        "issn": issns,
        "publisher": [{"value": s["publisher"], "source": name} for name, s in sources.items() if s and s["publisher"]],
        "medline": {"value": n["medline"] if n else None,
                    "detail": n["medline_detail"] if n else "not found in the NLM Catalog",
                    "source": "NLM Catalog", "url": n["url"] if n else ""},
        "doaj": {"listed": d is not None, "has_apc": d["has_apc"] if d else None, "apc": d["apc"] if d else [],
                 "seal": d["seal"] if d else False, "source": "DOAJ", "url": d["url"] if d else ""},
        "crossref": {"listed": c is not None, "source": "Crossref", "url": c["url"] if c else ""},
        "claims": sorted(set(claims)), "open_access_claimed": bool(open_access),
        "signals": _signals(query, issn, set(claims), open_access, list(concerns), sources),
        "notes": {}, "picked": None,
    }


def load(project: Path) -> dict:
    p = Path(project) / JSON_REL
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.is_file() else {"journals": []}


def _write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(text)


def _cell(s) -> str:
    return str(s).replace("|", "/").replace("\n", " ")


def table(data: dict) -> str:
    out = ["# Journal shortlist", "",
           "Facts come from tool lookups (source and date in `journals.json`); scope, limits and reference style from "
           "the journal's own pages (URL given). Signals are not verdicts: the author decides "
           "(Think. Check. Submit.: https://thinkchecksubmit.org/).", "", COLS, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for r in data["journals"]:
        notes = r.get("notes") or {}

        def note(k: str) -> str:
            return str(notes[k]["value"]) if k in notes else "not noted"
        med = r["medline"]
        med_txt = {True: "yes", False: "no", None: "unknown"}[med["value"]] + f" ({med['detail']}; {r['looked_up']})"
        dj = r["doaj"]
        if dj["listed"]:
            apc = ", ".join(dj["apc"]) or ("none listed" if dj["has_apc"] is False else "not stated")
            oa = f"in DOAJ; APC {apc}" + ("; DOAJ seal" if dj["seal"] else "")
        else:
            oa = "not in DOAJ"
        scope = notes.get("scope")
        title = r["title"][0]["value"] if r["title"] else r["query"]
        if r.get("picked"):
            title += f" — picked by {r['picked']['by']}"
        cells = (title, ", ".join(dict.fromkeys(i["value"] for i in r["issn"])) or "-", med_txt, oa,
                 f"{note('word_limit')} / {note('abstract_limit')}", note("ref_style"),
                 f"{scope['value']} ({scope['source']})" if scope else "not noted",
                 "; ".join(f"{s['signal']} [{s['source']}]" for s in r["signals"]) or "none")
        out.append("| " + " | ".join(_cell(c) for c in cells) + " |")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def save(project: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    _write(Path(project) / JSON_REL, json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    _write(Path(project) / MD_REL, table(data))


def find(data: dict, key: str) -> dict | None:
    k = norm_issn(key) or fold(key)
    for r in data["journals"]:
        if r["key"] == k or any(i["value"] == k for i in r["issn"]) or any(fold(t["value"]) == k for t in r["title"]):
            return r
    return None


def note(project: Path, key: str, source_url: str, scope=None, scope_url=None, words=None, abstract=None,
         ref_style=None, today: str | None = None) -> dict:
    data = load(project)
    rec = find(data, key)
    if rec is None:
        raise Refused(f"no journal '{key}' in {JSON_REL}: run lookup first")
    today = today or dt.date.today().isoformat()
    for field, val, url in (("scope", scope, scope_url or source_url), ("word_limit", words, source_url),
                            ("abstract_limit", abstract, source_url), ("ref_style", ref_style, source_url)):
        if val is not None:
            rec.setdefault("notes", {})[field] = {"value": val, "source": url, "date": today}
    save(project, data)
    return rec


def set_target(project: Path, values: dict) -> None:
    """Replace or add `  key: value` lines under the top-level `target:` block of project.yaml (LF written)."""
    path = Path(project) / "project.yaml"
    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n")
    if "target:" not in lines:
        at = len(lines) - 1 if lines and lines[-1] == "" else len(lines)
        lines.insert(at, "target:")
    start = lines.index("target:")
    end = start + 1
    while end < len(lines) and lines[end].startswith(" "):
        end += 1
    for key, val in values.items():
        if val is None:
            text = "null"
        elif isinstance(val, int) and not isinstance(val, bool):
            text = str(val)
        else:       # projectcfg reads no escapes: keep the value inside plain double quotes
            text = '"' + str(val).replace("\\", "/").replace('"', "'") + '"'
        new = f"  {key}: {text}"
        idx = next((i for i in range(start + 1, end) if re.match(rf"^  {re.escape(key)}:", lines[i])), None)
        if idx is None:
            lines.insert(start + 1, new)
            end += 1
        else:
            lines[idx] = new
    _write(path, "\n".join(lines))


def pick(project: Path, key: str, by: str, today: str | None = None) -> dict:
    data = load(project)
    rec = find(data, key)
    if rec is None:
        raise Refused(f"no journal '{key}' in {JSON_REL}: run lookup first")
    notes = rec.get("notes") or {}
    values: dict = {"journal": rec["title"][0]["value"] if rec["title"] else rec["query"]}
    if "abstract_limit" in notes:
        values["word_limit"] = int(notes["abstract_limit"]["value"])
    if "word_limit" in notes:
        values["text_words"] = int(notes["word_limit"]["value"])
    set_target(project, values)
    for r in data["journals"]:
        r["picked"] = None
    rec["picked"] = {"by": by, "at": today or dt.date.today().isoformat()}
    save(project, data)
    return values


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    lk = sub.add_parser("lookup")
    lk.add_argument("queries", nargs="+")
    lk.add_argument("--claim", action="append", choices=["medline", "scopus"], default=[])
    lk.add_argument("--open-access", action="store_true")
    lk.add_argument("--concern", action="append", default=[])
    nt = sub.add_parser("note")
    nt.add_argument("key")
    nt.add_argument("--source-url", required=True)
    nt.add_argument("--scope")
    nt.add_argument("--scope-url")
    nt.add_argument("--words", type=int)
    nt.add_argument("--abstract", type=int)
    nt.add_argument("--ref-style")
    sub.add_parser("table")
    pk = sub.add_parser("pick")
    pk.add_argument("key")
    pk.add_argument("--by", required=True)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not project.is_dir():
        print(f"journal_check: project folder {a.project} not found", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "lookup":
            recs = [lookup(q, a.claim, a.open_access, a.concern) for q in a.queries]   # all lookups before any write
            data = load(project)
            for rec in recs:
                old = find(data, rec["key"])
                if old is not None:
                    rec["notes"], rec["picked"] = old.get("notes") or {}, old.get("picked")
                    data["journals"][data["journals"].index(old)] = rec
                else:
                    data["journals"].append(rec)
                title = rec["title"][0]["value"] if rec["title"] else rec["query"]
                print(f"{rec['key']}: {title}; {len(rec['signals'])} signal(s)")
                for s in rec["signals"]:
                    print(f"  signal [{s['source']}]: {s['signal']}")
            save(project, data)
        elif a.cmd == "note":
            note(project, a.key, a.source_url, a.scope, a.scope_url, a.words, a.abstract, a.ref_style)
            print(f"journal_check: noted {a.key}")
        elif a.cmd == "table":
            data = load(project)
            save(project, data)
            print(f"journal_check: {MD_REL} ({len(data['journals'])} journals)")
        else:
            values = pick(project, a.key, a.by)
            print("journal_check: project.yaml target " + ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in values.items()))
    except Refused as e:
        print(f"journal_check: {e}")
        return 1
    except (NetworkError, ValueError, KeyError, ET.ParseError, OSError) as e:
        print(f"journal_check: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Implementation notes: the `set_target` loop stops at the first non-indented, non-empty line after `target:`; the template's `target:` block is followed by `evidence_sources:`. If the controller's project has no `target:` block, the block is appended. `ValueError` covers JSON errors (`json.JSONDecodeError`).

`plugins/orchestra/references/submission/journal-selection.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Journal selection: facts, signals and the author's choice

Used by the skill `journal-selection`. Nothing here is a fact about any journal: facts come from
`journal_check.py` lookups (NLM Catalog, DOAJ, Crossref) and from the journal's own pages, each recorded with
its URL and date in `08_submission/journals.json`.

## Building the shortlist
1. Candidates: journals that published the studies the manuscript cites (from `ledgers/references.json`), the
   author's suggestions, and journals named in an invitation the author received. Never a journal "known" from
   memory without a lookup.
2. `journal_check.py . lookup <ISSN or title> …` for each. Add `--claim medline` or `--claim scopus` when the
   journal's site or an invitation claims that indexing, `--open-access` when it presents itself as open access,
   and `--concern "<text>"` for each concern the author states.
3. Read each journal's own aims-and-scope and instructions-for-authors pages (WebFetch). Record what was read
   with `journal_check.py . note <key> --source-url <URL> [--scope "<one line>" --scope-url <URL>] [--words N]
   [--abstract N] [--ref-style "<style>"]`. A limit not found on the page is not noted (never guessed).
4. `journal_check.py . table` rewrites `08_submission/journals.md`. The "Scope fit" column is one sentence by
   Claude, tied to the scope page it read.

## Signals, not verdicts
A signal is a fact with a source that deserves the author's attention: an ISSN no registry knows, an invalid
check digit, a claimed MEDLINE indexing the NLM Catalog does not confirm, a Scopus claim these open sources cannot
check, an open-access journal missing from DOAJ, publisher or title disagreeing across sources, or the author's
own concern. Report each with its source. Never call a journal predatory, fake or a scam, in any language; point
the author to the Think. Check. Submit. checklist (https://thinkchecksubmit.org/) and let them decide.

## Picking
Only the author picks. After the author names the journal in the conversation, run
`journal_check.py . pick <key> --by "<author name>"`: it writes `target.journal`, `target.word_limit` (abstract)
and `target.text_words` (main text) in `project.yaml`. The reference style is shown in `journals.md`; changing
`citation_style` is a separate decision the author makes. Never pick for the author, even when asked to "just
choose": present the table and ask.
```

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import gate
import init_project
import response
from crolib import drafts

RES = {"id": "R-A1-001", "analysis_id": "A1", "status": "prespecified", "label": "adjusted OR", "value": 1.42,
       "ci": [1.05, 1.91], "n": 749, "kind": "or", "source_file": "05_analysis/output/log.txt", "locator": "line 1",
       "raw": "R-A1-001 value=1.42"}
DRAFTS = {
    "02-introduction.md": "# Introduction\n\nReadmission after a diabetes admission is common (fictional).\n",
    "03-methods.md": "# Methods\n\n## Participants\n\nAdults admitted with type 2 diabetes were included.\n",
    "04-results.md": "# Results\n\nThe adjusted OR was {{R-A1-001.full}}.\n",
    "05-discussion.md": "# Discussion\n\nHigher HbA1c was associated with readmission.\n",
}
COMMENTS = """Editor
1. Please shorten the abstract.

Reviewer 1
1. The exclusion criteria are unclear.
2. Please add a subgroup analysis by sex.

Reviewer #2
The discussion overstates the findings.

Please discuss residual confounding.
"""
CLEAN = """# Response to reviewers

## E1 (editor)
> Please shorten the abstract.

No change because the abstract already meets the journal's limit.

## R1.1
> The exclusion criteria are unclear.

We have clarified the exclusion criteria.
[[CHANGE: 06_writing/manuscript/03-methods.md | "Patients transferred from another hospital were excluded."]]

## R1.2
No change because a subgroup analysis by sex was not planned.

## R2.1
No change because the discussion already reports the adjusted OR of {{R-A1-001}} as an association.

## R2.2
We have added residual confounding to the limitations.
[[CHANGE: 06_writing/manuscript/05-discussion.md | "Residual confounding may remain."]]
"""


def open_g4(p: Path, d: str = "manuscript") -> None:
    files = {f.relative_to(p).as_posix(): gate.sha256(f) for f in drafts.tracked_files(p, d)}
    (p / "reviews" / "audit.json").write_text(json.dumps({"deliverable": d, "deliverables": [d], "at": "t",
                                                          "blocks": 0, "warns": 0, "files": files}), encoding="utf-8")
    gate.open_gate(p, "G4", by="Example author")


class TestResponse(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        p = self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "Fictional cohort", "manuscript", "cohort", "en", "en")
        (p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text(json.dumps({"results": [RES]}), encoding="utf-8")
        d = p / "06_writing" / "manuscript"
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for name, text in DRAFTS.items():
            (d / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
        self.comments = p / "00_inputs" / "reviews.txt"
        self.comments.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.comments.write_text(COMMENTS, encoding="utf-8")
        open_g4(p)

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def cli(self, *argv):
        out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            code = response.main([str(self.p), *argv])
        return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()

    def respond(self, text):
        (self.p / response.RESPONSE).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

    def edit(self, name, extra):
        f = self.p / "06_writing" / "manuscript" / name
        f.write_text(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + extra, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

    def made_changes(self):
        self.edit("03-methods.md", "Patients transferred from another hospital were excluded.\n")
        self.edit("05-discussion.md", "\n## Limitations\n\nResidual confounding may remain.\n")

    def blocks(self):
        return [f"{c} {loc}: {m}" for c, lv, loc, m in response.check(self.p) if lv == "block"]

    def test_split_ids_and_text(self):
        items = response.split_comments(COMMENTS)
        self.assertEqual([(i["id"], i["who"]) for i in items],
                         [("E1", "Editor"), ("R1.1", "Reviewer 1"), ("R1.2", "Reviewer 1"), ("R2.1", "Reviewer 2"),
                          ("R2.2", "Reviewer 2")])
        self.assertEqual(items[2]["text"], "Please add a subgroup analysis by sex.")
        self.assertEqual([i["id"] for i in response.split_comments("Reviewer 1:\n- a\n- b\n\nReferee 2\n(1) c\n")],
                         ["R1.1", "R1.2", "R2.1"])
        self.assertEqual(len(response.split_comments("<!-- Fictional: invented for a test. -->\n" + COMMENTS)), 5)

    def test_init_snapshots_and_refuses_twice(self):
        code, out, err = self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        data = json.loads((self.p / response.COMMENTS).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual((data["deliverable"], data["approved"], len(data["items"])), ("manuscript", True, 5))
        self.assertEqual(data["source"], "00_inputs/reviews.txt")
        self.assertEqual(set(data["submitted"]), {f"06_writing/manuscript/{n}" for n in DRAFTS})
        for rel, sha in data["submitted"].items():
            self.assertEqual(gate.sha256(self.p / response.SUBMITTED / rel), sha)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("init", str(self.comments))[0], 1)

    def test_init_needs_the_approved_version(self):
        self.edit("04-results.md", "Edited after approval.\n")
        code, out, _ = self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("--unapproved", out)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("init", str(self.comments), "--unapproved")[0], 0)
        self.assertIs(json.loads((self.p / response.COMMENTS).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["approved"], False)
        self.assertEqual(response.main([str(self.p), "init"]), 2)

    def test_clean_response_passes_only_after_the_edits(self):
        self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.respond(CLEAN)
        before = self.blocks()
        self.assertEqual(len(before), 2, before)
        self.assertTrue(all("is not in the current" in b for b in before), before)
        self.made_changes()
        self.assertEqual(response.check(self.p), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("check")[0], 0)

    def test_claim_without_locator_and_unchanged_quote_block(self):
        self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.made_changes()
        self.respond(CLEAN.replace(
            'We have clarified the exclusion criteria.\n[[CHANGE: 06_writing/manuscript/03-methods.md | '
            '"Patients transferred from another hospital were excluded."]]',
            "We have revised the Methods to clarify the exclusion criteria.").replace(
            "No change because a subgroup analysis by sex was not planned.",
            'We have clarified the population.\n[[CHANGE: 06_writing/manuscript/03-methods.md | '
            '"Adults admitted with type 2 diabetes were included."]]'))
        b = " | ".join(self.blocks())
        self.assertIn("R1.1: the response says the manuscript was changed but gives no [[CHANGE", b)
        self.assertIn("R1.2: \"Adults admitted with type 2 diabetes were included.\" was already in the submitted", b)

    def test_numbers_masks_new_analysis_unknown_and_missing(self):
        self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.made_changes()
        self.respond(CLEAN.replace(
            "No change because a subgroup analysis by sex was not planned.",
            "NEW-ANALYSIS: subgroup analysis by sex, requested by Reviewer 1").replace(
            "No change because the discussion already reports the adjusted OR of {{R-A1-001}} as an association.",
            "The odds ratio was 1.42 (see lines 120–125 and our reply to R1.2), as {{R-A1-001}} shows; "
            "the CI was 2.5 wide.").split("## R2.2")[0] + "## R9.1\nNo change because this is a test.\n")
        found = response.check(self.p)
        b = " | ".join(f"{c} {m}" for c, lv, _, m in found if lv == "block")
        self.assertIn("E-NUM R2.1: 2.5 is not in the results ledger", b)
        for tok in ("1.42 is", "120 is", "125 is", "1.2 is"):
            self.assertNotIn(tok, b)
        self.assertIn("E-RESP R1.2: NEW-ANALYSIS awaits the author's decision", b)
        self.assertIn("R9.1 is not a comment", b)
        self.assertIn("R2.2 has no response", b)
        warns = [m for c, lv, _, m in found if lv == "warn"]
        self.assertTrue(any(m.startswith("R2.1: no change and no") for m in warns), warns)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("check")[0], 1)

    def test_check_without_init_is_an_input_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(response.InputError):
            response.check(self.p)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("check")[0], 2)

    def test_build_needs_a_clean_check_and_g4_after_the_revision(self):
        self.cli("init", str(self.comments))
        self.respond(CLEAN)
        code, out, _ = self.cli("build")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("is not in the current", out)
        self.made_changes()
        code, out, _ = self.cli("build")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)            # the drafts changed: G4 is closed until a fresh audit
        self.assertIn("G4", out)
        open_g4(self.p)
        code, out, err = self.cli("build")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(self.p / response.LETTER) as z:
            xml = z.read("word/document.xml").decode("utf-8")
        for s in ("Response to the editor and reviewers", "R1.1", "Participants", "Limitations", "1.42",
                  "Patients transferred from another hospital were excluded."):
            self.assertIn(s, xml)
        self.assertNotIn("[[CHANGE", xml)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```python
class TestP6Gate(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_open_wording_names_the_lock(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            p = project(t)
            (p / "03_protocol" / "sap.md").write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
            gate.open_gate(p, "G1", by="Dr A")
            gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by="Dr A", files=["03_protocol/sap.md", "project.yaml"])
            for g, start in (("G1", "G1: open — approved by Dr A on "),
                             ("G2", "G2: open (locked, 2 files unchanged) — approved by Dr A on ")):
                out = io.StringIO()
                with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):

                self.assertTrue(out.getvalue().startswith(start), out.getvalue())

    def test_final_names_and_deliverables(self):

            self.assertIsNone(gate.final_deliverable(p, "ch3-results.docx"))

            (p / "09_revision").mkdir()
```

`plugins/orchestra/scripts/response.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reviewer response: split the comments, snapshot the submitted drafts, verify every claimed change, build the letter.

  python response.py <project> init <comments-file> [--deliverable manuscript|case-report|review] [--unapproved]
  python response.py <project> check
  python response.py <project> build

init splits pasted editor/reviewer comments into items E1, E2 …, R1.1, R1.2 …, R2.1 … (an "Editor" line starts the
editor's section, "Reviewer N"/"Referee N"/"Phản biện N" reviewer N's; numbered or bulleted lines start items,
otherwise blank lines do) into 09_revision/comments.json, and copies the deliverable's drafts (and order.txt) into
09_revision/submitted/<same paths> with their sha256. It refuses when comments.json exists, and when G4 is not open
for the deliverable unless --unapproved (the snapshot must be the submitted version).
09_revision/response.md has one "## <id>" section per item: lines starting ">" quote the comment; a change is
[[CHANGE: <project-relative file> | "<exact new text>"]].
check (E-RESP; numbers E-NUM) blocks: an item without a response; an id not in comments.json; a CHANGE file not in
the project; a CHANGE quote not in the current draft, or already in the submitted snapshot; a response that says
the manuscript was changed with no CHANGE locator; a NEW-ANALYSIS: line (the author decides; never sent); a number
not in the results ledger ({{R-…}} placeholders are rendered; item ids and page/line references are ignored).
Warns: no CHANGE and no "no change because …".
build writes out/response-letter.docx (comments, responses, change locations with section names) only when
gate.final_problems(project, "response-letter.docx") is empty: G4 open for the deliverable after a fresh audit of
the revised drafts and no check block.
Exit codes: 0 = done / no block; 1 = refused or a block; 2 = usage, missing project, comments.json or file, I/O error.
Standard library only.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import json
import re
import shutil
import sys
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import drafts, reviews  # noqa: E402
import build_docx  # noqa: E402
import check_numbers  # noqa: E402
import gate  # noqa: E402
import render_results  # noqa: E402

COMMENTS = "09_revision/comments.json"
RESPONSE = "09_revision/response.md"
SUBMITTED = "09_revision/submitted"
LETTER = "out/response-letter.docx"
PUBLISHING = ("manuscript", "case-report", "review")
EDITOR = re.compile(r"(?i)^\s*(?:#+\s*)?(?:\*\*)?\s*(?:(?:associate|academic|handling|section|deputy)\s+)?editor(?:'s)?"
                    r"\s*(?:comments?)?\s*[:.]?\s*(?:\*\*)?\s*:?\s*$"
                    r"|^\s*(?:#+\s*)?(?:\*\*)?\s*(?:(?:ý kiến|nhận xét)\s+(?:của\s+)?)?(?:ban\s+)?biên tập(?:\s+viên)?"
                    r"\s*[:.]?\s*(?:\*\*)?\s*$")
REVIEWER = re.compile(r"(?i)^\s*(?:#+\s*)?(?:\*\*)?\s*(?:reviewer|referee|người phản biện|phản biện)\s*#?\s*(\d+)"
                      r"\s*(?:'s)?\s*(?:comments?|nhận xét)?\s*[:.)]?\s*(?:\*\*)?\s*:?\s*$")
ITEM = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:\(?\d{1,3}[.)]|[-*•]|comment\s+\d+\s*[:.])\s+", re.IGNORECASE)
SECTION = re.compile(r"^##\s+(E\d+|R\d+\.\d+)\b")
CHANGE = re.compile(r'\[\[CHANGE:\s*([^|\]]+?)\s*\|\s*"([^"]+)"\s*\]\]')
NO_CHANGE = re.compile(r"(?i)\bno change\b|\bnot changed\b|\bunchanged\b|không (?:sửa|thay đổi|chỉnh sửa)")
CLAIM = re.compile(r"(?i)\bwe (?:have )?(?:now )?(?:revised|changed|added|updated|rewritten|rewrote|clarified|expanded|"
                   r"removed|deleted|moved|corrected|amended|modified|included|shortened)\b|"
                   r"\b(?:has|have) been (?:revised|changed|added|updated|rewritten|clarified|expanded|removed|deleted|"
                   r"moved|corrected|amended|modified|shortened)\b|"
                   r"đã (?:sửa|thêm|bổ sung|chỉnh|viết lại|xoá|xóa|cập nhật|làm rõ|thay đổi|điều chỉnh|rút gọn)")
MASK = re.compile(r"\b(?:E\d+|R\d+\.\d+)\b|(?i:\b(?:pages?|p\.|lines?|l\.|dòng|trang)\s*\d+(?:\s*[–-]\s*\d+)?)")
LETTER_TEXT = {"en": {"title": "Response to the editor and reviewers", "changed": "Changed in"},
               "vi": {"title": "Thư trả lời biên tập viên và người phản biện", "changed": "Đã sửa tại"}}


class InputError(Exception):
    """Exit 2."""


class Refused(Exception):
    """Exit 1."""


def _write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(text)


def _chunks(lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    numbered = any(ITEM.match(ln) for ln in lines)
    out: list[str] = []
    cur: list[str] = []

    def flush() -> None:
        text = "\n".join(cur).strip()
        if text:
            out.append(text)
        cur.clear()
    for ln in lines:
        if numbered:
            if ITEM.match(ln):
                flush()
                cur.append(ITEM.sub("", ln, count=1))
            else:
                cur.append(ln)
        elif ln.strip():
            cur.append(ln)
        else:
            flush()
    flush()
    return out


def split_comments(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    sections: list[list] = [["E", "Editor", []]]
    text = re.sub(r"<!--.*?-->", "", text, flags=re.S)      # labels such as "fictional" are not comments
    for line in text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n"):
        m = REVIEWER.match(line)
        if m:
            sections.append([f"R{int(m.group(1))}", f"Reviewer {int(m.group(1))}", []])
        elif EDITOR.match(line):
            sections.append(["E", "Editor", []])
        else:
            sections[-1][2].append(line)
    items, count = [], {}
    for key, who, lines in sections:
        for text in _chunks(lines):
            count[key] = count.get(key, 0) + 1
            items.append({"id": f"E{count[key]}" if key == "E" else f"{key}.{count[key]}", "who": who, "text": text})
    return items


def init(project: Path, comments_file: str, deliverable: str = "manuscript", unapproved: bool = False) -> dict:
    project = Path(project)
    if deliverable not in PUBLISHING:
        raise InputError(f"deliverable must be one of {', '.join(PUBLISHING)}")
    if (project / COMMENTS).exists():
        raise Refused(f"{COMMENTS} exists: the revision is already initialised (the snapshot must stay the "
                      "submitted version)")
    if not drafts.draft_files(project, deliverable):
        raise InputError(f"no drafts in 06_writing/{deliverable}/")
    ok, why = gate.check_gate(project, "G4", deliverable)
    if not ok and not unapproved:
        raise Refused("the drafts are not the G4-approved version (" + "; ".join(why) + "); if the submitted "
                      "version was exported another way and these drafts are exactly it, rerun with --unapproved")
    src = Path(comments_file)
    if not src.is_file():
        raise InputError(f"{comments_file} not found")
    raw = src.read_bytes()
    items = split_comments(raw.decode("utf-8-sig"))
    if not items:
        raise InputError(f"no comments found in {comments_file}")
    root = project.resolve()
    submitted = {}
    for f in drafts.tracked_files(project, deliverable):
        rel = f.resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix()
        dst = project / SUBMITTED / rel
        dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(f, dst)
        submitted[rel] = gate.sha256(dst)
    try:
        source = src.resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix()
    except ValueError:
        source = src.name
    data = {"deliverable": deliverable, "source": source, "source_sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
            "created": dt.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"), "approved": ok, "items": items,
            "submitted": submitted}
    _write(project / COMMENTS, json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    return data


def load_comments(project: Path) -> dict:
    p = Path(project) / COMMENTS
    if not p.is_file():
        raise InputError(f"{COMMENTS} not found: run response.py <project> init <comments-file>")
    try:
        data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    except ValueError as e:
        raise InputError(f"{COMMENTS} is unreadable: {e}") from e
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not isinstance(data.get("items"), list):
        raise InputError(f"{COMMENTS} has no 'items' list")
    return data


def parse_response(text: str) -> dict:
    out: dict = {}
    cur = None
    for n, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
        m = SECTION.match(line)
        if m:
            cur = out.setdefault(m.group(1), {"line": n, "lines": []})
        elif re.match(r"^#{1,2}\s", line):
            cur = None
        elif cur is not None:
            cur["lines"].append((n, line))
    return out


def locate(text: str, quote: str):
    words = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", quote).split()
    if not words:
        return None
    return re.search(r"\s+".join(map(re.escape, words)), unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text))


def section_of(text: str, start: int, fallback: str) -> str:
    before = re.findall(r"(?m)^#{1,6}\s+(.+?)\s*$", text[:start])
    if before:
        return before[-1]
    first = re.search(r"(?m)^#{1,6}\s+(.+?)\s*$", text)
    return first.group(1) if first else fallback


def _ledger(project: Path) -> list[dict]:
    p = Path(project) / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json"
    if not p.is_file():
        return []
    data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    return [r for r in (data.get("results") or []) if isinstance(r, dict)] if isinstance(data, dict) else []


def _short(q: str) -> str:
    return q if len(q) <= 60 else q[:57] + "…"


def deliverable_of(data: dict) -> str:
    return data.get("deliverable") if data.get("deliverable") in PUBLISHING else "manuscript"


def check(project: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    data = load_comments(project)
    rp = project / RESPONSE
    if not rp.is_file():
        return [("E-RESP", "block", f"{RESPONSE}:1", "not written: one '## <id>' section per comment")]
    sections = parse_response(rp.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    ids = [i.get("id") for i in data["items"]]
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []

    def add(code: str, level: str, n: int, msg: str) -> None:
        found.append((code, level, f"{RESPONSE}:{n}", msg))
    for sid, sec in sections.items():
        if sid not in ids:
            add("E-RESP", "block", sec["line"], f"{sid} is not a comment in {COMMENTS}")
    ledger = _ledger(project)
    by_id = {r["id"]: r for r in ledger if isinstance(r.get("id"), str)}
    lang = drafts.lang_of(project, deliverable_of(data))
    root = project.resolve()
    for iid in ids:
        sec = sections.get(iid)
        body = [(n, ln) for n, ln in (sec["lines"] if sec else []) if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith(">")]
        if not body:
            add("E-RESP", "block", sec["line"] if sec else 1, f"{iid} has no response")
            continue
        locators = [(n, m.group(1).strip().replace("\\", "/"), m.group(2)) for n, ln in body for m in CHANGE.finditer(ln)]
        for n, rel, quote in locators:
            path = project / rel
            if Path(rel).is_absolute() or not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root) or not path.is_file():
                add("E-RESP", "block", n, f"{iid}: CHANGE file {rel} is not a file in the project")
                continue
            if locate(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"), quote) is None:
                add("E-RESP", "block", n, f'{iid}: "{_short(quote)}" is not in the current {rel}: the claimed change '
                                          "is not in the draft")
                continue
            sub = project / SUBMITTED / rel
            if sub.is_file() and locate(sub.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"), quote):
                add("E-RESP", "block", n, f'{iid}: "{_short(quote)}" was already in the submitted {rel}: the change '
                                          "was not made")
        prose = CHANGE.sub(" ", "\n".join(ln for _, ln in body))
        for n, ln in body:
            m = reviews.REQUEST.match(ln)
            if m and not reviews.is_none(m.group(1)):
                add("E-RESP", "block", n, f"{iid}: NEW-ANALYSIS awaits the author's decision (if accepted: a POST-HOC "
                                          "line in decisions.md and the statistical-analysis route; never run here); "
                                          "the letter cannot go out with it")
        if not locators and CLAIM.search(prose):
            add("E-RESP", "block", body[0][0], f'{iid}: the response says the manuscript was changed but gives no '
                                               '[[CHANGE: <file> | "<new text>"]] locator')
        elif not locators and not NO_CHANGE.search(prose):
            add("E-RESP", "warn", body[0][0], f"{iid}: no change and no 'no change because …': say what changed or why "
                                              "nothing did")
        for n, ln in body:
            if reviews.REQUEST.match(ln):
                continue
            rendered, probs = render_results.render_text(MASK.sub(" ", CHANGE.sub(" ", ln)), by_id, lang)
            for p in probs:
                add("E-NUM", "block", n, f"{iid}: {p.partition(': ')[2] or p}")
            for _, tok in check_numbers.find_unmatched(rendered, ledger, lang):
                add("E-NUM", "block", n, f"{iid}: {tok} is not in the results ledger (use a {{{{R-…}}}} placeholder)")
    return found


def letter_markdown(project: Path, data: dict, lang: str) -> str:
    project = Path(project)
    t = LETTER_TEXT.get(lang, LETTER_TEXT["en"])
    sections = parse_response((project / RESPONSE).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    by_id = {r["id"]: r for r in _ledger(project) if isinstance(r.get("id"), str)}

    def where(m) -> str:
        rel, quote = m.group(1).strip().replace("\\", "/"), m.group(2)
        text = (project / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        hit = locate(text, quote)
        name = section_of(text, hit.start() if hit else 0, Path(rel).stem)
        return f"*{t['changed']} {name} ({Path(rel).name}):* “{quote}”"
    out = [f"# {t['title']}", ""]
    for item in data["items"]:
        out += [f"## {item['who']} — {item['id']}", ""]
        out += [f"> {ln}" if ln.strip() else ">" for ln in item["text"].splitlines()] + [""]
        for _, ln in sections.get(item["id"], {"lines": []})["lines"]:
            if ln.lstrip().startswith(">") or reviews.REQUEST.match(ln):
                continue
            out.append(render_results.render_text(CHANGE.sub(where, ln), by_id, lang)[0])
        out.append("")
    return "\n".join(out)


def build(project: Path) -> Path:
    project = Path(project)
    data = load_comments(project)
    why = gate.final_problems(project, "response-letter.docx")
    if why:
        raise Refused(f"the response letter is a final export (G4 open for the {deliverable_of(data)} after a fresh "
                      "audit of the revised drafts, and a clean response.py check): " + "; ".join(why))
    lang = drafts.lang_of(project, deliverable_of(data))
    out = project / LETTER
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_bytes(build_docx.build(letter_markdown(project, data, lang), build_docx.settings_from_project(project),
                                     template=build_docx.template_from_project(project)))
    return out


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["init", "check", "build"])
    ap.add_argument("comments", nargs="?", help="init: the file with the pasted editor and reviewer comments")
    ap.add_argument("--deliverable", choices=PUBLISHING, default="manuscript")
    ap.add_argument("--unapproved", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not project.is_dir():
        print(f"response: project folder {a.project} not found", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "init":
            if not a.comments:
                print("response: init needs the comments file", file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
            data = init(project, a.comments, a.deliverable, a.unapproved)
            print(f"response: {len(data['items'])} comments → {COMMENTS}; {len(data['submitted'])} files in {SUBMITTED}/")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "build":
            print(f"response: wrote {build(project).relative_to(project).as_posix()}")
            return 0
        found = check(project)
    except Refused as e:
        print(f"response: refused — {e}")
        return 1
    except (InputError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f"response: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    for code, lv, loc, msg in found:
        print(f"{code} {lv} {loc}: {msg}")
    blocks = sum(1 for _, lv, _, _ in found if lv == "block")
    print(f"response: {blocks} block, {len(found) - blocks} warn")
    return 1 if blocks else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`gate.py`: docstring — replace the `check prints …` paragraph by "check prints (first token kept): `G3: open (locked, <n> files unchanged) — results locked by <by> on <date>` (G1/G2: `approved by`; a gate without files: `G1: open — approved by <by> on <date>`; open = the author approved; it does NOT mean the files are free to change), `G3: not open — not locked yet`, or `G3: closed` plus the reasons", and add: "FINAL_NAMES maps the final export names to deliverables; out/response-letter.docx belongs to 09_revision/comments.json's deliverable (default manuscript). final_problems(project, name) = export_problems for that deliverable, plus every block of response.py check for the response letter: the one rule for build_docx.py, response.py build and the hook." Code, after `export_problems`:

```python
FINAL_NAMES = {"thesis.docx": "thesis", "manuscript.docx": "manuscript", "case-report.docx": "case-report",
               "review.docx": "review", "response-letter.docx": None}
REVISION = Path("09_revision") / "comments.json"


def revision_deliverable(project: Path) -> str:
    try:
        d = json.loads((Path(project) / REVISION).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")).get("deliverable")
    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        d = None
    return d if d in DELIVERABLES else "manuscript"


def final_deliverable(project: Path, name: str) -> str | None:
    """Deliverable whose G4 a final export `name` needs (None: not a final name)."""
    if name not in FINAL_NAMES:
        return None
    return FINAL_NAMES[name] or revision_deliverable(project)


def final_problems(project: Path, name: str) -> list[str]:
    """Reasons writing out/<name> is refused; [] = allowed or not a final name."""
    project = Path(project)
    d = final_deliverable(project, name)
    if d is None:
        return []
    problems = export_problems(project, d)
    if name == "response-letter.docx":
        import response  # lazy: response imports gate
        try:
            found = response.check(project)
        except response.InputError as e:
            found = [("E-RESP", "block", f"{response.COMMENTS}:1", str(e))]
        for code, level, loc, msg in found:
            w = f"{code} {loc}: {msg}"
            if level == "block" and w not in problems:
                problems.append(w)
    return problems
```

Code:

```python
import tempfile  # with the other imports

OPTS_WITH_VALUE = {"assemble.py": {"--lang"}, "build_docx.py": {"--project", "--template"},
                   "response.py": {"--deliverable"}}
MODULES = {"assemble": "assemble.py", "build_docx": "build_docx.py", "response": "response.py"}
FINAL_NAMES = tuple(gate.FINAL_NAMES)


def map_path(s: str, nt: bool | None = None) -> str:
    """Git Bash paths on Windows: `/c/x` -> `C:/x`, `/tmp/x` -> <temp folder>/x; `~` -> the home folder."""
    if s.startswith("~"):
        s = os.path.expanduser(s)
    win = os.name == "nt" if nt is None else nt
    if win and (s == "/tmp" or s.startswith("/tmp/")):
        return Path(tempfile.gettempdir()).as_posix() + s[4:]
    if win and re.match(r"^/[A-Za-z](/|$)", s):
        s = s[1].upper() + ":" + (s[2:] or "/")
    return s
```

```python
import json

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
from _paths import ROOT
import autorater

FENCE = "`" * 3


def result_line(text, cost=0.5, turns=3):
    return json.dumps({"type": "result", "result": text, "total_cost_usd": cost, "num_turns": turns}) + "\n"


def fake_claude(args, cwd, out):
    cwd = Path(cwd)
    if "--plugin-dir" in args:                       # a side run: old or new plugins
        side = Path(args[args.index("--plugin-dir") + 1]).parent.name
        (cwd / "06_writing").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (cwd / "06_writing" / "x.md").write_text(f"draft by {side}\n", encoding="utf-8")
        tool = {"type": "assistant", "message": {"content": [{"type": "tool_use", "name": "Bash",
                                                              "input": {"command": "python meta.py . run"}}]}}
        Path(out).write_text(json.dumps(tool) + "\n" + result_line(f"answer {side}"), encoding="utf-8")
        return 0
    text = (cwd / "materials.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")       # the judge: prefers the "new" attempt
    winner = "A" if text.index("answer new") < text.index("# Attempt B") else "B"
    answer = f'{FENCE}json\n{{"winner": "{winner}", "reasons": "fixture", "rubric": []}}\n{FENCE}'
    Path(out).write_text(result_line(answer, cost=0.25, turns=1), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0


class TestAutorater(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.saved = (autorater.run_claude, autorater.extract_base, autorater.copy_head, autorater.git)
        autorater.run_claude = fake_claude
        autorater.extract_base = lambda ref, dest: dest
        autorater.copy_head = lambda dest: dest
        autorater.git = lambda *a: b"abc1234def\n" if a[0] == "rev-parse" else b""

    def tearDown(self):
        autorater.run_claude, autorater.extract_base, autorater.copy_head, autorater.git = self.saved
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_verdict_needs_both_orders_to_agree(self):
        v = autorater.verdict
        self.assertEqual(v("B", "A"), "new")
        self.assertEqual(v("A", "B"), "old")
        self.assertEqual(v("tie", "tie"), "tie")
        for w1, w2 in (("A", "A"), ("B", "B"), ("tie", "A"), ("B", None), (None, None)):
            self.assertEqual(v(w1, w2), "inconsistent", (w1, w2))

    def test_parse_judgement(self):
        good = f'Reasoning first.\n{FENCE}json\n{{"winner": "B", "reasons": "r", "rubric": []}}\n{FENCE}\n'
        self.assertEqual(autorater.parse_judgement(good)["winner"], "B")
        self.assertEqual(autorater.parse_judgement('{"winner": "tie", "reasons": ""}')["winner"], "tie")
        self.assertIsNone(autorater.parse_judgement('{"winner": "C"}'))
        self.assertIsNone(autorater.parse_judgement("no json here"))

    def test_starter_cases_are_complete(self):
        self.assertEqual(autorater.list_cases(), ["case-anonymisation", "sr-pooling", "thesis-discussion-overclaim"])
        for name in autorater.list_cases():
            c = autorater.load_case(name)
            self.assertTrue(c["prompt"] and c["rubric"] and c["skills"] and c["allowed_tools"], name)
            self.assertTrue((ROOT / c["fixture"]).is_dir(), name)
        self.assertEqual(autorater.list_cases("thesis-writing"), ["thesis-discussion-overclaim"])
        self.assertEqual(autorater.list_cases("systematic-review"), ["sr-pooling"])
        with self.assertRaises(autorater.Usage):
            autorater.load_case("no-such-case")

    def test_run_case_with_mocked_claude(self):
        res = autorater.run_case("sr-pooling", "main", results=self.t)
        self.assertEqual(res["verdict"], "new")
        self.assertEqual([j["winner"] for j in res["judgements"]], ["B", "A"])
        self.assertEqual(res["runs"]["old"]["changed"], ["06_writing/x.md"])
        self.assertEqual((res["head"], res["dirty"], res["base"]), ("abc1234def", False, "main"))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(res["cost_usd"], 1.5)

        self.assertEqual(saved["verdict"], "new")

    def test_cli(self):

        self.assertEqual(autorater.main(["run", "nope", "--base", "main", "--results", str(self.t)]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(autorater.main(["report", "--results", str(self.t)]), 0)

    def test_results_ignored_and_agents_rule(self):

        agents = (ROOT / "AGENTS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("tools/autorater.py list --skill", agents)
```
