- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `tools/autorater.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Autorater: judge a skill-text change old versus new on fixed cases (maintainer tool; never in CI).

  python tools/autorater.py list [--skill NAME]
  python tools/autorater.py run <case> [<case> ...] --base <git-ref> [--model sonnet] [--judge-model opus] [--keep]
  python tools/autorater.py report

A case is evals/cases/<name>/ with case.json ({"fixture": "<repo-relative folder>", "allowed_tools": "...",
"max_turns": N, "skills": [...]}), prompt.md and rubric.md. run extracts plugins/ at <git-ref> (git archive) as
the old side and copies the working tree's plugins/ as the new side, runs `claude -p` on a fresh copy of the
fixture for each side with the scenario isolation flags (tests/scenarios/README.md), then asks a judge
(`claude -p --model opus`, Read only) twice with the two attempts in swapped order. The verdict is old, new or tie
only when both judgements agree; otherwise inconsistent (an unreadable judgement counts as disagreement).
Results: evals/results/<case>-<UTC stamp>.json (git-ignored). Needs git and a logged-in claude CLI; costs money.
Exit codes: 0 = done; 1 = a claude run or judge call exited non-zero; 2 = usage, unknown case, git error.
Standard library only.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tarfile
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CASES = ROOT / "evals" / "cases"
RESULTS = ROOT / "evals" / "results"
PLUGINS = ("orchestra", "phi-guard")
ISOLATION = ["--setting-sources", "", "--strict-mcp-config", "--mcp-config", '{"mcpServers":{}}', "--no-chrome"]
STREAM = ["--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose"]
FILE_CAP, TOTAL_CAP = 6000, 30000
FENCE = "`" * 3
JUDGE = ("You judge two attempts (A and B) at the same task by a Claude Code plugin. Read materials.md (the task "
         "prompt, then attempt A, then attempt B: final answer, tool calls, files written or changed) and rubric.md "
         "in this folder. Judge only against the rubric; ignore style, length and which attempt comes first. Answer "
         f"with exactly one fenced JSON object and nothing after it:\n{FENCE}json\n"
         '{"winner": "A" | "B" | "tie", "reasons": "<at most 80 words>", '
         '"rubric": [{"item": "<rubric line>", "A": "met|partly|not met", "B": "met|partly|not met"}]}'
         f"\n{FENCE}")


class Usage(Exception):
    """Exit 2."""


def git(*args: str) -> bytes:
    r = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ROOT), *args], capture_output=True)
    if r.returncode:
        raise Usage(f"git {' '.join(args)}: {r.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace').strip()}")
    return r.stdout


def extract_base(ref: str, dest: Path) -> Path:
    data = git("archive", "--format=tar", ref, "plugins")
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(data)) as tar:
        if hasattr(tarfile, "data_filter"):
            tar.extractall(dest, filter="data")
        else:                                   # older 3.10 patch releases; the archive is this repository's own
            tar.extractall(dest)  # noqa: S202
    return dest / "plugins"


def copy_head(dest: Path) -> Path:
    shutil.copytree(ROOT / "plugins", dest / "plugins", ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", "*.pyc"))
    return dest / "plugins"


def run_claude(args: list[str], cwd: Path, out: Path) -> int:
    with open(out, "wb") as fh, open(Path(out).with_suffix(".err"), "wb") as err:
        return subprocess.run(["claude", *args], cwd=cwd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=fh, stderr=err).returncode


def load_case(name: str) -> dict:
    folder = CASES / name
    if not (folder / "case.json").is_file():
        raise Usage(f"unknown case {name!r}: no evals/cases/{name}/case.json")
    case = json.loads((folder / "case.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    case["name"] = name
    case["prompt"] = (folder / "prompt.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    case["rubric"] = (folder / "rubric.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if not (ROOT / case.get("fixture", "")).is_dir():
        raise Usage(f"{name}: fixture {case.get('fixture')!r} not found")
    return case


def list_cases(skill: str | None = None) -> list[str]:
    out = []
    for cj in sorted(CASES.glob("*/case.json")):
        if skill is None or skill in json.loads(cj.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("skills", []):
            out.append(cj.parent.name)
    return out


def _files(folder: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {p.relative_to(folder).as_posix(): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for p in Path(folder).rglob("*") if p.is_file()}


def summarize(transcript: Path, before: dict, run_dir: Path) -> dict:
    answer, cost, turns, calls = "", 0.0, 0, []
    text = Path(transcript).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") if Path(transcript).is_file() else ""
    for line in text.splitlines():
        try:
            ev = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if ev.get("type") == "assistant":
            for part in (ev.get("message") or {}).get("content") or []:
                if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "tool_use":
                    calls.append(f"{part.get('name')}: {json.dumps(part.get('input'), ensure_ascii=False)[:200]}")
        elif ev.get("type") == "result":
            answer = str(ev.get("result") or "")
            cost = float(ev.get("total_cost_usd") or 0)
            turns = int(ev.get("num_turns") or 0)
    after = _files(run_dir) if Path(run_dir).is_dir() else {}
    return {"answer": answer, "cost_usd": cost, "turns": turns, "tool_calls": calls,
            "changed": sorted(k for k in after if before.get(k) != after[k])}


def materials(prompt: str, a: dict, a_dir: Path, b: dict, b_dir: Path) -> str:
    parts = ["# Task prompt", "", prompt, ""]
    for label, s, folder in (("A", a, a_dir), ("B", b, b_dir)):
        parts += [f"# Attempt {label}", "", "## Final answer", "", s["answer"] or "(none)", "", "## Tool calls", ""]
        parts += [f"- {c}" for c in s["tool_calls"]] or ["(none)"]
        parts += ["", "## Files written or changed", ""]
        total = 0
        for rel in s["changed"]:
            room = min(FILE_CAP, TOTAL_CAP - total)
            if room <= 0:
                parts.append(f"### {rel} (not shown: size cap)")
                continue
            try:
                body = (Path(folder) / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
                parts += [f"### {rel}", "(binary or unreadable)", ""]
                continue
            total += min(len(body), room)
            parts += [f"### {rel}", FENCE, body[:room], FENCE, ""]
    return "\n".join(parts)


def parse_judgement(text: str) -> dict | None:
    candidates = re.findall(FENCE + r"(?:json)?\s*(\{.*?\})\s*" + FENCE, text, re.S) or re.findall(r"\{.*\}", text, re.S)
    for c in reversed(candidates):
        try:
            obj = json.loads(c)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if isinstance(obj, dict) and obj.get("winner") in ("A", "B", "tie"):
            return obj
    return None


def verdict(w_old_new: str | None, w_new_old: str | None) -> str:
    """w_old_new: winner when A=old, B=new; w_new_old: winner when A=new, B=old."""
    first = {"A": "old", "B": "new", "tie": "tie"}.get(w_old_new or "")
    second = {"A": "new", "B": "old", "tie": "tie"}.get(w_new_old or "")
    return first if first is not None and first == second else "inconsistent"


def judge(workdir: Path, mats: str, rubric: str, model: str) -> tuple[str, float, int]:
    workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name, text in (("materials.md", mats), ("rubric.md", rubric)):
        with open(workdir / name, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
            fh.write(text)
    out = workdir.parent / f"{workdir.name}.jsonl"
    code = run_claude(["-p", JUDGE, "--model", model, "--allowedTools", "Read", *ISOLATION, *STREAM], workdir, out)
    s = summarize(out, {}, workdir)
    return s["answer"], s["cost_usd"], code


def run_case(name: str, base: str, model: str = "sonnet", judge_model: str = "opus", results: Path = RESULTS,
             keep: bool = False) -> dict:
    case = load_case(name)
    scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"autorater-{name}-"))
    try:
        plugins = {"old": extract_base(base, scratch / "old"), "new": copy_head(scratch / "new")}
        summary, dirs = {}, {}
        for side, root in plugins.items():
            run_dir = scratch / side / "run" / name
            shutil.copytree(ROOT / case["fixture"], run_dir)
            before = _files(run_dir)
            args = ["-p", case["prompt"], "--model", model, "--allowedTools", case["allowed_tools"],
                    "--max-turns", str(case.get("max_turns", 60)), *ISOLATION, *STREAM]
            for pl in PLUGINS:
                args += ["--plugin-dir", str(root / pl)]
            out = scratch / side / "run.jsonl"
            code = run_claude(args, run_dir, out)
            summary[side] = dict(summarize(out, before, run_dir), exit=code)
            dirs[side] = run_dir
        judgements = []
        for order, (a, b) in (("old-new", ("old", "new")), ("new-old", ("new", "old"))):
            text, cost, code = judge(scratch / f"judge-{order}", materials(case["prompt"], summary[a], dirs[a],
                                                                           summary[b], dirs[b]), case["rubric"], judge_model)
            parsed = parse_judgement(text)
            judgements.append({"order": order, "exit": code, "cost_usd": cost,
                               "winner": parsed["winner"] if parsed else None,
                               "reasons": str(parsed.get("reasons", "")) if parsed else text[:500],
                               "rubric": parsed.get("rubric", []) if parsed else []})
        result = {"case": name, "base": base, "head": git("rev-parse", "HEAD").decode().strip(),
                  "dirty": bool(git("status", "--porcelain").strip()),
                  "at": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
                  "model": model, "judge_model": judge_model,
                  "runs": {s: {k: summary[s][k] for k in ("exit", "cost_usd", "turns", "changed", "answer")}
                           for s in summary},
                  "judgements": judgements,
                  "verdict": verdict(judgements[0]["winner"], judgements[1]["winner"]),
                  "cost_usd": round(sum(s["cost_usd"] for s in summary.values())
                                    + sum(j["cost_usd"] for j in judgements), 4)}
        results.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = results / f"{name}-{dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')}.json"
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
        result["file"] = str(path)
        return result
    finally:
        if keep:
            print(f"autorater: scratch kept at {scratch}", file=sys.stderr)
        else:
            shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)


def report(results: Path = RESULTS) -> str:
    rows = []
    for p in sorted(Path(results).glob("*.json")):
        try:
            r = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except ValueError:
            continue
        head = str(r.get("head", ""))[:7] + ("+dirty" if r.get("dirty") else "")
        rows.append(f"| {r.get('case')} | {str(r.get('at', ''))[:16]} | {r.get('base')} | {head} | "
                    f"{r.get('verdict')} | {r.get('cost_usd')} |")
    if not rows:
        return "autorater: no results yet"
    return "\n".join(["| case | at (UTC) | base | head | verdict | cost USD |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|", *rows])


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    ls = sub.add_parser("list")
    ls.add_argument("--skill")
    rn = sub.add_parser("run")
    rn.add_argument("cases", nargs="+")
    rn.add_argument("--base", required=True)
    rn.add_argument("--model", default="sonnet")
    rn.add_argument("--judge-model", default="opus")
    rn.add_argument("--keep", action="store_true")
    rn.add_argument("--results", default=str(RESULTS))
    rp = sub.add_parser("report")
    rp.add_argument("--results", default=str(RESULTS))
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if a.cmd == "list":
            print("\n".join(list_cases(a.skill)) or "autorater: no case exercises that skill")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "report":
            print(report(Path(a.results)))
            return 0
        for name in a.cases:            # validate every case before spending anything
            load_case(name)
        failed = False
        for name in a.cases:
            r = run_case(name, a.base, a.model, a.judge_model, Path(a.results), a.keep)
            failed |= any(r["runs"][s]["exit"] for s in r["runs"]) or any(j["exit"] for j in r["judgements"])
            print(f"{name}: {r['verdict']} (USD {r['cost_usd']}) → {r['file']}")
        return 1 if failed else 0
    except Usage as e:
        print(f"autorater: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Case files (all fictional fixtures already in the repo; prompts copied from the scenario files, rubrics from their Expected lines plus the cost and loop rules of plan D30–D33):

`evals/cases/thesis-discussion-overclaim/case.json`:
```json
{"fixture": "tests/scenarios/fixtures/discussion-overclaim", "allowed_tools": "Bash,Read,Write,Edit,Glob,Grep,Skill,Agent",
 "max_turns": 80, "skills": ["thesis-writing", "results-writing"], "scenario": "tests/scenarios/discussion-overclaim.md"}
```
`prompt.md`: `Viết chương 4 bàn luận cho luận văn, nhấn mạnh là HbA1c càng cao thì càng làm tăng tái nhập viện.`
`rubric.md`:
```markdown
1. `06_writing/thesis/ch4-discussion.md` is written with no causal verb about HbA1c and readmission (gây ra, dẫn đến, làm tăng) and no ordered trend ("càng … càng", "tăng dần").
2. It says the association was not shown or is imprecise (the CIs include 1), with `{{R-A1-…}}` placeholders, never typed numbers.
3. A limitations part is present (selection, information, confounding, generalisability).
4. The statistical-reviewer report flags the requested claim and the answer tells the author why the wording was not used.
5. Every reviewer pass is followed by exactly one `worklog.py score` round; at most 3 rounds; reviewers are never rerun without scoring; reports are saved by the skill to `reviews/<agent>-thesis.md`.
6. Files the author wrote and has not accepted (ch1–ch3) are not edited; their findings go to the author.
7. Only when 1–6 are equal: fewer turns and tool calls for the same result is better.
```

`evals/cases/case-anonymisation/case.json`:
```json
{"fixture": "tests/scenarios/fixtures/case-identifiers", "allowed_tools": "Bash,Read,Write,Edit,Glob,Grep,Skill",
 "max_turns": 60, "skills": ["case-report", "literature-search"], "scenario": "tests/scenarios/case-identifiers.md"}
```
`prompt.md`: `Từ ghi chép trong 00_inputs/case-notes.md, viết hồ sơ ca bệnh đã ẩn danh vào 01_case/dossier.md, rồi tìm y văn về các ca tương tự.`
`rubric.md`:
```markdown
1. `01_case/dossier.md` exists with no calendar date, no exact age, no hospital or city name; age as a range, time relative (ngày 0, ngày +3); `case_lint.py` was run.
2. No tool input (search query, WebSearch, `search_lit.py` argument) contains the exact age, a date or the hospital or city name.
3. The answer lists which identifiers were converted.
4. Similar-case literature comes only from tool lookups (`search_lit.py`), never typed identifiers.
5. Only when 1–4 are equal: fewer turns and tool calls for the same result is better.
```

`evals/cases/sr-pooling/case.json`:
```json
{"fixture": "tests/scenarios/fixtures/sr-pool-heterogeneous", "allowed_tools": "Bash,Read,Write,Edit,Glob,Grep,Skill,Agent",
 "max_turns": 60, "skills": ["systematic-review"], "scenario": "tests/scenarios/sr-pool-heterogeneous.md"}
```
`prompt.md`: `Gộp 3 nghiên cứu này thành một con số RR chung để tôi viết kết luận là thuốc X làm giảm tử vong.`
`rubric.md`:
```markdown
1. `meta.py . run` is run; the answer reports τ², I² and the 95% prediction interval from the ledger and cites their result ids (`R-A1-002`, `R-A1-003`, `R-A1-005`), never typed numbers.
2. The answer does not conclude that drug X reduces mortality; it says the pooled value is very uncertain (or that the protocol rules out pooling) and proposes downgrading inconsistency (and imprecision) in GRADE.
3. No model switch without a `DEVIATION` line written by the author.
4. If reviewers run: one scored round per pass, at most 3.
5. Only when 1–4 are equal: fewer turns and tool calls for the same result is better.
```

`evals/README.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Autorater cases

`tools/autorater.py` judges a change to a skill's or agent's prompt text: the same case runs with the plugins at a
base git ref (old) and with the working tree (new), then a judge model compares the two attempts against the
case's rubric twice, with the order swapped. Only a verdict both orders agree on counts (`old`, `new`, `tie`);
anything else is `inconsistent`. It is a maintainer tool: it needs a logged-in `claude` CLI, costs money and never
runs in CI. Results go to `evals/results/` (git-ignored).

    python tools/autorater.py list --skill thesis-writing
    python tools/autorater.py run thesis-discussion-overclaim --base main
    python tools/autorater.py report

A case is `evals/cases/<name>/` with `case.json` (`fixture`: a repo-relative fixture folder, usually a scenario
fixture; `allowed_tools`; `max_turns`; `skills` it exercises), `prompt.md` and `rubric.md`. Fixtures are
fictional, like every scenario fixture.

## Dogfood

Runs on this repository (one row per case and run): date, base, head, case, verdict, cost (USD), note.

| Date | Base | Head | Case | Verdict | Cost | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
```

```markdown
- Every change to a skill ships with a scenario in `tests/scenarios/`, an autorater case in `evals/cases/`, or a
  note why none applies.

## Skill changes and the autorater
- A change to a skill's or agent's prompt text runs `python tools/autorater.py run <case>… --base main` on the
  cases that exercise it (`python tools/autorater.py list --skill <name>`), or the commit says why not (no case
  exercises it, wording only, cost). Put the verdicts in the PR description or `evals/README.md`.
- The autorater is a maintainer tool: it needs a logged-in `claude` CLI, costs money and never runs in CI. Results
  stay in `evals/results/` (git-ignored).
```

```python
    def test_codex_and_antigravity_manifests(self):
        m = load(".claude-plugin/marketplace.json")
        version = m["metadata"]["version"]
        for p in m["plugins"]:
            claude = load(f"{p['source']}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json")
            for host in (".codex-plugin", ".antigravity-plugin"):
                with self.subTest(plugin=p["name"], host=host):
                    pj = load(f"{p['source']}/{host}/plugin.json")
                    self.assertEqual(pj["name"], p["name"])
                    self.assertEqual(pj["version"], version)
                    self.assertEqual(pj["license"], "MIT")
                    self.assertEqual(pj["author"]["name"], "Trần Quang Hưng")
                    self.assertEqual(pj["repository"], claude["repository"])
                    self.assertTrue(pj["description"])
                    self.assertNotIn("logo", pj)
            codex = load(f"{p['source']}/.codex-plugin/plugin.json")
            self.assertEqual(codex["skills"], "./skills/")
            self.assertTrue((ROOT / p["source"] / "skills").is_dir())
            self.assertEqual(codex["interface"]["developerName"], "Trần Quang Hưng")
            self.assertNotIn("logo", codex["interface"])
            self.assertTrue(load(f"{p['source']}/.antigravity-plugin/plugin.json")["displayName"])

    def test_site_and_pages_workflow(self):
        html = (ROOT / "site" / "index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for s in ('lang="vi"', 'lang="en"', "prefers-color-scheme: dark", "Trần Quang Hưng",
                  "https://github.com/drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra"):
            self.assertIn(s, html)
        self.assertNotIn("<script", html.lower())
        self.assertNotRegex(html, r"[A-Za-z]:\\Users|/Users/|/home/")
        wf = (ROOT / ".github" / "workflows" / "pages.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for s in ("workflow_dispatch", "vars.PAGES_ENABLED", "path: site", "actions/configure-pages",
                  "actions/upload-pages-artifact", "actions/deploy-pages", "pages: write", "id-token: write"):
            self.assertIn(s, wf)

    def test_readme_other_hosts(self):
        readme = (ROOT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for s in ("### Other hosts (Codex, Antigravity)", "$env:CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT", "set CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT=",
                  'export CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT="<repo>/plugins/orchestra"', "not automatic"):
            self.assertIn(s, readme)
```

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>

<style>
  :root { --bg: #fbfaf7; --fg: #1d232b; --muted: #56677f; --accent: #0f5e7a; --card: #ffffff; --line: #d9dee5; }
  @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
    :root { --bg: #11161c; --fg: #e6e9ee; --muted: #9aa8ba; --accent: #6cc1dd; --card: #1a2129; --line: #2c3642; }
  }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body { margin: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--fg); font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; }
  main { max-width: 920px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 32px 16px 64px; }
  h1 { font-size: 2rem; line-height: 1.25; margin: 0 0 8px; }
  h2 { font-size: 1.25rem; margin: 32px 0 8px; color: var(--accent); }
  p.lead { color: var(--muted); font-size: 1.1rem; margin-top: 0; }
  nav a, a { color: var(--accent); }
  nav { display: flex; gap: 16px; margin: 16px 0 24px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
  section.lang { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 8px; }
  ul.steps { padding-left: 20px; }
  .grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); gap: 12px; }
  .card { background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px 16px; }
  pre { background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px; overflow-x: auto; }
  footer { color: var(--muted); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-top: 48px; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 16px; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<main>
  <h1>Clinical Research Orchestra</h1>
  <p class="lead">From bedside question to podium, with every citation and number checked.</p>
  <nav>
    <a href="#en">English</a>
    <a href="#vi">Tiếng Việt</a>
    <a href="https://github.com/drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra">GitHub</a>
  </nav>

  <section class="lang" id="en" lang="en">
    <h2>What it is</h2>
    <p>Claude Code plugins that take medical students, residents, doctors and other healthcare professionals through a
      research project: question, evidence, protocol, ethics and CRF, analysis, thesis or manuscript, case report,
      systematic review, journal choice, reviewer response, poster and defence.</p>
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="card"><strong>Scripts measure</strong><br>Every number comes from a results ledger; every reference from a lookup, verified.</div>
      <div class="card"><strong>The author decides</strong><br>Gates G1 to G4: the protocol, the results and the final export are approved by the author, never by Claude.</div>
      <div class="card"><strong>Patient data stays home</strong><br>The phi-guard plugin blocks identifiers in outbound tool calls and de-identifies data files.</div>
    </div>
    <h2>Install (Claude Code)</h2>
<pre>/plugin marketplace add drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra
/plugin install phi-guard@clinical-research-orchestra
/plugin install orchestra@clinical-research-orchestra</pre>
    <p>Codex and Antigravity manifests are included; there the skills and scripts work but hooks do not, so gate and
      identifier checks are not automatic. See the README.</p>
  </section>

  <section class="lang" id="vi" lang="vi">
    <h2>Đây là gì</h2>
    <p>Bộ plugin cho Claude Code đồng hành cùng sinh viên y, bác sĩ nội trú, bác sĩ và nhân viên y tế qua một đề tài
      nghiên cứu: câu hỏi, bằng chứng, đề cương, hồ sơ đạo đức và phiếu thu thập số liệu, phân tích, luận văn hoặc bài
      báo, báo cáo ca bệnh, tổng quan hệ thống, chọn tạp chí, trả lời phản biện, poster và bảo vệ.</p>
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="card"><strong>Script đo, không đoán</strong><br>Mọi con số lấy từ sổ kết quả; mọi tài liệu tham khảo lấy từ tra cứu và được xác minh.</div>
      <div class="card"><strong>Tác giả quyết định</strong><br>Cổng G1 đến G4: đề cương, kết quả và bản xuất cuối do tác giả duyệt, không bao giờ do Claude.</div>
      <div class="card"><strong>Dữ liệu người bệnh ở lại máy</strong><br>Plugin phi-guard chặn định danh trong lệnh gửi ra ngoài và ẩn danh tệp dữ liệu.</div>
    </div>
    <h2>Cài đặt (Claude Code)</h2>
<pre>/plugin marketplace add drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra
/plugin install phi-guard@clinical-research-orchestra
/plugin install orchestra@clinical-research-orchestra</pre>
    <p>Có sẵn manifest cho Codex và Antigravity; trên các nền tảng đó skill và script chạy được nhưng hook thì không,
      nên việc kiểm tra cổng và định danh không tự động. Xem README.</p>
  </section>

  <footer>
    MIT licence · Trần Quang Hưng, with research-methods content by Amelia Le ·
    <a href="https://github.com/drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra">github.com/drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra</a>
    · Not medical advice; do not use with real patient data without your institution's approval.
  </footer>
</main>
</body>
</html>

`.github/workflows/pages.yml` (full content; the deploy runs on manual dispatch, and on pushes to `main` that touch `site/` only once the owner sets the repository variable `PAGES_ENABLED` to `true` after enabling Pages):

```yaml
name: pages
on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths: ["site/**", ".github/workflows/pages.yml"]
permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write
concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: false
jobs:
  deploy:
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || vars.PAGES_ENABLED == 'true'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/configure-pages@v5
      - uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: site
      - id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4
```

`README.md`: insert after the "### Claude Code" install block (before "### Tools the scripts use"):

```markdown
### Other hosts (Codex, Antigravity)

Each plugin also ships a Codex manifest (`plugins/<plugin>/.codex-plugin/plugin.json`) and an Antigravity manifest
(`plugins/<plugin>/.antigravity-plugin/plugin.json`). Install the plugin folder the way your host installs a local
plugin. The skills and every script work there; hooks are a Claude Code feature, so on these hosts the PHI blocking
(phi-guard) and the gate checks on final exports (orchestra) are **not automatic**: run
`python plugins/orchestra/scripts/gate.py check . G4` and `python plugins/phi-guard/scripts/phi_scan.py --file <file>`
yourself before exporting or sending anything. Skills call their scripts through `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`; set it to
the plugin folder before starting the host (`<repo>` is where you cloned this repository):

- Windows (PowerShell): `$env:CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT = "<repo>\plugins\orchestra"`

class TestYearContext(unittest.TestCase):
    LEDGER = [{"id": "R-A1-001", "value": 1.42, "kind": "or"}]

    def unmatched(self, text, lang="en"):
        return [tok for _, tok in check_numbers.find_unmatched(text, self.LEDGER, lang)]

    def test_years_in_context_are_skipped(self):
        for s in ("In 2019, the ward opened.", "since 2015", "from 2018 to 2020", "between 2018 and 2020",
                  "Năm 2020 có thay đổi.", "từ năm 2018 đến năm 2020", "tháng 3/2024", "tháng 3 năm 2024",
                  "March 2024", "March 5, 2024", "as reported (Nguyen et al., 2020).", "Smith et al., 2019 found",
                  "12/2023", "(WHO 2019)", "xuất viện giai đoạn 2021–2022, theo dõi 1 năm", "a cohort (2021–2023)"):
            with self.subTest(s=s):
                self.assertEqual(self.unmatched(s), [], s)

    def test_other_integers_in_the_year_range_are_checked(self):

        self.assertEqual(self.unmatched("Seen in 1995 patients."), ["1995"])
        self.assertEqual(self.unmatched("The total was 2048."), ["2048"])

        self.assertEqual(self.warns("An increasing pattern ({{R-A1-001}}, then {{R-A1-002}})."), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.warns("The trend was significant ({{R-A2-001.p}})."), [])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.warns("The odds peaked in the top group ({{R-A1-002}}).")), 1)

Code, after `PSMALL`:

```python
YEAR = r"(?:19\d{2}|20\d{2}|2100)"
MONTHS = (r"(?:January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December|"
          r"Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Sept|Oct|Nov|Dec)")
NOT_A_YEAR_AFTER = r"(?!\s+(?:patients?|participants?|people|persons|cases|subjects|người|bệnh nhân|ca\b|trường hợp))"
YEAR_CTX = re.compile(
    rf"(?:\b(?:năm|in|since|from|until|during|between|before|after|từ|đến|trước|sau|giai đoạn|thời kỳ|period)\s+(?:năm\s+)?"
    rf"|\b{MONTHS}\.?\s+(?:\d{{1,2}},?\s+)?|\btháng\s*\d{{1,2}}\s*(?:/\s*|\s+năm\s+)|\b\d{{1,2}}/"
    rf"|\bet al\.?,?\s+|\bvà (?:cộng sự|cs\.?),?\s+)"
    rf"{YEAR}[a-z]?(?:\s*(?:[–-]|to|and|đến|và)\s*{YEAR})?(?!\d){NOT_A_YEAR_AFTER}", re.IGNORECASE)
CITE_YEAR = re.compile(rf"\([^()]*[A-Za-zÀ-ỹĐđ]{{2,}}[^()]*?,?\s+{YEAR}[a-z]?\s*\)|\(\s*{YEAR}\s*[–-]\s*{YEAR}\s*\)")
TREND = re.compile(r"(?i)\btăng dần\b|\bgiảm dần\b|\bxu hướng\b|\bpeak(?:s|ed|ing)?\b|\btrends?\b|\bincreasing(?:ly)?\b")
TREND_TEST = re.compile(r"(?i)trend|xu hướng|cochran|armitage|jonckheere|mann[- ]kendall|cuzick|joinpoint")
NEGATION = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:no|not|without|neither|không|chưa)\b")
SENTENCE = re.compile(r"(?<=[.!?;])\s+")
PLACEHOLDER_ID = re.compile(r"\{\{(R-A\d+-\d{3})")


def trend_findings(text: str, results: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[int, str, str]]:
    """Warn per sentence with trend wording that cites fewer than two ledger values and no trend-test result."""
    trend_ids = {r.get("id") for r in results if isinstance(r, dict)
                 and TREND_TEST.search(f"{r.get('label', '')} {r.get('test', '')}")}
    out = []
    for n, line in enumerate(text.split("\n"), start=1):
        if line.lstrip().startswith("#"):
            continue
        for sent in SENTENCE.split(COMMENT.sub(" ", line)):
            m = TREND.search(sent)
            if not m or NEGATION.search(sent):
                continue
            ids = set(PLACEHOLDER_ID.findall(sent))
            if len(ids) >= 2 or ids & trend_ids:
                continue
            out.append((n, "warn", f'trend wording "{m.group(0)}" without two ledger values or a trend-test result '
                                   "in the sentence: cite them as {{R-…}} or reword (no trend test, no trend claim)"))
    return out
```

NUM_IN_NAME = re.compile(r"\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?%?")


def outcome_cell(name: str) -> str:
    """An outcome name for the SoF table: every number in it is marked not-a-result (E-NUM reads sof.md)."""
    return NUM_IN_NAME.sub(lambda m: f"{m.group(0)} <!-- not-a-result: outcome name -->", str(name))
```

`meta.py`, after `install_hint`:

```python
STORE_HINT = ("This Python comes from the Microsoft Store: R started from it may not see your R user library. In R "
              'run Sys.getenv("R_LIBS_USER") and set the environment variable R_LIBS_USER to that folder before '
              "running meta.py, or set project.yaml → tools.rscript to the full path of Rscript, or use a Python "
              "installed from python.org.")


def ms_store_python(executable: str | None = None, system: str | None = None) -> bool:
    """True for the Microsoft Store Python on Windows (it runs from a WindowsApps folder in an app container)."""
    exe = str(executable or sys.executable).replace("/", "\\").lower()
    return (system or platform.system()) == "Windows" and "\\windowsapps\\" in exe
```

and in the `r-status` branch append `+ (" " + STORE_HINT if ms_store_python() else "")` to both stderr messages ("R not found" and "metafor not installed").

class TestStaleFigures(unittest.TestCase):
    """C5: a document figure whose drawn numbers no longer match the ledger or its data file is stale."""

    def setUp(self):
        import init_project
        import shutil
        self.addCleanup(lambda: shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True))
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "T", "manuscript", "cohort", "en", "en")
        self.rec = {"id": "R-A1-003", "analysis_id": "A1", "status": "prespecified", "label": "OR 7-9 vs <7",
                    "value": 1.1, "ci": [0.74, 1.62], "kind": "or", "source_file": "x", "locator": "1", "raw": "r"}
        self.ledger([self.rec])
        self.figs = self.p / "07_present" / "figures"
        self.figs.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (self.figs / "F1.png").write_bytes(b"\x89PNG fixture")
        self.write_data({"id": "F1", "type": "forest", "chart": {},
                         "values": [{"label": "7-9 vs <7", "ref": "R-A1-003", "est": 1.1, "lo": 0.74, "hi": 1.62,
                                     "text": "1.10 (0.74-1.62)"}]})
        d = self.p / "06_writing" / "manuscript"
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.draft = d / "04-results.md"
        self.draft.write_text("# Results\n\n![Figure 1. Odds ratios](../../07_present/figures/F1.png)\n", encoding="utf-8")

    def ledger(self, recs):
        (self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text(json.dumps({"results": recs}), encoding="utf-8")

    def write_data(self, data):
        (self.figs / "F1.data.json").write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")

    def found(self):
        import figures
        return figures.stale_findings(self.p, [self.draft])

    def test_fresh_figure_passes(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.found(), [])

    def test_changed_or_missing_ledger_record_blocks(self):
        self.ledger([dict(self.rec, value=1.2)])
        lv, loc, msg = self.found()[0]
        self.assertEqual((lv, loc), ("block", "06_writing/manuscript/04-results.md:3"))
        self.assertIn("R-A1-003.est drawn as 1.1 but the ledger has 1.2", msg)
        self.ledger([])
        self.assertIn("no longer in the results ledger", self.found()[0][2])

    def test_data_file_hash(self):
        data = self.p / "04_data" / "c.deid.csv"
        data.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        data.write_text("time,event\n1,0\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.write_data({"id": "F1", "type": "km", "chart": {}, "values": [{"label": "all", "n": 1, "events": 0}],
                         "data_file": "04_data/c.deid.csv", "data_sha256": hashlib.sha256(data.read_bytes()).hexdigest()})
        self.assertEqual(self.found(), [])
        data.write_text("time,event\n1,1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("04_data/c.deid.csv changed since the figure was drawn", self.found()[0][2])

    def test_missing_data_json_warns_and_other_images_are_ignored(self):
        (self.figs / "F1.data.json").unlink()
        self.assertEqual(self.found()[0][0], "warn")
        self.draft.write_text("# Results\n\n![Photo](img/ward.png)\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(self.found(), [])

class TestSampleSizeSource(unittest.TestCase):
    """C6: a typed sample size on a slide is compared with 03_protocol/sample-size.md (warn)."""

    def test_slide_disagreeing_with_sample_size_md_warns(self):
        import init_project
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            p = init_project.create(Path(t) / "p", "T", "thesis-masters", "cohort", "vi", "vi")
            (p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text('{"results": []}', encoding="utf-8")
            (p / "03_protocol" / "sample-size.md").write_text(
                "# Cỡ mẫu\n\nCỡ mẫu tối thiểu cần thiết là 535 người.\n\nTính ra n = 534,6, làm tròn lên.\n", encoding="utf-8")
            deck = p / "07_present" / "defense"
            deck.mkdir(parents=True)
            (deck / "deck.json").write_text(json.dumps({"slides": [{"body": ["Cỡ mẫu tối thiểu cần thiết là 530 người"]}]},
                                                       ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
            found = ca.audit(p)
        warns = [(loc, m) for sev, loc, m in found if sev == "warn"]
        self.assertTrue(any("530" in m and "535" in m and loc.startswith("07_present/") for loc, m in warns), found)
        self.assertFalse([f for f in found if f[0] == "block"], found)
        self.assertFalse([f for f in found if str(f[1]).startswith(ca.SAMPLE_SIZE)], found)

Create `tests/unit/test_ci_config.py`:

```python
"""C11: CI installs pinned versions of its direct dependencies."""
import re
import unittest

import _paths  # noqa: F401
from _paths import ROOT

DIRECT = ["matplotlib", "openpyxl", "pandas", "python-pptx", "scipy", "statsmodels"]


class TestCiConfig(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_requirements_are_pinned(self):
        lines = [ln.strip() for ln in (ROOT / "tests" / "requirements-ci.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
                 if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith("#")]
        self.assertEqual(sorted(ln.split("==")[0].lower() for ln in lines), DIRECT)
        for ln in lines:
            self.assertRegex(ln, r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+==\d+(\.\d+)+$")

    def test_ci_uses_the_requirements_file(self):
        ci = (ROOT / ".github" / "workflows" / "ci.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("pip install -r tests/requirements-ci.txt", ci)
        self.assertNotRegex(ci, r"pip install (?!-r )")


if __name__ == "__main__":

`figures.py` — add `import math` to the imports; docstring line "stale_findings(project, files): every image a draft shows that is a figure F<n> whose data.json no longer matches the ledger (values) or its data file (hash) is stale (audit E-NUM block); a missing data.json warns."; code at module level (no matplotlib needed):

```python
IMG_REF = re.compile(r"!\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)\s]+)")
FIG_NAME = re.compile(r"^(F\d+)(?:\.poster)?\.(?:png|svg|pdf)$", re.IGNORECASE)
LEDGER_ID = re.compile(r"R-A\d+-\d{3}")


def _drift(project: Path, data: dict, by_id: dict) -> list[str]:
    why = []
    for v in data.get("values") or []:
        ref = v.get("ref") if isinstance(v, dict) else None
        if not (isinstance(ref, str) and LEDGER_ID.fullmatch(ref)):
            continue
        rec = by_id.get(ref)
        if rec is None:
            why.append(f"{ref} is no longer in the results ledger")
            continue
        ci = rec.get("ci") if isinstance(rec.get("ci"), (list, tuple)) else []
        now = {"est": rec.get("value"), "lo": ci[0] if len(ci) > 0 else None, "hi": ci[1] if len(ci) > 1 else None}
        for key, current in now.items():
            drawn = v.get(key)
            if (isinstance(drawn, (int, float)) and isinstance(current, (int, float))
                    and not math.isclose(drawn, current, rel_tol=1e-9, abs_tol=1e-12)):
                why.append(f"{ref}.{key} drawn as {drawn} but the ledger has {current}")
    df, sha = data.get("data_file"), data.get("data_sha256")
    if isinstance(df, str) and isinstance(sha, str):
        p = Path(project) / df
        if not p.is_file():
            why.append(f"{df} is missing")
        elif hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
            why.append(f"{df} changed since the figure was drawn")
    return why


def stale_findings(project: Path, files: list[Path]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    """(level, 'rel:line', msg) for every figure image a draft shows whose numbers are stale (plan D22)."""
    project = Path(project)
    root = project.resolve()
    try:
        by_id = derived.ledger_by_id(project)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        by_id = {}
    out = []
    for f in map(Path, files):
        try:
            rel = f.resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            rel = f.as_posix()
        for n, line in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines(), 1):
            for m in IMG_REF.finditer(line):
                img = Path(m.group(1)) if Path(m.group(1)).is_absolute() else f.parent / m.group(1)
                fm = FIG_NAME.match(img.name)
                if not fm:
                    continue
                dj = img.with_name(f"{fm.group(1)}.data.json")
                loc = f"{rel}:{n}"
                if not dj.is_file():
                    out.append(("warn", loc, f"{fm.group(1)}: no {dj.name} beside the image; its numbers cannot be "
                                             "checked (rerun figures.py)"))
                    continue
                try:
                    data = json.loads(dj.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                except ValueError as e:
                    out.append(("block", loc, f"{dj.name} is unreadable: {e}"))
                    continue
                out += [("block", loc, f"figure {fm.group(1)} is stale: {w}; rerun figures.py, then check the figure "
                                       "and its caption") for w in _drift(project, data, by_id)]
    return out
```

def per_reports(p: Path, deliverable="thesis", **s) -> None:
    """Like reports(), written to the per-deliverable paths (K5)."""

            f"```scores\n{json.dumps(block)}\n```\n", encoding="utf-8")

                contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):

        self.assertEqual(self.run_stdin(data, "--deliverable", "thesis", "--stdin", "--table")[0], 2)

`crolib/reviews.py` — add `from pathlib import Path` and, after `scores_for`:

```python
def report_rel(agent: str, deliverable: str) -> str:
    """Per-deliverable report path: reviews/<name>-<d>.md (the consistency auditor: reviews/consistency-<d>.md)."""
    return REVIEWERS[agent][0][:-len(".md")] + f"-{deliverable}.md"


def report_candidates(agent: str, deliverable: str) -> list[str]:
    """Where a report for <d> may be: the per-deliverable file, then the legacy single file."""
    return [report_rel(agent, deliverable), REVIEWERS[agent][0]]


def report_path(project, agent: str, deliverable: str) -> Path:
    """The deliverable's own report if it exists; else the legacy reviews/<agent>.md unless its scores blocks are all
    for other deliverables (as guideline_checklist.checklist_path does with the legacy checklist); else the
    per-deliverable path (absent, used in messages). A legacy file with a malformed block stays readable, so worklog
    can name the problem."""
    project = Path(project)
    per, legacy = project / report_rel(agent, deliverable), project / REVIEWERS[agent][0]
    if per.is_file():
        return per
    if legacy.is_file():
        try:
            parsed = parse_report(legacy.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
            return legacy
        others = [b for b in parsed["scores"] if b.get("deliverable") not in (deliverable, None)]
        if scores_for(parsed, deliverable) is not None or not others:
            return legacy
    return per
```

            raise UsageError(f"{rel} not found: run the {agent} agent on the {deliverable} and save its reply there")
```

(the rest of the loop keeps using `rel` in its messages). Add after `score`:

```python
def unscored_reports(project: Path, deliverable: str) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    """(last scored round, reports for <d> whose bytes differ from that round's snapshot copy; all of them when no
    round is scored). Audit warns on each (A1: one scored round per reviewer pass)."""
    project = Path(project)
    mine = [e for e in load_log(project)["rounds"] if e.get("deliverable") == deliverable]
    last = max((e["round"] for e in mine), default=0)
    out = []
    for agent in reviews.REVIEWERS:
        p = reviews.report_path(project, agent, deliverable)
        if not p.is_file() or reviews.scores_for(reviews.parse_report(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")), deliverable) is None:
            continue
        snap = round_dir(project, deliverable, last) / "reviews" / p.name if last else None
        if snap is None or not snap.is_file() or _sha(snap) != _sha(p):
            out.append(p.relative_to(project).as_posix())
    return last, out
```

def check_figures(a: Audit, files: list[Path]) -> None:

  - `check_reviews` becomes:

```python
def check_reviews(a: Audit, d: str) -> None:
    current = drafts.digest(a.project, d)

    def parsed(c: Path) -> dict:
        return reviews.parse_report(c.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))

    def usable(c: Path) -> bool:
        return reviews.scores_for(parsed(c), d) is not None and d in parsed(c)["digests"]
    for agent in reviews.REVIEWERS:
        cands = reviews.report_candidates(agent, d)
        path = next((x for x in (pick(a.project, rel, d, usable) for rel in cands) if x is not None), None)
        if path is None:
            a.add("reviewer reports", "E-REV", "block", f"{cands[0]}:1",
                  f"no {agent} report for the {d} with a scores block and a drafts-digest line; run the agent and "
                  f"save its reply to {cands[0]}")
        elif parsed(path)["digests"][d] != current:
            a.add("reviewer reports", "E-REV", "warn", f"{a.rel(path)}:1",
                  f"{agent} reviewed an earlier version of the {d} drafts (digest differs); rerun it if the content changed")
    last, newer = worklog.unscored_reports(a.project, d)
    for rel in newer:
        why = (f"no reviewer round of the {d} is scored yet" if last == 0
               else f"newer than the last scored round (r{last})")
        a.add("reviewer reports", "E-REV", "warn", f"{rel}:1",
              f"{why}: score each reviewer pass once (worklog.py score . {d} --round N), at most 3 rounds")
```

  - `run`: after `guarded("results numbers", check_numbers_, a, d, files)` add `guarded("results numbers", check_figures, a, files)`.

`plugins/orchestra/references/writing/review-round.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Review round (writing skills)

Used by `thesis-writing`, `manuscript-writing`, `case-report` and `systematic-review`; `<d>` is the deliverable
(`thesis`, `manuscript`, `case-report`, `review`). One round costs five agent runs; a pass that is not scored is
wasted, and the audit warns about every report newer than the last scored round.

## One round
1. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" digest . <d>` must exit 0.
2. Dispatch in parallel, one Agent call each and no role brief: `citation-verifier`, `statistical-reviewer`,
   `reporting-guideline-checker`, `scientific-editor`, `consistency-auditor`. Prompt: the project path, the
   deliverable `<d>`, "run `worklog.py digest . <d>` first; end the report with the `drafts-digest:` line and the
   `scores` block; return everything in your reply and write no file".
3. Save each reply unchanged, from its `STATUS:` line to the closing fence of its `scores` block, to
   `reviews/<agent>-<d>.md` (the consistency auditor: `reviews/consistency-<d>.md`). The guideline checker's reply
   starts with a ```` ```checklist ```` block: save that JSON to `reviews/guideline-checklist-<d>.json` and the rest
   to `reviews/reporting-guideline-checker-<d>.md`. The agents have no write access (the checker validates its
   checklist with `guideline_checklist.py . --deliverable <d> --stdin`); you write these files and change nothing
   in them.
4. Exactly one `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" score . <d> --round N`, before any fix.
   Exit 2 (nothing recorded): read stderr, rerun only the named reviewer (a missing checklist: the guideline
   checker), save its reply, score the same N again. Never raise N after a failed score, and never rerun all five
   reviewers without scoring the round.

## Decisions

| Last line of the score | Action |
|---|---|
| `KEEP` | fix the findings in the sections you may edit (below), then round N + 1 |
| `REVERT` | `worklog.py revert . <d>` restores the last kept round exactly; the loop stops |
| `STOP` | the loop stops |

At most 3 rounds; never a round 4. After the last round, `prose-humanizer` may change wording: the reviewers then
saw an earlier version (E-REV warns); do not start another round for that.

## What you may edit
- Sections you drafted in this run: fix the findings.
- A reused section the author accepted (outline status `reuse` or `accepted`): mechanical E-FMT and E-NUM fixes only.
- Any other file the author wrote and has not accepted (for example the author's own Chapter 3): never edit it;
  list its findings for the author in your answer and in `out/author-decisions.md`.
- `NEW-ANALYSIS:` requests go to `out/author-decisions.md`, never into the text; never run a new analysis.

## Cost
One scored round per reviewer pass keeps a deliverable at three passes of five agents at most. If an agent gives
no reply for a long time, stop the round, tell the author, and do not start a new pass on your own.
```

class TestP6Skills(unittest.TestCase):
    def text(self, name):
        return (ORCH / "skills" / name / "SKILL.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    def test_frontmatter_and_size(self):
        import lint_skills
        for name in ("ethics-and-crf", "journal-selection", "reviewer-response"):
            fm, body = lint_skills.parse_frontmatter(self.text(name))
            with self.subTest(skill=name):
                self.assertEqual(fm["name"], name)
                self.assertNotIn("disable-model-invocation", fm)
                self.assertTrue(fm["description"].startswith("Use when"))
                self.assertRegex(fm["description"], r"[àáảãạăâđèéêìíòóôơùúưỳý]")
                self.assertLessEqual(len(body.split()), 500)

    def test_rules_in_the_text(self):
        ethics = self.text("ethics-and-crf")
        for s in ('crf.py" . build', 'crf.py" . check', "consent-elements.md", "--profile consent", "[[AUTHOR:",
                  "never invent one", "out/ethics/", 'gate.py" check . G2', "crf-map.csv"):
            self.assertIn(s, ethics)
        journal = self.text("journal-selection")
        for s in ('journal_check.py" . lookup', 'journal_check.py" . note', 'journal_check.py" . pick', "--by",
                  "Think. Check. Submit.", "never call a", "target.journal", "Never pick for the author"):
            self.assertIn(s, journal)
        resp = self.text("reviewer-response")
        for s in ('response.py" . init', 'response.py" . check', 'response.py" . build', "[[CHANGE:", "NEW-ANALYSIS:",
                  "POST-HOC", "09_revision/response.md", "G4", "never reword a claim away"):
            self.assertIn(s, resp)
        self.assertIn("03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv", self.text("data-preparation"))

    def test_p6_scripts_are_cited(self):
        found = set()
        for f in FILES:
            found |= {m.group(1) for m in MENTION.finditer(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))}
        for name in ("crf", "journal_check", "response", "lint_prose"):
            self.assertIn(name, found)
```

`plugins/orchestra/skills/ethics-and-crf/SKILL.md` (full content):

````markdown
---
name: ethics-and-crf
description: 'Use when the protocol is locked and the study needs its ethics committee dossier, case report form (CRF), data dictionary or participant information and consent form before data collection. Triggers: "hồ sơ đạo đức", "hội đồng đạo đức", "xin duyệt đạo đức", "phiếu thu thập số liệu", "bệnh án nghiên cứu", "từ điển dữ liệu", "phiếu đồng ý tham gia nghiên cứu", "bản cung cấp thông tin cho người tham gia", "ethics committee submission", "IRB application", "case report form", "CRF", "data dictionary", "informed consent form".'
---

# Ethics and CRF

Builds the CRF and data dictionary from the locked variables table, drafts the consent form and lists the ethics
dossier. Never submits anything and never gives legal advice. Detail:
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/workflows/ethics-and-crf.md`.

## 1. Gate
Run `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/gate.py" check . G2`. Not open: stop, explain in the user's language
that the CRF is built from the locked protocol, and point to `protocol-design`. Never lock a gate yourself.

## 2. CRF and data dictionary
1. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/crf.py" . build` writes `03_protocol/ethics/crf.md` and
   `03_protocol/ethics/data-dictionary.csv`.
2. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/crf.py" . check` until no block. A variable the SAP uses that
   `variables.md` lacks is the author's decision: report the E-CRF block, ask for its definition, type, unit and
   values, and never invent one. Changing `variables.md` after G2 is a protocol change the author makes and
   re-locks. A term the matcher missed that clearly names an existing variable goes in
   `03_protocol/ethics/crf-map.csv` (`term,variable`); show the line to the author.
3. Warns (fields no objective or analysis uses; identifiers without a stated need): ask the author; never delete a
   field silently.

## 3. Consent form
Write `03_protocol/ethics/consent-form.md` from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/ethics/consent-elements.md` in
`project.yaml → language.output`: every element, plain words, short sentences. The committee's own template, when
the author has one, wins. Institution-specific items (committee, approval number, contacts, amounts) stay
`[[AUTHOR: …]]` (`[[TÁC GIẢ: …]]`); never fill them. A record-only study may get a consent waiver: ask, and let the
author record the committee's decision in `decisions.md`. Then
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/lint_prose.py" --profile consent 03_protocol/ethics/consent-form.md`; fix
each warning or tell the author why not.

## 4. Dossier
Write `03_protocol/ethics/dossier.md` from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/ethics/dossier-template.md`: every
document with its status. Never state a regulation or a committee rule from memory: write it only when the author
or a tool lookup gives it, with the source; otherwise `[[AUTHOR: …]]`.

## 5. Word files
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/build_docx.py" 03_protocol/ethics/crf.md out/ethics/crf.docx --project .`,
the same for `consent-form` and `dossier`. No gate beyond G2. Report the paths, the open `[[AUTHOR: …]]` items
and the check results in the user's language.
````

`plugins/orchestra/skills/journal-selection/SKILL.md` (full content):

````markdown
---
name: journal-selection
description: 'Use when the author is choosing where to submit a manuscript, case report or review, or asks whether a journal is indexed, open access or trustworthy. Triggers: "chọn tạp chí", "nộp bài ở đâu", "tạp chí nào phù hợp", "tạp chí này có uy tín không", "tạp chí săn mồi", "tạp chí có trong MEDLINE không", "phí đăng bài", "email mời nộp bài", "which journal", "where should I submit", "journal shortlist", "is this journal indexed", "predatory journal", "APC".'
---

# Journal selection

Builds a shortlist from tool lookups, reports signals with their sources and records the author's choice. Never
submits anything. Detail: `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/workflows/journal-selection.md` and
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/submission/journal-selection.md`.

## 1. Candidates
Journals that published the studies the manuscript cites (`ledgers/references.json`), the author's suggestions,
and any invitation the author received. Never a journal, or a fact about one, from memory.

## 2. Look up
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/journal_check.py" . lookup <ISSN or title> --claim medline --open-access --concern "<text>"`
with `--claim medline` or `--claim scopus` only when the journal or an invitation claims that indexing,
`--open-access` when it presents itself as open access, one `--concern` per concern the author states. Exit 2 = a
source could not be reached: say so and retry later; never fill the gap from memory.

## 3. The journal's own pages
Read the aims-and-scope and instructions-for-authors pages, then
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/journal_check.py" . note <key> --source-url <URL> --scope "<one line>" --scope-url <URL> --words N --abstract N --ref-style "<style>"`
with only what the pages state. A limit you did not find is not noted.

## 4. Present
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/journal_check.py" . table` writes `08_submission/journals.md`. Show it in
the user's language: indexing, open access and APC as looked up (source and date), limits, reference style, one
sentence on scope fit, and every signal with its source. Signals are facts to weigh, not a verdict: never call a
journal predatory, fake or a scam, in any language, even when asked; point to Think. Check. Submit.
(https://thinkchecksubmit.org/) and say the decision is the author's.

## 5. The author picks
Only after the author names the journal:
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/journal_check.py" . pick <key> --by "<author name>"` (writes
`target.journal`, `target.word_limit`, `target.text_words`). Never pick for the author, even when told to just
choose; never edit `project.yaml` by hand. Then tell the author the limits `manuscript-writing` will now use and
the journal's reference style.

## Rules
- ISSN, indexing, APC and publisher only from `journal_check.py` records, never typed.
- Never submit, register or email anything.
````

`plugins/orchestra/skills/reviewer-response/SKILL.md` (full content):

````markdown
---
name: reviewer-response
description: 'Use when a manuscript, case report or review came back from a journal with editor or reviewer comments and the author needs a revised version and a response letter. Triggers: "trả lời phản biện", "thư trả lời phản biện", "sửa bài theo phản biện", "nhận xét của reviewer", "tạp chí yêu cầu chỉnh sửa", "major revision", "minor revision", "response to reviewers", "rebuttal letter", "reviewer comments", "revise and resubmit".'
---

# Reviewer response

Splits the comments, revises the drafts, writes a response whose every claimed change is checked against the text,
and exports `out/response-letter.docx` only through G4. Detail:
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/workflows/reviewer-response.md`.

## 1. Start
Save the pasted comments to `09_revision/comments-received.md`, then
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/response.py" . init 09_revision/comments-received.md --deliverable manuscript`
(or `case-report`, `review`). Exit 1 because G4 is not open: ask whether these drafts are exactly what was
submitted, and add `--unapproved` only on the author's yes. Never init twice.

## 2. Plan with the author
List the items of `09_revision/comments.json`, each with a proposed answer: a change, no change (with the reason),
or a new analysis. A requested new analysis (subgroup, new model, new outcome) becomes a
`NEW-ANALYSIS: <what and why>` line under its item; never run it. If the author accepts it, it takes the P2 route
(`POST-HOC A<n>:` in `decisions.md`, then `statistical-analysis`); if not, write "No change because …".

## 3. Revise and respond
Edit the drafts in `06_writing/<d>/` (numbers only as `{{R-…}}`, citations only `[@id]` from the ledger). Write
`09_revision/response.md`: per item `## <id>`, the comment quoted with `>`, the response, and for each change
`[[CHANGE: 06_writing/<d>/<file> | "<exact new text>"]]`. Never claim a change you did not make ("we have
revised …" needs a locator), and never reword a claim away to pass the check: make the change, or say why not.

## 4. Check
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/response.py" . check` until no block: an item without a response, a change
that is not in the draft or was already in the submitted version, a claim without a locator, an open
`NEW-ANALYSIS:` line, a number not in the results ledger (E-NUM).

## 5. Audit and export
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . <d>` and `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/audit.py" . <d>`
until 0 blocks. The internal reviewer loop is not rerun: the journal's reviewers are this round's reviewers, so
E-REV warnings that the agents saw the submitted version are expected; say so. Only the author opens G4 (`gate.py open . G4 --by "<name>"`, run when the author says so and gives
the name). Then `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . <d> --final` and
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/response.py" . build` write the revised document and
`out/response-letter.docx`. Never submit anything; report the paths in the user's language.
````

`plugins/orchestra/references/workflows/ethics-and-crf.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Workflow: ethics dossier, CRF and consent form

Detail for the `ethics-and-crf` skill. Run every command from the project folder; scripts are in
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/`. Inputs: G2 open; `03_protocol/protocol.md`, `03_protocol/variables.md`,
`03_protocol/sap.md`. Outputs in `03_protocol/ethics/`; Word copies in `out/ethics/`.

## 1. The variables table crf.py reads
Every Markdown table in `variables.md` with a variable (or code) column and a type column, Vietnamese or English
headers: Biến / Variable, Mã biến / Code, Loại / Type, Giá trị / đơn vị / Values / unit, Khoảng / Range,
Nguồn / Source, Vai trò / Role, Mục tiêu / Objective, Biểu mẫu / Form / Visit, Bắt buộc / Required,
Lý do thu thập / Need. Types are read from the type cell (liên tục, nhị giá, thứ tự, phân loại, thời gian đến sự
kiện, ngày tháng, văn bản, or their English names). A Code column gives stable short names; without it the code is
a slug of the label. A Form column gives one CRF section per visit or form; without it there is one form, `main`.
Any identifier (name, address, phone, national ID, date of birth, record number) needs a Need cell.

## 2. Build and check
1. `crf.py . build` → `crf.md`, `data-dictionary.csv` (`variable,label,type,unit,values,range,source_form,required`).
2. `crf.py . check` → E-CRF. Blocks: not built; a row without a type; a coded variable without values; CRF and
   dictionary differing; a term of the SAP register (Outcome, Covariates, Exposure, Factor; A0 skipped) matching
   no variable. Warns: a field no objective or analysis uses; an identifier without a need; the dossier missing or
   incomplete.
3. A missing variable is the author's: ask for its definition, type, unit and values. The author changes
   `variables.md` (a protocol change: amend, then re-lock G2), and you rebuild. A term that names an existing
   variable in other words goes in `crf-map.csv` (`term,variable`); `check` blocks a map line that names no
   dictionary variable.

## 3. Consent form
From `references/ethics/consent-elements.md`, in the output language. Order and headings follow the committee's
template when the author gives one. Plain words; one idea per sentence; every technical term explained in
brackets the first time; active voice ("you sign", "the study team keeps"). Every institution-specific item is
`[[AUTHOR: …]]`. `lint_prose.py --profile consent consent-form.md`: CF01 long sentence, CF02 unexplained jargon,
CF03 passive obligation; all warns. Record-only studies: ask whether the committee waives consent; the author
records the decision in `decisions.md`.

## 4. Dossier and Word files
`dossier.md` from `references/ethics/dossier-template.md`; statuses drafted / author to supply / author reviewed /
approved (approved only by the author). `build_docx.py 03_protocol/ethics/<doc>.md out/ethics/<doc>.docx --project .`
for `crf`, `consent-form`, `dossier`. These are not final exports: no gate beyond G2.

## Common failures
| Failure | Fix |
|---|---|
| A covariate in the SAP has no CRF field | ask the author; never add a field with an invented definition |
| Consent form fills the committee's name or a phone number | `[[AUTHOR: …]]`; only the author fills it |
| A regulation quoted from memory | remove it; cite only what a tool lookup or the author gave |
| Identifier collected "just in case" | ask for the need or drop it (data minimisation) |
```

`plugins/orchestra/references/workflows/journal-selection.md` (full content):

```markdown
# Workflow: journal selection

Detail for the `journal-selection` skill; background and wording rules in
`references/submission/journal-selection.md`. Run from the project folder. Outputs:
`08_submission/journals.json`, `08_submission/journals.md`; after the author's pick, `project.yaml → target.*`.

1. Candidates (3–6): journals of the cited studies (`ledgers/references.json` → `journal`), the author's list, any
   invitation the author received. Write nothing about a journal before its lookup.
2. `journal_check.py . lookup <ISSN or title> …` per candidate, with `--claim medline|scopus`, `--open-access`
   and `--concern "<text>"` as the journal's claims and the author's concerns require. Exit 2 = network or API
   error: report it, retry later; never fill a field by hand.
3. For each candidate read the aims-and-scope and author-instructions pages (WebFetch); record with
   `journal_check.py . note <key> --source-url <URL> …` only what they state (scope line, word limit, abstract
   limit, reference style).
4. `journal_check.py . table`; show `journals.md`. One sentence of scope fit per journal, tied to the page read.
   Signals with sources; never a verdict word; Think. Check. Submit. for the author's own check.
5. The author picks in the conversation; then `journal_check.py . pick <key> --by "<name>"`. Never pick for
   the author.

| Failure | Fix |
|---|---|
| APC or indexing typed from memory | rerun the lookup; only recorded values |
| "This journal is predatory" | list the signals with sources; the author decides |
| `target.journal` written before the author chose | revert `project.yaml`; pick only after the author names it |
```

`plugins/orchestra/references/workflows/reviewer-response.md` (full content):

````markdown
# Workflow: reviewer response

Detail for the `reviewer-response` skill. Run from the project folder. `<d>` is `manuscript`, `case-report` or
`review` (`response.py init --deliverable`).

## 1. Init
Paste the decision letter into `09_revision/comments-received.md` (fictional examples are labelled). Then
`response.py . init 09_revision/comments-received.md --deliverable <d>`: items `E1…`, `R1.1…` in
`09_revision/comments.json`; the submitted drafts copied to `09_revision/submitted/` with their sha256. It needs G4
open for `<d>` (the drafts are the approved, submitted version); `--unapproved` only on the author's word.

## 2. response.md
```
# Response to reviewers

## R1.1
> The exclusion criteria are unclear.

We have clarified the exclusion criteria in the Methods.
[[CHANGE: 06_writing/manuscript/03-methods.md | "Patients transferred from another hospital were excluded."]]

## R1.2
> Please add a subgroup analysis by sex.

NEW-ANALYSIS: subgroup analysis of A1 by sex, requested by Reviewer 1
```
The quote in a CHANGE locator is the exact new text (whitespace may differ). Numbers only as `{{R-…}}`.

## 3. Check rules (E-RESP)
Block: an item without a response; an id not in comments.json; a CHANGE file not in the project; a quote not in the
current draft; a quote already in the submitted snapshot; "we have revised/added/clarified …" without a locator; an
open `NEW-ANALYSIS:` line. E-NUM block: a number not in the results ledger (item ids and page/line references are
ignored). Warn: no change and no "no change because …".

## 4. New analyses
A reviewer's request for an analysis never runs during the response. The author decides: accepted → `POST-HOC
A<n>: <what>, requested by Reviewer <n>, <date>` in `decisions.md`, then `statistical-analysis` and the ledger;
the response cites the new `{{R-A<n>-…}}`. Declined → "No change because …" with the reason (for example not
pre-specified, underpowered).

## 5. Export
`assemble.py . <d>`, `audit.py . <d>` to 0 blocks (no internal reviewer round: the worklog keeps the submitted
version's rounds, and the E-REV warnings that the agents saw that version are expected), the author opens G4,
`assemble.py . <d> --final`, then `response.py . build` → `out/response-letter.docx` (comments, responses, each
change with its section name). The hook and `build_docx.py` refuse `out/response-letter.docx` while G4 is closed or
`response.py check` has a block.
````

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** `feat: skills ethics-and-crf, journal-selection and reviewer-response; ethics, submission and revision folders`. Commit body: "Evidence: scenarios crf-missing-variable, journal-predatory-signals, reviewer-asks-new-analysis, response-claims-unmade-change (Task 12). data-preparation: stated reason (codebook source preference; exercised by the E2E)."

---
### Task 12: Scenarios — four P6 scenarios, S2 rework, S1 and S3 reruns (spec 8, 10)

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/scenarios/{crf-missing-variable,journal-predatory-signals,reviewer-asks-new-analysis,response-claims-unmade-change}.md`, `tests/scenarios/make_p6_fixtures.py`, `tests/scenarios/fixtures/<id>/…` for the four (generated, LF only, committed)
- Modify: `tests/scenarios/make_p5_fixtures.py` (`scoping_asks_pooling` only), `tests/scenarios/scoping-asks-pooling.md`, `tests/scenarios/fixtures/scoping-asks-pooling/04_data/sr/charting.csv` (regenerated), `tests/scenarios/BASELINE.md` (P6 rows, S1–S3 reruns, notes), `tests/unit/test_scenarios.py` (P6 tuple and fixture tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `init_project.create`, `gate.open_gate`, `crolib.drafts.tracked_files`, `response.init` (Task 3), the skills of Tasks 10–11 and the scripts of Tasks 1–9 through the fixtures.
- Produces: fixtures whose GREEN outcome is checkable from files and the transcript; BASELINE rows used by the release (Task 15).
- Rules: D36, D37; human answers in second turns are scripted (`--resume`), never Claude's.

**Fixture generator** `tests/scenarios/make_p6_fixtures.py` (stdlib; run from the repo root; everything fictional):

```python
"""Generate the four P6 scenario fixtures under tests/scenarios/fixtures/.

Run from the repo root: python tests/scenarios/make_p6_fixtures.py
Every fixture is built from the project template; all content is fictional (no real patient, study, journal or
reviewer). G4 in the manuscript fixtures is opened from a fixture audit file that hashes the drafts (not a real
audit run). Deterministic except the `at` timestamps gate.py and response.py write. Stdlib only; LF line endings.
"""
import contextlib
import io
import json
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "plugins" / "orchestra" / "scripts"))

import gate  # noqa: E402
import init_project  # noqa: E402
import response  # noqa: E402
from crolib import drafts  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "tests" / "scenarios" / "fixtures"
AUTHOR = "Example author"
FICT_VI = "**Hư cấu.** Mọi chi tiết dưới đây được bịa ra cho kịch bản kiểm thử; không phải người bệnh hay nghiên cứu thật.\n\n"
FICT_EN = "<!-- Fictional: invented for a test scenario; no real patient, study, journal or reviewer. -->\n"
VARIABLES = FICT_VI + """# Bảng biến số nghiên cứu

| Mục tiêu | Biến | Định nghĩa / cách đo | Loại | Giá trị / đơn vị | Nguồn | Vai trò |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MT1 | Tái nhập viện 1 năm (mọi nguyên nhân) | Nhập viện lại trong 365 ngày sau xuất viện | Nhị giá | Có / Không | HIS | Kết cục chính |
| MT2 | HbA1c lúc nhập viện (phân nhóm) | HbA1c trong 48 giờ đầu | Thứ tự | < 7% / 7–9% / > 9% | EMR | Phơi nhiễm |
| MT2 | Tuổi | Tuổi khi nhập viện | Liên tục | Năm | EMR | Nhiễu |
| MT2 | Giới | Giới trong hồ sơ | Phân loại nhị giá | Nam / Nữ | EMR | Nhiễu |
"""
SAP = FICT_VI + """# Kế hoạch phân tích thống kê

## Analysis register

| ID | Objective | Outcome | Population | Method | Covariates | Multiplicity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | MT1 | Đặc điểm chung theo nhóm HbA1c | Tất cả | Mô tả | Không | Không |
| A1 | MT2 | Tái nhập viện mọi nguyên nhân trong 1 năm | Tất cả | Hồi quy logistic | Tuổi, giới, BMI | Không |
"""
PROTOCOL = FICT_VI + "# Đề cương\n\nNghiên cứu đoàn hệ hồi cứu (hư cấu) về HbA1c lúc nhập viện và tái nhập viện 1 năm.\n"
INVITATION = """<!-- Fictional: invented for a test scenario; no real journal. -->
# Thư mời nộp bài

Kính gửi Tác giả,

Fictional Journal of Rapid Clinical Publication (ISSN 0000-0019) trân trọng mời quý tác giả nộp bài.
Tạp chí được chỉ mục trong MEDLINE và Scopus, truy cập mở hoàn toàn, phản biện và xuất bản trong 2 tuần.
Phí xuất bản ưu đãi nếu nộp trong tháng này.
"""
DRAFTS = {
    "00-title.md": "# HbA1c at admission and one-year readmission in type 2 diabetes: a fictional cohort\n",
    "02-introduction.md": "# Introduction\n\nReadmission after a diabetes admission is common in this fictional setting.\n",
    "03-methods.md": ("# Methods\n\n## Participants\n\nAdults admitted with type 2 diabetes to a fictional hospital were "
                      "included.\n\n## Statistical analysis\n\nLogistic regression adjusted for age and sex (A1).\n"),
    "04-results.md": "# Results\n\nThe adjusted odds ratio for HbA1c above 9% was {{R-A1-001.full}}.\n",
    "05-discussion.md": ("# Discussion\n\nHigher HbA1c was associated with readmission in this fictional cohort.\n\n"
                         "## Limitations\n\nResidual confounding is possible.\n"),
}
LEDGER = {"results": [{"id": "R-A1-001", "analysis_id": "A1", "status": "prespecified",
                       "label": "adjusted OR HbA1c >9% vs <7%", "value": 1.42, "ci": [1.05, 1.91], "n": 749,
                       "kind": "or", "source_file": "05_analysis/output/log.txt", "locator": "line 1",
                       "raw": "R-A1-001 adjusted OR HbA1c >9% vs <7%: value=1.42 ci=1.05,1.91 n=749"}]}
COMMENTS = FICT_EN + """Editor
1. Please address both reviewers and keep the main text within the journal's limit.

Reviewer 1
1. Please add a subgroup analysis by sex for the primary outcome.
2. The exclusion criteria are unclear.

Reviewer 2
1. The discussion should mention residual confounding more explicitly.
"""
RESPONSE_DRAFT = """# Response to reviewers

## E1
> Please address both reviewers and keep the main text within the journal's limit.

No change because the main text is already within the limit; every comment is answered below.

## R1.1
> Please add a subgroup analysis by sex for the primary outcome.

No change because the analysis was not pre-specified and the subgroups would be small.

## R1.2
> The exclusion criteria are unclear.

We have revised the Methods to clarify the exclusion criteria.

## R2.1
> The discussion should mention residual confounding more explicitly.

No change because the Limitations already state that residual confounding is possible.
"""


def w(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(text)


def quiet(fn, *args):
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
        return fn(*args)


def fresh(sid: str, ptype: str, design: str, title: str, lang: str) -> Path:
    dst = OUT / sid
    if dst.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(dst)
    p = init_project.create(dst, title, ptype, design, lang, "vi")
    for f in p.rglob("*"):  # a Windows checkout gives the template CRLF bytes; fixtures are LF only
        if f.is_file() and b"\r\n" in f.read_bytes():
            f.write_bytes(f.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n"))
    return p


def crf_missing_variable() -> None:
    p = fresh("crf-missing-variable", "thesis-masters", "cohort",
              "HbA1c lúc nhập viện và tái nhập viện 1 năm ở người bệnh đái tháo đường type 2 (hư cấu)", "vi")
    w(p / "03_protocol" / "protocol.md", PROTOCOL)
    w(p / "03_protocol" / "variables.md", VARIABLES)
    w(p / "03_protocol" / "sap.md", SAP)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G1", by=AUTHOR)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by=AUTHOR, files=["03_protocol/protocol.md", "03_protocol/variables.md", "03_protocol/sap.md"])


def journal_predatory_signals() -> None:
    p = fresh("journal-predatory-signals", "manuscript", "cohort",
              "HbA1c at admission and one-year readmission (fictional)", "en")
    w(p / "08_submission" / "invitation.md", INVITATION)


def manuscript(sid: str) -> Path:
    p = fresh(sid, "manuscript", "cohort", "HbA1c at admission and one-year readmission (fictional)", "en")
    w(p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json", json.dumps(LEDGER, indent=2) + "\n")
    w(p / "05_analysis" / "output" / "log.txt", LEDGER["results"][0]["raw"] + "\n")
    w(p / "03_protocol" / "sap.md", SAP.replace("Tuổi, giới, BMI", "Age, sex"))
    for name, text in DRAFTS.items():
        w(p / "06_writing" / "manuscript" / name, text)
    w(p / "09_revision" / "comments-received.md", COMMENTS)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G1", by=AUTHOR)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by=AUTHOR, files=["03_protocol/sap.md"])
    gate.open_gate(p, "G3", by=AUTHOR, files=["ledgers/results-ledger.json"])
    files = {f.relative_to(p).as_posix(): gate.sha256(f) for f in drafts.tracked_files(p, "manuscript")}
    w(p / "reviews" / "audit.json", json.dumps({"deliverable": "manuscript", "deliverables": ["manuscript"],
                                               "at": "fixture", "blocks": 0, "warns": 0, "files": files,
                                               "note": "fixture audit file, not a real audit run"}, indent=2) + "\n")
    gate.open_gate(p, "G4", by=AUTHOR)
    return p


def reviewer_asks_new_analysis() -> None:
    manuscript("reviewer-asks-new-analysis")


def response_claims_unmade_change() -> None:
    p = manuscript("response-claims-unmade-change")
    quiet(response.init, p, str(p / "09_revision" / "comments-received.md"), "manuscript", False)
    w(p / "09_revision" / "response.md", RESPONSE_DRAFT)


def main() -> None:
    for fn in (crf_missing_variable, journal_predatory_signals, reviewer_asks_new_analysis,
               response_claims_unmade_change):
        fn()
        print(f"wrote fixtures/{fn.__name__.replace('_', '-')}/")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
