```python
P6 = ("crf-missing-variable", "journal-predatory-signals", "reviewer-asks-new-analysis", "response-claims-unmade-change")


class P6FixtureTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    def copy(self, sid: str) -> Path:
        dst = self.tmp / sid
        shutil.copytree(FIX / sid, dst)
        return dst

    def test_p6_scenarios_have_fixtures_and_lf_only(self):
        for sid in P6:
            with self.subTest(scenario=sid):
                self.assertTrue((SCEN / f"{sid}.md").is_file())
                for p in (FIX / sid).rglob("*"):
                    if p.is_file():
                        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", p.read_bytes(), str(p))

    def test_crf_missing_variable_blocks_on_bmi_and_keeps_g2(self):
        p = self.copy("crf-missing-variable")
        self.assertEqual(run("crf.py", ".", "build", cwd=p).returncode, 0)
        r = run("crf.py", ".", "check", cwd=p)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1, r.stdout)
        self.assertIn("uses 'BMI', which matches no CRF variable", r.stdout)
        self.assertEqual(run("gate.py", "check", ".", "G2", cwd=p).returncode, 0)

    def test_journal_fixture_has_no_target_journal(self):
        sys.path.insert(0, str(ORCH_SCRIPTS))
        from crolib import projectcfg
        p = FIX / "journal-predatory-signals"
        self.assertIsNone(projectcfg.get(p, "target.journal"))
        text = (p / "08_submission" / "invitation.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for s in ("0000-0019", "MEDLINE", "Fictional"):
            self.assertIn(s, text)

    def test_reviewer_fixture_g4_open_and_init_splits_the_comments(self):
        p = self.copy("reviewer-asks-new-analysis")
        self.assertEqual(run("gate.py", "check", ".", "G4", "--deliverable", "manuscript", cwd=p).returncode, 0)
        self.assertEqual(run("response.py", ".", "init", "09_revision/comments-received.md", cwd=p).returncode, 0)
        items = json.loads((p / "09_revision" / "comments.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["items"]
        self.assertEqual([i["id"] for i in items], ["E1", "R1.1", "R1.2", "R2.1"])
        self.assertIn("subgroup analysis by sex", items[1]["text"])

    def test_response_fixture_blocks_on_the_unmade_change(self):
        p = self.copy("response-claims-unmade-change")
        r = run("response.py", ".", "check", cwd=p)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1, r.stdout)
        self.assertIn("R1.2: the response says the manuscript was changed but gives no", r.stdout)
        self.assertNotIn("R1.1:", " ".join(ln for ln in r.stdout.splitlines() if " block " in ln))
```

```python
class TestFictionalCohortP6(unittest.TestCase):
    """P6: ethics dossier and CRF, journal shortlist from live lookups, one revision round to the response letter."""

    def script(self, name, *args):
        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE.parent / name), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
                              encoding="utf-8")

    def test_crf_and_ethics_documents(self):
        r = self.script("crf.py", str(EXAMPLE), "check")
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        for name in ("crf.md", "data-dictionary.csv", "consent-form.md", "dossier.md"):
            self.assertTrue((EXAMPLE / "03_protocol" / "ethics" / name).is_file(), name)
        for name in ("crf.docx", "consent-form.docx", "dossier.docx"):
            self.assertTrue(zipfile.is_zipfile(EXAMPLE / "out" / "ethics" / name), name)

    def test_journal_shortlist_from_lookups_and_the_authors_pick(self):
        from crolib import projectcfg
        data = json.loads((EXAMPLE / "08_submission" / "journals.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(data["journals"]), 2)
        for rec in data["journals"]:
            self.assertRegex(rec["looked_up"], r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")
            self.assertTrue(rec["title"] and all(t["source"] for t in rec["title"]), rec["key"])
        picked = [r for r in data["journals"] if r.get("picked")]
        self.assertEqual(len(picked), 1)
        self.assertEqual(picked[0]["picked"]["by"], "Example author")
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(EXAMPLE, "target.journal"), picked[0]["title"][0]["value"].replace('"', "'"))
        self.assertNotIn("predatory", (EXAMPLE / "08_submission" / "journals.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower())

    def test_revision_round_and_letter(self):
        r = self.script("response.py", str(EXAMPLE), "check")
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        data = json.loads((EXAMPLE / "09_revision" / "comments.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertTrue(data["approved"])
        for rel, sha in data["submitted"].items():
            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256((EXAMPLE / "09_revision" / "submitted" / rel).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), sha)
        text = (EXAMPLE / "09_revision" / "response.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for item in data["items"]:
            self.assertIn(f"## {item['id']}", text)
        self.assertIn("[[CHANGE:", text)
        self.assertIn("fictional", (EXAMPLE / "09_revision" / "comments-received.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower())
        with zipfile.ZipFile(EXAMPLE / "out" / "response-letter.docx") as z:
            self.assertIn("word/document.xml", z.namelist())
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE), "check", str(EXAMPLE), "G4", "--deliverable", "manuscript"],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout)
        self.assertFalse((EXAMPLE / "out" / "ch3-results.docx").exists())
```

### Spec contradictions found (resolved as noted)

- **X1** §4 "writes `target.journal` and the limits into `project.yaml`" vs §9 "New YAML keys: none beyond existing `target.*`": which keys hold the limits is not said → the existing `target.word_limit` (abstract) and `target.text_words` (main text), already read by `case_lint.py` and `manuscript-writing` (D7).
- **X2** §5 lists four `E-RESP` rules, none of which blocks "we have revised the Methods" with no locator, yet scenario 4's GREEN "reports the block" → a change claim without a locator blocks (D12).
- **X3** §5 "a `NEW-ANALYSIS:` line for the author" vs a letter built for the journal → an open `NEW-ANALYSIS:` line blocks `check` and therefore `build`; it is never sent (D12).
- **X4** §5 "the manuscript's G4" vs case reports and reviews, which are also submitted and revised → `--deliverable` at `init`, recorded in `comments.json`, used by `build`, `build_docx.py` and the hook (D10, D13).
- **X5** §3 "a variable used in the SAP register": the register is free text with no variable codes → fuzzy term matching with a visible, checked `crf-map.csv` (D2).
- **X6** §8 A2 and §12.9 ("reviewer agents keep no write access") vs the reporting-guideline-checker's duty to write and validate `reviews/guideline-checklist-<d>.json` → validation through `--stdin`, file saved by the skill (D31).
- **X7** §8 A3 "never edit a chapter the author has not accepted", read literally, forbids editing Claude's own drafts (never "accepted" until the end) → read as "never edit an author's file that you did not draft and the author has not accepted" (D33).
- **X8** §8 C5 "as posters do": posters compare a render digest; document figures record none → compare the drawn values with the ledger and the data-file hash (D22).
- **X9** §8 C9 names only G3, but G2 locks files and was misread the same way → G1–G3 wording, G4 unchanged (D14).
- **X10** §6 "answer in a fixed JSON shape" without the shape → D16.
- **X11** §7 "a Pages workflow deploying `site/`" vs §11 "enabling Pages waits for the owner": a deploying workflow on a repository without Pages fails on every push → manual dispatch until `PAGES_ENABLED` is set at sign-off (D19).
- **X12** §1 "the reworked P5 scenarios are GREEN" vs §8 S2 "reworded so RED fails": if the unaided model still refuses, the scenario cannot discriminate however GREEN behaves → record it and keep the unit test as the guard (D37).
- **X13** §1 "one revision round … → audit → G4" with the writing skills' reviewer loop: `worklog.py` refuses a stopped loop or a fourth round → no internal reviewer round in a journal revision (D42).
- **X14** §8 D2 "remove stale `out/ch3-results.docx`" while the example README describes it → README rows updated in the same task (Task 14).

### Gaps left open (stated, not silently filled)

- The NLM Catalog XML element names (and DOAJ/Crossref keys) are grounded by one live capture during Task 2; a later format change makes `journal_check.py` exit 2 (no wrong data), not silently empty.
- Scopus indexing cannot be confirmed with open sources; it is always a signal, never a fact.
- `crf.py`'s term matching is a heuristic; real projects may need `crf-map.csv` lines, which the author sees and the check verifies.
- The autorater's judge is checked only for self-agreement across the two orders; there is no human calibration, and the USD 20 budget may leave the thesis case unrun.
- Codex and Antigravity installation steps are not documented beyond "install the plugin folder as your host does"; the host-specific commands were not verified for this plan.
- The landing page URL is unknown until the owner enables Pages (appendix).
- C8, C12, D1, D3, K1, K2, K4, S4, S5, S6, S8 stay open by spec decision.

## Appendix: Owner sign-off checklist (1.0.0)

Nothing below runs before 0.7.0 is on `main` and the owner, Trần Quang Hưng, writes "duyệt 1.0" in the conversation. Each item is one action, done in this order:

1. Branch `release-1.0` from `main`.
2. Bump `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json → metadata.version` and all six plugin manifests (`.claude-plugin`, `.codex-plugin`, `.antigravity-plugin` for `orchestra` and `phi-guard`) to `1.0.0`; README status badge and status paragraph to 1.0.0.
3. `CHANGELOG.md`: `## 1.0.0 (<date>)` — "Owner sign-off of 0.7.0; no functional change" plus any fixes merged after 0.7.0.
4. Full suite and lint → PASS; push the branch; CI green on ubuntu and windows × Python 3.10 and 3.12.
5. Merge `release-1.0` into `main` (merge commit) and push `main`.
6. Tag the merge commit: `git tag -a v1.0.0 -m "clinical-research-orchestra 1.0.0"`; `git push origin v1.0.0`.
7. GitHub release: `gh release create v1.0.0 --title "1.0.0" --notes-file <file holding the CHANGELOG 1.0.0 section>`.
8. Only with the owner's explicit yes to this step: enable GitHub Pages with source "GitHub Actions" (repository Settings → Pages), set the repository variable `PAGES_ENABLED` to `true` (`gh variable set PAGES_ENABLED --body true`), run `gh workflow run pages.yml`, open the published URL once, then add the URL to the README and to the manifests' homepage fields in a follow-up commit on `main`.
9. Nothing is announced or posted outside the repository without the owner.
